TL;DR: 提出统一具身智能体运行时HROS及其住宅巡检实现Argos，整合异构感知、可复用自治技能与记忆增强认知，实现可追溯巡检闭环，实验验证导航与巡检性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足巡检系统多通过任务专用接口集成功能，导致上下文协调、知识复用与受控适配困难；可部署系统需在同一可追溯运行闭环内连接异构感知、可复用自治能力、多模态场景理解、人机交互与企业侧响应。

Method: HROS划分为机器人运行时、具身自治技能、认知智能体运行时、交互与运维四个平面；以共享上下文连接物理状态与智能体推理，流式ASR/TTS支持语音任务交互，分层工作/情节/语义记忆保存操作知识，安全门控自演化环将执行轨迹转化为版本化候选更新；Argos原型集成Vbot四足平台、Fast-LIO2、Hobot-Stereo、PCT-Planner、EGO-Planner及OpenClaw编排的Qwen3-VL分析。

Result: 住宅物业环境中航点可达率100%，室外定位误差低于10 cm，局部避障响应延迟低于200 ms，典型隐患检测率85–95%，告警投递与结构化报告生成成功率99%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了所部署的导航与巡检闭环；HROS为记忆增强、语音感知且可持续改进的具身巡检智能体提供可扩展软件基础。

Abstract: Autonomous property inspection requires more than robust robot navigation: a deployable system must connect heterogeneous sensing, reusable autonomy capabilities, multimodal scene understanding, human interaction, and enterprise response within a traceable operational loop. Existing quadruped inspection systems commonly integrate these functions through task-specific interfaces, making contextual coordination, knowledge reuse, and controlled adaptation difficult. This paper presents \textit{Harness Robotic OS} (HROS), a unified embodied-agent runtime, and Argos, its realization for residential-community inspection. HROS organizes the system into robot runtime, embodied autonomy skills, cognitive agent runtime, and interaction and operations planes. A shared context connects physical state with agent reasoning; streaming ASR/TTS supports voice-based mission interaction; hierarchical working, episodic, and semantic memory preserves operational knowledge; and a safety-gated self-evolution loop converts execution traces into versioned candidate updates without permitting unconstrained online modification. The Argos prototype integrates a Vbot quadruped, Fast-LIO2 localization and mapping, Hobot-Stereo depth perception, PCT-Planner global planning, EGO-Planner local motion generation, and OpenClaw-orchestrated Qwen3-VL inspection analysis. Experiments in a residential property environment achieved 100\% waypoint reachability, outdoor localization error below 10~cm, local obstacle-response latency below 200~ms, representative hazard-detection rates of 85--95\%, and 99\% success in alarm delivery and structured-report generation. These results validate the deployed navigation and inspection closed loop, while HROS provides an extensible software foundation for memory-augmented, voice-aware, and continuously improvable embodied inspection agents.

</details>


### [74] [Beyond Noise Steering: Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning for Generative Robot Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11270)
*Pengfei Zhang,Teng Sun,Xianchao Xiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DLSRL双潜空间强化学习框架，在冻结生成器中同时进行初始噪声引导与表示级控制，加速在线机器人策略自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法仅调节生成过程的噪声空间，无法调控生成器中间的动作表示，导致性能下降与效率低下。

Method: Actor网络预测两个潜变量：初始噪声潜变量引导行为生成，动作表示潜变量用于中间特征调制；表示潜变量映射为适配器特征，并通过残差连接注入中间动作token的隐藏状态，实现不更新基础策略的双重控制。

Result: 在多种生成式策略架构与机器人操作任务上的实验表明，DLSRL有效加速在线策略自适应并取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DLSRL通过表示级内部控制补充噪声引导，为预训练生成式机器人策略的高效在线适配提供了新思路。

Abstract: Pretrained generative robot policies learn expressive action priors from demonstrations. However, existing reinforcement learning methods only steer the noisy space but fail to modulate intermediate action representations during the generation process, resulting in performance degradation and inefficiency. To address this limitation, we propose a novel Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning (DLSRL) framework, which complements initial-noise steering with representation-level control inside the frozen generator. Specifically, our actor network predicts two distinct latent variables: an initial-noise latent variable that steers behavior generation, and an action-representation latent variable for intermediate feature modulation. Moreover, this representation latent variable is mapped to adapter features and ingeniously injected into the hidden states of intermediate action tokens via residual connections. Our dual-control design enables direct adjustment of action representations without updating the base policy. Experiments across generative policy architectures and robotic manipulation tasks show that DLSRL effectively accelerates online robot policy adaptation and achieves competitive performance. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}.

</details>


### [75] [2AM: Grounding Agent-Side Memory as Guidance for Steerable Action Models in Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11308)
*Yutong Hu,Fengjiao Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Renaud Detry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出2AM框架：由多模态Agent独占任务记忆并生成子任务语言与2D操作提示，单一RGB、回合内无状态的Action Model负责执行，在LIBERO-Mem上平均完成率76.3%，较最强基线14.8%提升61.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要记忆能力，但现有agentic系统（VLA+规划器+几何工具，甚至额外深度或标定几何）存在归因混淆问题：性能提升可能来自更丰富的观测或替代运动工具，失败也可能源于策略本身或语言接口描述不足。作者希望隔离并厘清"记忆应放在哪里"以及"策略能力受何种因素制约"这一问题。

Method: 采用刻意受限的设计：减少工具广度，但提高接口带宽。2AM让多模态Agent成为任务记忆的唯一持有者，仅由单一基于RGB、回合内无状态的Action Model执行全部任务相关运动。Agent将交互历史编译为子任务语言，以及可选的2D抓取、放置和移动提示，在不同时间尺度上绑定其物理意图。为让VLA学会这种可引导性，在示范中增强结构化提示标签，并在条件dropout、空间噪声和时间抖动下训练，以容忍Agent输出的不完美。

Result: 在LIBERO-Mem上，不使用深度、在线几何或基于规划器的物体运动，2AM达到76.3%的平均完成率，较报告的最强基线14.8%提升61.5个百分点；同时取得63.0%的宽松成功率和11.8%的严格成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明任务记忆可以保留在Agent侧，无需内嵌于动作策略中；同时说明Action Model的能力不仅取决于策略学到了什么，还取决于Agent能以多高的精度对其进行引导。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires memory, but not necessarily inside the action policy. To address such tasks, current agentic systems often combine VLAs with planners and geometric tools, sometimes using additional depth or calibrated geometry. These systems confound attribution: gains may come from richer observations or alternative motor tools, while failures may stem from either the policy or an under-specified language interface. We isolate this question through a deliberately constrained design: less tool breadth, but greater interface bandwidth. 2AM makes a multimodal Agent the sole holder of task memory and a single RGB-based, episodically stateless Action Model the sole executor of task-relevant motion. The Agent compiles interaction history into subtask language and optional 2D grasp, place, and move hints that bind its physical intention at different time scales. To teach this steerability to the VLA, we augment demonstrations with structured hint labels and train under condition dropout, spatial noise, and temporal jitter to tolerate imperfect Agent outputs. On LIBERO-Mem, without depth, online geometry, or planner-based object motion, 2AM reaches 76.3% average completion, a 61.5-point improvement over the strongest reported baseline of 14.8%, together with 63.0% relaxed and 11.8% strict success. These results show that task memory can remain Agent-side. They further show that Action Model capability depends not only on what the policy has learned, but on how precisely the Agent can steer it.

</details>


### [76] [Modular Kinematic Reduction of Closed-Chain Mechanisms Using Path Assembly and Defect Homotopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11338)
*Mohammad Dastranj,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PACDM框架，实现闭链机构的模块化闭合求解与运动学降维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭链机构的主动与被动坐标通过非线性闭合约束耦合，给模块化建模带来困难。

Method: 以有序变换路径对比构造闭合单元，用SE(3)对数表达失配并组装雅可比；由最小成对闭合单元构建多路径模块，秩揭示分析选取局部独立标量约束；用缺陷同伦沿可行正则连续路径恢复闭合一致的被动坐标；在正则位形下隐式微分得到主动到被动的局部微分映射，并用于预测-校正连续过程。

Result: 在含双路径与三路径闭链模块的七自由度重载机械臂上验证，与Simscape Multibody相比轨迹均方根误差低于8.5×10^-10 rad，预测-校正连续过程比逐样本缺陷同伦约快45.8倍。

Conclusion: PACDM为模块化闭链求解与运动学降维提供了有效方案，兼具高精度与高计算效率。

Abstract: Closed kinematic chains complicate modular modeling by coupling active and passive coordinates through nonlinear closure constraints. This paper presents a Path-Assembled Closure Differential Mapping (PACDM) framework for modular closure resolution and kinematic reduction. Each closure element compares two ordered transformation paths with common endpoints, with their mismatch expressed through the logarithm on SE(3) and the corresponding Jacobian assembled from local transformation derivatives. Multi-path modules are constructed from a minimal set of pairwise closure elements, while rank-revealing analysis selects locally independent scalar constraints. A defect homotopy recovers closure-consistent passive coordinates from approximate estimates along a feasible and regular continuation path. At regular configurations, implicit differentiation yields the local active-to-passive differential mapping, which is subsequently used in a predictor-corrector continuation procedure for prescribed motion. The framework is evaluated on a seven-degree-of-freedom heavy-duty manipulator containing two-path and three-path closed-chain modules. Comparison with Simscape Multibody yields trajectory root-mean-square errors below 8.5 x 10^-10 rad, while predictor-corrector continuation is approximately 45.8 times faster than applying defect homotopy at every trajectory sample.

</details>


### [77] [Morphology-Aware Human Motion Retargeting for Wheeled-Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11357)
*Chenbo Xia,Chao Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将多数据集SMPLX人体动作转换为Galaxea R1 Pro轮式人形机器人可执行的移动-操作行为，构建了从人体动作到物理可跟踪行为的完整重定向与学习管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-人形重定向多集中于足式平台，轮式人形系统支持由通用人体动作驱动的移动与操作耦合的研究较少。R1 Pro 无腿关节，需将人体下肢运动重新分配至底盘运动与躯干姿态，同时保持操作相关的臂部几何。

Method: 基于GMR可配置通用运动重定向与BeyondMimic的R1 Pro物理仿真学习框架，管线包括：规范体型预处理、平面底盘归一化、形态感知微分逆运动学、肩部为根的分层臂部重定向、用于弯腰与下蹲的连续躯干替代；再由参考扭转驱动的规划层将平面底盘运动解码为满足滞回、运动学连续性、加速度及执行器速率限制的连续三轮转向与滚动指令；最后在Isaac Lab训练21维BaseDecode策略，采用方向性关节限位缩放、聚焦上身跟踪与分阶段轮-地接触奖励。

Result: 构建了从人体动作数据到物理可跟踪的轮式人形移动-操作行为的完整桥接系统，而非仅可视化的重定向器；定量策略对比留待后续修订。

Conclusion: 该工作为轮式人形提供可复现的通用运动重定向与仿真学习管线，实现移动-操作耦合；定量评估尚待补充。

Abstract: Human-to-humanoid retargeting has largely been studied on legged platforms, while comparatively few wheeled-humanoid systems support coupled locomotion and manipulation from general human motion. Building on GMR's configurable general-motion retargeting and BeyondMimic's physically simulated R1 Pro learning framework, we present a reproducible pipeline that converts multi-dataset SMPLX motion into executable loco-manipulation behavior for the Galaxea R1 Pro wheeled humanoid. The robot has a planar three-wheel base, a serial torso, and two arms but no leg joints, so human lower-body motion must be redistributed across base motion and torso posture without sacrificing manipulation-relevant arm geometry. Our pipeline combines canonical body-shape preprocessing, planar-base normalization, morphology-aware differential inverse kinematics, shoulder-rooted hierarchical arm retargeting, and continuous torso substitution for bending and squatting. A reference-twist-driven planning layer then decodes planar base motion into continuous three-wheel steering and rolling commands subject to hysteresis, kinematic continuity, acceleration, and actuator-rate limits. Finally, a 21-dimensional BaseDecode policy is trained in Isaac Lab with directional joint-limit scaling, focused upper-body tracking, and a staged wheel-contact reward. The resulting system provides a complete bridge from human motion data to physically trackable wheeled-humanoid loco-manipulation rather than a visualization-only retargeter; quantitative policy comparisons remain scheduled for a later revision.

</details>


### [78] [GeoTrussRover: Morphological Computation with Contact-Semantic Control Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11361)
*Muyuan Ma,Yi Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuanyan Zheng,Ruiqi Hu,Boxuan Ke,Zhenyu Chen,Yicong Lin,Xin Hao Yang,Daliang Xiao,Zhinan Hou,Wanhao Niu,Yuan Sun,Yan Yang,Yue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GeoTrussRover，将电驱动可变几何桁架、轮式底盘与接触语义形态规划控制相结合，用四个接触语义基元低维表示21杆协调，实现跨台阶高度的快速运动迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构机器人可在固定机体无法越过障碍时改变接触几何。可变几何桁架(VGT)能把形状变化分布到承载结构中，但与移动底盘耦合后会产生高维协调问题，因此需要一个低维、可复用的表示与规划控制框架。

Method: GeoTrussRover集成电驱动VGT、轮式底盘与接触语义形态规划控制。先求解一次源穿越，提取描述21个构件协调的四个接触语义基元；用物理约束投影将其适配到具有相同接触拓扑的未见过台阶高度；仅当某相位违反新物理约束时重算该相位；再用全空间QP跟踪适配运动并修正构件与车轮误差。

Result: 从0.10m迁移到0.075m时，相比完全重算，目标函数评估减少63.7%。接触相位可行性分析覆盖0.10–0.46m台阶高度（1.08–4.97轮半径），上限接近理论可行边界。电驱动原型可穿越2.11轮半径。

Conclusion: 所得低维表示能在超冗余、承载形态中存储任务协调，并在运动过程中复用，从而避免每次完全重算运动，提升对未见过台阶高度的适应效率。

Abstract: Reconfigurable robots can change their contact geometry when a fixed body cannot negotiate an obstacle. A variable-geometry truss (VGT) distributes this shape change through a load-bearing structure, but coupling it to a mobile base creates a high-dimensional coordination problem. GeoTrussRover combines an electrically actuated VGT, a wheeled base, and contact-semantic morphology planning and control. We solve one source traversal and extract four contact-semantic primitives that describe coordination among 21 members. Physics-constrained projection adapts them to unseen step heights with the same contact topology. When every phase remains feasible, adaptation does not recompute the complete motion. If one phase violates the new physical constraints, only that phase is recomputed. A full-space QP then tracks the adapted motion and corrects member and wheel errors. For transfer from 0.10m to 0.075m, the method reduces objective-function evaluations by 63.7% relative to full recomputation. Contact-phase feasibility analysis covers step heights from 0.10 to 0.46m, or 1.08 to 4.97 wheel radii, with the upper value near the theoretical feasible boundary. The electric prototype traverses 2.11 wheel radii. The resulting low-dimensional representation stores task coordination in a hyper-redundant, load-bearing morphology and reuses it during locomotion.

</details>


### [79] [SwarmNxt: Open-source Software-Hardware Platform for Fast and Agile Aerial Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11382)
*Charbel Toumieh,Niel Mistry,Benjamin Jarvis,Simon Jeger,Peize Liu,Shaojie Shen,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwarmNxt：基于开源OmniNxt硬件的开源集群导航软件平台，提供端到端工具链并完成六机与四机真实室内飞行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人集群可用于安防、安防与搜救等时间敏感任务，可快速测绘灾区、在无GPS环境下建图并搜索复杂区域，缩短响应时间、降低救援人员风险。但实现该潜力需鲁棒的自主集群导航，仍是开放难题。现有商用无人机多为闭源或缺乏敏捷视觉集群飞行所需的机载算力；同时多机软件部署与维护工程量大，制约研究进展。

Method: 提出SwarmNxt——建立在开源OmniNxt无人机硬件之上的开源软件平台。提供端到端工具包：含详细硬件装配说明与视频教程、支持并行软件部署与全集群更新的自动化工具，以及基于ROS 2的自主导航框架。平台将先进的控制、规划与深度估计集成到单一ROS 2多智能体系统中，构成面向实体集群实验的开放研究基础设施。

Result: 通过两个真实实验验证：1）六机集群执行去中心化规划并实现高速机间避碰；2）四机集群在布满障碍的环境中完成带机载深度估计的集群飞行。两项实验均在室内进行，全局位置来自外部动捕系统，感知、规划与控制均机载运行。

Conclusion: SwarmNxt通过开源软硬件一体化方案降低了多机自主集群的研究门槛，验证了去中心化规划、机间避碰与机载深度估计在真实集群飞行中的可行性，为物理集群实验提供开放基础设施。

Abstract: Aerial robot swarms have the potential to transform time-critical safety, security, and search-and-rescue operations. By coordinating multiple robots, they can rapidly survey disaster sites, map collapsed or GPS-denied environments, and search cluttered areas faster than a single robot, reducing response times and minimizing risks to first responders. Realizing this potential, however, requires robust autonomous swarm navigation, which remains an active research challenge. Progress is further constrained by existing platforms, as commercial drones are often closed-source or lack the onboard computational resources needed for agile, vision-based collective flight. Moreover, developing, deploying, and maintaining software across multiple aerial robots requires significant engineering effort. To address these challenges, we present SwarmNxt, an open-source software platform built on the open-source OmniNxt drone hardware. SwarmNxt provides an end-to-end toolkit, including detailed hardware assembly instructions with a video tutorial, automation tools for parallel software deployment and swarm-wide updates, and a ROS 2-based framework for autonomous navigation. The platform integrates state-of-the-art control, planning, and depth estimation into a single ROS 2 multi-agent system, providing an open research infrastructure for physical swarm experimentation. We validate SwarmNxt through two real-world experiments: a six-drone swarm performing decentralized planning with high-speed inter-drone collision avoidance, and a four-drone swarm executing collective flight with onboard depth estimation in an obstacle-filled environment. Both experiments were run indoors with global position from external motion capture; perception, planning, and control run onboard.

</details>


### [80] [Safety-aware Skill Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11433)
*A K M Nadimul Haque,Sheila Sutjipto,Marc G. Carmichael,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dist-GPRL，一种距离感知与安全引导的强化学习框架，用于结构化机器人技能自适应，通过 GP 参数化与局部窗口轨迹点序列更新实现安全、稳定的技能适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习的技能自适应框架通常依赖固定观测或严格控制的探索计划等限制性假设以保持稳定性，但在杂乱动态环境中，无约束探索易导致不安全行为与学习不稳定，尤其当任务相关观测靠近障碍物或涉及移动物体时。

Method: 在基于高斯过程（GP）的技能参数化基础上，Dist-GPRL 顺序自适应稀疏轨迹途经点的重叠局部窗口，而非在每个策略步修改完整技能；原始策略输出通过 GP 协方差结构关联，产生时间一致的轨迹更新，并降低全局轨迹自适应带来的动作空间与信用分配难度。安全引导包含两部分：由 Hausdorff Approximation Planner（HAP）导出的安全子空间先验，将策略探索偏向可行区域；动态更新的距离场间隙与梯度奖励提供局部障碍物感知。此外引入轨迹运动学相似性正则项，以在自适应过程中保留示教的速度与加速度特性。

Result: 在仿真中的两个动态物体操作任务上评估，并将学习到的策略迁移至真实机器人执行。实验结果表明，相比基线方法，该框架具有更高的任务成功率、更低的碰撞频率和更稳定的学习过程，同时保留示教技能的运动学特性。

Conclusion: Dist-GPRL 通过 GP 协方差关联的局部窗口更新、HAP 安全子空间先验、距离场奖励与运动学正则化，实现了安全、稳定且保持示教特性的机器人技能自适应，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证了其优于基线的性能。

Abstract: Skill adaptation frameworks based on reinforcement learning often require restrictive assumptions to maintain stability, such as fixed observations or tightly controlled exploration schedules. In cluttered and dynamic environments, however, unrestricted exploration can lead to unsafe behaviour and unstable learning, particularly when task-relevant observations lie near obstacles or involve moving objects. In this work, we present Dist-GPRL, a distance-aware and safety-guided reinforcement learning framework for structured robot skill adaptation. Building upon Gaussian Process (GP)-based skill parameterisation, our framework sequentially adapts overlapping local windows of sparse trajectory via-points rather than modifying the complete skill at every policy step. Raw policy outputs are correlated through the GP covariance structure, producing temporally coherent trajectory updates while reducing the action-space and credit-assignment difficulties associated with global trajectory adaptation. Safety is incorporated through two complementary forms of guidance. A safe-subspace prior derived from the Hausdorff Approximation Planner (HAP) biases policy exploration toward feasible regions, while dynamically updated distance field clearance and gradient rewards provide local obstacle awareness. A trajectory-kinematics similarity regulariser further preserves the demonstrated velocity and acceleration characteristics during adaptation. We evaluate the framework on two dynamic object-manipulation tasks in simulation and transfer the learned policy to real-world robot execution. Experimental results demonstrate higher task success, lower collision frequency, and more stable learning than the baselines, while preserving the kinematic characteristics of the demonstrated skill.

</details>


### [81] [FARM: Reading Failure Signals from the Internal Predictive States of a Frozen Robotic World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11445)
*Haoran Pei,Mingrui Luo,Senbao Wang,Haoran Lv,Jie Guo,Sheng Zhong,Ruixi Ci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FARM，仅训练33,985参数读出层，从冻结VLA-JEPA世界模型预测状态中解码失败信息，实现因果、可迁移、低开销的机器人执行监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人可靠部署需要在线失败监控，但现有监控器多依赖代理信号或需训练专用监控组件。本文探究冻结预训练机器人世界模型的内部预测状态是否已包含可直接解码的失败信息。

Method: FARM在冻结的VLA-JEPA预测状态上训练一个33,985参数的监督读出层，输出逐步失败分数与因果轨迹风险；通过五折out-of-fold评估及固定读出迁移/仅读出适配测试部署偏移，不更新预测主干。

Result: 七个源任务上 pooled AUROC/AUPRC 达85.68/88.59，在10任务基准上与15个匹配基线相比Seen性能最佳；在PIPER X、SO-101、Franka四个真实机器人群体上验证迁移；能区分失败与部分因果历史；冻结状态可用后额外平均CUDA延迟0.2256 ms。

Conclusion: 冻结的预测世界模型状态可作为可复用特征，支持因果、可迁移且低开销的执行监控。

Abstract: Reliable robot deployment requires online failure monitoring, yet existing monitors mainly derive risk from proxy signals or train dedicated monitoring components. We ask whether the internal predictive states of a frozen pretrained robotic world model already contain directly decodable failure information. Failure-Aware Readout from World Models (FARM) trains only a 33,985-parameter supervised readout over frozen VLA-JEPA predictive states, producing step-wise failure scores and causal trajectory risk. Five-fold out-of-fold evaluation across seven source tasks reaches 85.68/88.59 pooled AUROC/AUPRC, and FARM gives the best Seen performance among 15 matched baselines on the 10-task benchmark. Across four real-robot populations on PIPER X, SO-101, and Franka, fixed-readout transfer and readout-only adaptation test deployment shifts without updating the predictive backbone. FARM also discriminates failures from partial causal histories and adds 0.2256 ms mean CUDA latency once the frozen state is available. These results support frozen predictive world-model states as reusable features for causal, transferable, and low-overhead execution monitoring.

</details>


### [82] [3D Euler-Angle Orientation Control for Two-Ray Fading Mitigation in Maritime Air-to-Sea Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11476)
*Mohammed Bajja,Abdoul Karim A. H. Saliah,Hajar El Hammouti,Daniel Bonilla Licea,Giuseppe Silano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将三维姿态作为物理层控制变量，通过天线相位中心位移重构海面双径干涉，结合软最小值准则与NMPC提升海上空海链路吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上空海链路受视距直射径与海面反射径的破坏性叠加影响，产生深度衰落。现有缓解方法主要优化无人机位置或轨迹，未利用姿态这一自由度。

Method: 在小角度与远场假设下，将相长干涉条件化为欧拉角上的仿射约束，得到最小范数姿态候选集的闭式解；再用可微软最小值规则生成平滑参考，由全驱动倾转多旋翼上的约束非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）进行跟踪。

Result: 相比仅俯仰基准，累积吞吐量提升11.4%；相比零姿态基准，提升22.2%；同时保持轨迹跟踪并满足执行器限制。

Conclusion: 三维姿态可作为有效的物理层控制变量，用以塑造双径干涉、缓解海面反射衰落，并在多旋翼平台上实现吞吐量增益。

Abstract: Maritime Air-to-Sea links are dominated by a line-of-sight ray and a sea-surface reflected ray whose destructive combination produces deep fades. Existing mitigation strategies optimize Unmanned Aerial Vehicle position or trajectory but leave attitude unexploited. This paper treats the full three dimensional attitude as a physical-layer control variable that shapes the two-ray interference through antenna phase-center displacement. Under small-angle and far-field assumptions, the constructive-interference condition reduces to an affine constraint in the Euler angles and admits a closed-form family of minimum-norm attitude candidates. A differentiable soft-minimum rule yields a smooth reference tracked by a constrained Nonlinear Model Predictive Control controller on a fully-actuated tilting multirotor. The proposed scheme increases cumulative throughput by 11.4% over a pitch-only benchmark and 22.2% over a zero-orientation baseline, while preserving trajectory tracking and respecting actuator limits.

</details>


### [83] [CARLAverse: A Highly Modular, Distributed, and Multimodal Framework for Human-in-the-Loop Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11478)
*Patrick Rebling,Philipp Nenninger,Reiner Kriesten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARLAverse 是一个开源多模态仿真生态系统，通过分布式物理架构解决多智能体人在环（HITL）仿真中的网络延迟与同步瓶颈，支持驾驶、骑行和行人三种脆弱道路使用者（VRU）在同一虚拟环境中进行跨机构的高保真交互实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要在涉及脆弱道路使用者（VRU）的混合交通场景中进行全面测试，但纯人工智能体难以还原真实的人类社会性协商行为。现有 HITL 多智能体平台受限于网络延迟与同步约束，无法提供高保真的触觉反馈，从而影响实验的有效性与安全性。

Method: 提出 CARLAverse：在模块化硬件抽象的基础上，将 DrivoCARLA（驾驶）、CycloCARLA（骑行）与 WalkoCARLA（行人）三类模拟器集成到共享虚拟环境。核心方法是分布式物理架构——把对延迟敏感的 ego 动力学与高频力反馈放在客户端节点本地计算，由中心 CARLA 服务器统一编排 NPC 物理与全局交通，从而将触觉控制环路与网络瓶颈解耦。

Result: 通过将触觉控制环路与网络瓶颈解耦，CARLAverse 能够在跨机构、可扩展的 HITL 实验中保持物理沉浸感，避免因网络延迟导致的触觉反馈失真，同时支持多类 VRU 模拟器在同一环境中的协同运行。

Conclusion: CARLAverse 以开源形式（代码与文档见所给链接）提供了一个可扩展、跨机构部署的多模态 HITL 仿真生态，为混合交通场景下 VRU 与自动驾驶的交互研究提供了高保真、低延迟的测试平台。

Abstract: The development of autonomous driving demands comprehensive testing in mixed-traffic scenarios involving vulnerable road users (VRUs), where purely artificial agents often fail to capture authentic human social negotiations. While human-in-the-loop (HITL) simulators enable safe investigation of these interactions, existing multi-agent platforms struggle with the network latency and synchronization constraints required for high-fidelity haptic feedback. To resolve this, we present CARLAverse, an open-source, multimodal simulation ecosystem. Extending modular hardware abstraction, CARLAverse integrates driving (DrivoCARLA), cycling (CycloCARLA), and pedestrian (WalkoCARLA) simulators into a shared virtual environment. Its core methodological contribution is a distributed physics architecture: latency-critical ego dynamics and high-frequency force feedback are computed locally on client nodes, while a central CARLA server orchestrates non-player character (NPC) physics and global traffic. By decoupling haptic control loops from network bottlenecks, CARLAverse enables scalable, cross-institutional HITL experiments without compromising physical immersion. Code and documentation: https://git.ieem-ka.de/simulator-environments/carlaverse

</details>


### [84] [Using Automated Vehicles Operational Data to Confirm Safety and Anticipate Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11549)
*Riccardo Donà,Espedito Rusciano,Germana Trentadue,Anastasios Tsakalidis,Maria Cristina Galassi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 欧盟新法规引入自动驾驶系统在役监测与报告（ISMR）数据采集框架，借鉴UNECE最佳实践及核能、交通领域经验，通过真实世界数据验证安全目标、构建场景库并共享经验，弥补传统型式认证的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统型式认证（homologation）验证方案无法涵盖自动驾驶系统（ADS）安全评估中大量未知因素，仅靠认证阶段的验证难以确认真实道路运行中的安全目标是否达成，因此需要引入真实世界数据采集机制。

Method: 欧盟立法者采纳了在役监测与报告（ISMR）数据采集条款，其框架借鉴UNECE层面的最佳实践，并参考核能生产与交通领域类似的数据采集监管方法。该机制采集ADS真实运行数据，并将安全相关驾驶场景存入场景库。

Result: 真实世界数据使主管安全机构能够获取确认型式认证安全目标所需信息；运行中发现的安全相关场景可存入场景库，用于研究其他ADS类型在该交通冲突中的应对方式；经验可在OEM与安全机构间共享。

Conclusion: ISMR被视为妥善应对ADS安全评估挑战的必要工具，尤其针对传统型式认证验证方案无法揭示的诸多未知因素。

Abstract: European Union (EU) policymakers adopted revolutionary data collection provisions for Automated Driving Systems (ADS) in the recently approved regulation that allows driverless vehicles to be operated on public roads. The framework is inspired by best practices developed at the United Nations Economic Commission for Europe(UNECE) level: the In-Service Monitoring and Reporting (ISMR); and by similar operational data collection regulatory approaches in nuclear energy production and transportation fields. The collection of real-world data will enable the competent safety authorities to gather the information needed to confirm the homologation safety target. Safety-relevant driving scenarios discovered during the real-world operation of a given ADS can also be stored in a scenario catalogue to investigate how other ADS types might have addressed such a traffic conflict. Moreover, lessons learnt deriving from the data collected can be shared among original equipment manufacturers (OEMs) and safety authorities. Ultimately, the ISMR is recognised as a necessary tool to properly tackle the challenges associated with ADS safety assessment given the number of unknowns that might remain undisclosed by leveraging the traditional homologation validation scheme only.

</details>


### [85] [CAP: Continuously Adaptive Perception-Blind Humanoid Locomotion via Learned Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11553)
*Hongjin Chen,Zijun Xu,Shihao Ma,Yi Zhao,Xilai Liu,Ke Ma,Wei Zhang,Chunyang Xie,Pengfei Li,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CAP——单阶段人形运动策略，用感知世界模型编码器作为学习式去噪器从损坏深度重建干净深度，并配本体感受变分编码器提供无深度状态信息，实现感知鲁棒的复杂地形运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂地形上的人形运动依赖前瞻性外部感知来预判障碍，但该信号在真实部署中不可靠，会部分或间歇性失效。现有感知策略假定深度观测干净且同分布；近期统一感知与盲控的尝试多在独立子策略间路由或切换，未利用部分损坏深度中仍可恢复的信息。

Method: CAP为单阶段人形运动策略：感知世界模型编码器经训练为学习式去噪器，从损坏深度输入重建干净深度；协同激活的本体感受变分编码器提供无深度的身体状态信息。训练配方将世界模型输入端的深度噪声课程与面向策略隐变量的世界模型特征dropout配对，使策略暴露于整个感知质量谱的失效情形。

Result: 仿真中，深度信息有效时CAP匹配或优于感知基线；随感知退化，其性能下降比二值切换基线更平滑。在Unitree G1上，受控试验与室内外部署验证了在间歇遮挡、真实传感器损坏与室外深度伪影下的感知鲁棒运动。

Conclusion: 通过去噪式世界模型重建与本体感受变分编码的耦合训练，CAP以单阶段策略统一利用可恢复的损坏深度信息，避免子策略切换，实现跨感知质量谱的平滑退化与真机感知鲁棒运动。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion across complex terrain demands forward-looking exteroception to anticipate obstacles, yet this signal is unreliable in real-world deployment, failing partially and intermittently. Existing perceptive policies often assume that depth observations remain clean and in-distribution, while recent attempts to unify perceptive and blind control typically route or switch between separate sub-policies, leaving recoverable information in partially corrupted depth unexploited. We instead propose CAP, a single-stage humanoid locomotion policy that recovers this signal with a perceptive world-model encoder trained as a learned denoiser to reconstruct clean depth from a corrupted input, together with a co-active proprioceptive variational encoder that supplies depth-free body-state information. A coupled training recipe pairs a depth-noise curriculum on the world-model input with world-model feature dropout on the policy-facing latent, exposing the policy to failures across the entire perception-quality spectrum. In simulation, CAP matches or improves upon perceptive baselines when depth remains informative, and degrades more smoothly than a binary-switching baseline as perception worsens. On the Unitree G1, controlled trials and indoor-outdoor deployments demonstrate perception-robust locomotion under intermittent occlusion, real-sensor corruption, and outdoor depth artifacts.

</details>


### [86] [Memory as Plans: World-Action Modeling with Memory-Grounded Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11561)
*Sizhe Zhao,Haozhe Xie,Weiyu Zhao,Chenchu Zhang,Huan Wang,Chenyang Wang,Qinglin Liu,Shengping Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MaP-WAM 提出"记忆即规划"（Memory-as-Plans）框架，将依赖记忆的世界-动作建模分解为记忆锚定的规划与规划条件化执行，把长期多模态情景记忆作为规划时证据而非反复喂给执行器，在 RMBench 上取得 83.3% 成功率、真机 78.0% 成功率，且执行器推理延迟随历史增长近似恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流机器人策略采用马尔可夫式建模，但许多真实操作任务本质上是非马尔可夫的，需要超出当前观测的长时程记忆。现有记忆机制多依赖语言摘要、不断增长的视觉窗口或两者组合，易丢失细粒度视觉证据，并在历史覆盖范围与执行效率之间面临权衡。

Method: 将记忆依赖的世界-动作建模分解为记忆锚定的规划与规划条件化执行。记忆表示为已完成分段记录，包含语言指令与稀疏视觉上下文，并将其转化为紧凑计划（下一分段的语言计划与对应视觉引导）。World-Action-Progress（WAP）模型在推理时联合预测动作块与执行进度，以未知时长执行每个计划；通过计划-观测对齐校准预测进度，实现自适应分段切换与闭环上下文更新。执行器上下文长度保持固定，结构化注意力支持规划与执行两端的键值缓存。

Result: 在 RMBench 上达到 83.3% 成功率（当前最优），真机任务成功率 78.0%；执行器推理延迟随任务历史增长基本保持恒定。

Conclusion: 以"记忆即规划"解耦规划与执行，可在保留细粒度多模态记忆证据的同时避免执行器上下文膨胀，兼顾长时程非马尔可夫任务的性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Mainstream robotic policies often adopt a Markovian formulation, but many complex real-world manipulation tasks are inherently non-Markovian, requiring long-horizon memory beyond the current observation. Existing memory mechanisms often rely on language summaries, growing visual windows, or their combinations, and may therefore lose fine-grained visual evidence or face a trade-off between history coverage and execution efficiency. We introduce MaP-WAM, a Memory-as-Plans framework that decomposes memory-dependent world-action modeling into memory-grounded planning and plan-conditioned execution, and uses long-term multimodal episodic context as planning-time evidence rather than repeatedly conditioning the executor on the full history. MaP-WAM represents memory as completed segment records containing language instructions and sparse visual context, and converts this episodic memory into compact plans comprising the next segment-level language plan and corresponding visual guidance. A World-Action-Progress (WAP) model executes each plan over an unknown duration by jointly predicting action chunks and corresponding execution progress at inference time, calibrating predicted progress through plan-observation alignment for adaptive segment transitions and closed-loop context updates. MaP-WAM keeps the executor context length fixed, while structured attention further enables key-value caching in both planning and execution. MaP-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on RMBench with an 83.3% success rate and attains 78.0% success on real-robot tasks, while maintaining approximately constant executor inference latency as task history grows.

</details>


### [87] [Quasi-static analysis of passive stability in a novel underactuated multi-finger hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11579)
*Léonie Plancoulaine,Sylvain Guégan,Franck Plestan,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出准静态解析方法评估欠驱动多指手的被动稳定性，设计了一种含差动弹簧滑块机构的三指手，并分析差动机构对抓取行为的影响及物体尺寸对圆柱/球形抓取稳定平衡位姿的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动机械手能以较少驱动器实现自适应抓取，但被抓物体的稳定平衡位姿预测仍是难题；差动机构如何影响整体抓取行为及尺寸变化下的稳定性尚不明确。

Method: 引入一种集成差动弹簧-滑块机构的新型三指手架构；采用准静态解析方法评估被动稳定性；针对圆柱形与球形两种典型抓取，分析差动机构作用及物体尺寸对稳定平衡构型的影响。

Result: 建立了面向欠驱动多指手的准静态稳定性评估框架，并揭示了差动机构对抓取行为的影响规律，以及物体尺寸与稳定平衡位姿之间的依赖关系（圆柱与球形两类抓取）。

Conclusion: 差动弹簧-滑块机构使三指手兼具通用性与自适应抓取能力；准静态解析方法可有效预测被动稳定性，物体尺寸显著影响稳定平衡构型。

Abstract: Underactuated robotic hands achieve adaptive and robust grasping with a reduced number of actuators, but predicting the stable equilibrium pose of the grasped object remains a significant challenge. This paper introduces a quasi-static analytical approach to assess passive stability in underactuated multi-finger hands. A novel three-finger hand architecture integrating a differential spring-loaded slider mechanism is introduced, enabling versatile and adaptive grasping. The study focuses on how the differential mechanism influences the overall grasp behavior and analyzes the effect of object size on the stable equilibrium configurations for two canonical grasp types: cylindrical and spherical.

</details>


### [88] [Contact-Aware Incremental Model Predictive Control for an Underactuated Aerial Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11661)
*Darwin Liu,Tamas Keviczky,Sihao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种接触感知控制框架，使欠驱动四旋翼搭配简单单连杆机械臂即可在竖直与倾斜表面实现五自由度末端位姿与接触力同步跟踪的空中书写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在空中平台上实现书写等接触作业，通常需要全驱动平台、复杂机械臂或专用力/力矩传感器，硬件要求高、成本大。作者希望验证：仅靠标准欠驱动四旋翼与简单刚性单连杆臂，也能实现稳健的接触力与末端位姿同步跟踪。

Method: 框架结合两种控制方法：一是非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），用于在小参考穿透深度下精确跟踪末端执行器位置与法向力，并在不同控制器整定下保持一致性能；二是全身增量式非线性动态逆（INDI），用于在接触过程中抵抗摩擦与气动扰动。验证平台为四旋翼空中机械臂，配备刚性、单连杆、单自由度机械臂。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界实验中验证，书写实验覆盖竖直与倾斜表面、多种参考力、不同摩擦条件以及风扰动。结果表明，在标准欠驱动四旋翼加简单刚性单连杆臂上，无需全驱动平台、复杂机械臂或专用力/力矩传感，即可实现稳健的五自由度末端位姿与接触力同步跟踪。

Conclusion: 该接触感知控制框架在欠驱动空中平台上实现了稳健的接触式书写，证明简化硬件即可完成高维度接触作业，为空中操作的低成本、轻量化实现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present a robust contact-aware control framework for aerial writing on an underactuated platform. The framework combines nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) for accurate end-effector position and normal-force tracking at small reference penetration depths, with consistent performance across controller tunings, with whole-body incremental nonlinear dynamic inversion (INDI) for robustness to frictional and aerodynamic disturbances during contact. The proposed controllers are validated on a quadrotor-based aerial manipulator with a rigid, single-link, one-degree-of-freedom (DoF) arm in simulation and real-world experiments. The aerial writing experiments span vertical and inclined surfaces, multiple reference forces, different friction conditions, and wind disturbances. The results demonstrate that robust simultaneous five-DoF end-effector pose and contact-force tracking is achievable on a standard underactuated quadrotor with a simple, rigid, single-link arm, without requiring a fully actuated platform, a complex arm, or dedicated force/torque sensing.

</details>


### [89] [ActSafeGuard: Differentiable and Training-Aligned Constraint Enforcement for Flow-Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11697)
*Jianming Ma,Rongjun Jin,Xiaxi Si,Yang Zhang,Yiheng Li,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ActSafeGuard，一种面向流匹配策略的可微、训练对齐安全防护层，将硬动作可行性集成到策略学习中，实现100%逐步安全率且不损任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA与WAM虽在通用机器人操作上表现强劲，但生成动作可能违反硬物理约束，导致部署不安全或不可行；已有安全方法或仅优化统计安全目标而缺乏逐步确定性保证，或仅在推理阶段纠正不安全动作，造成策略训练与执行不匹配。

Method: 提出ActSafeGuard，一种面向流匹配策略的可微、训练对齐安全防护层；通过解析射线缩放（ray-scaling）算子设计，使边界感知梯度引导模型自然学习受约束流形，将硬动作可行性嵌入策略学习而非作为推理期外部组件。

Result: 在多种标准基础骨干（π0.5与Fast-WAM）及多类任务上的大量实验表明，ActSafeGuard稳定达到100%逐步安全率，同时完全保持甚至提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: ActSafeGuard为安全具身智能部署提供了可扩展、最小侵入的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World-Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet their generated actions may violate hard physical constraints and therefore be unsafe or infeasible for deployment. Existing safety approaches either optimize statistical safety objectives without deterministic per-step guarantees or correct unsafe actions only during inference, creating a mismatch between policy training and execution. We introduce ActSafeGuard, a differentiable and training-aligned safeguard layer for flow-matching based policies. ActSafeGuard integrates hard action feasibility into policy learning, not merely treating safety as an inference-time external component. Through an analytical ray-scaling operator design, ActSafeGuard enables boundary-aware gradients to guide the model to naturally learn constrained manifolds. Extensive experiments on multiple standard foundation backbones ($π_{0.5}$ and Fast-WAM) across various tasks demonstrate that ActSafeGuard consistently achieves a $100\%$ step safety rate while fully preserving or even boosting task success rates, providing a scalable and minimally invasive solution for safe embodied AI deployment.

</details>


### [90] [Aerodynamic Prior-Free Coordinated Trajectory Generation and Tracking Control for a Tail-Sitter UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11698)
*Erchao Rong,Zihao Liu,Junning Liang,Jianguo Wang,Xiao Jie,Haoran Fu,Ziliang Chen,Ximin Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种尾座式无人机协同轨迹生成与跟踪控制框架，无需特定机体的气动先验，通过分阶段气动建模策略，在全飞行包络内实现高精度轨迹跟踪，并完成真实飞行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尾座式无人机在全飞行包络内气动高度非线性，传统方法依赖特定机体的气动辨识先验，成本高且泛化性差，因此需要一种不依赖气动先验、能应对强非线性气动的规划与控制框架。

Method: 针对规划与跟踪的不同功能特性，采用分阶段气动建模策略：在协同飞行下使用phi-theory模型推导解析微分平坦映射用于轨迹生成；同时建立简化但局部精确的模型用于预测控制，实现实时气动参数估计。

Result: 在仿真及温和风况下的真实飞行测试中进行了广泛评估，表现出高精度跟踪能力及对测试气动条件的适应性。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首次在不依赖气动辨识实验的情况下，于真实世界中实现尾座式无人机全飞行包络内测试飞行状态的精确轨迹跟踪，源代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper presents a coordinated trajectory generation and tracking control framework for a tail-sitter unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV), which does not require aerodynamic priors identified for a specific airframe while addressing the challenge of flight control under highly nonlinear aerodynamics across the full flight envelope. The core innovation lies in employing phase-specific aerodynamic modeling strategies for planning and tracking, tailored to their distinct functional characteristics, without requiring airframe-specific aerodynamic priors. Specifically, the phi-theory model under coordinated flight is employed to derive an analytic differential flatness mapping, and a simplified but locally accurate model is established for predictive control to enable real-time aerodynamic parameter estimation. The proposed framework is evaluated extensively through both simulation and challenging real-world flight tests under mild wind conditions, showing high-precision tracking and adaptability across the tested aerodynamic conditions. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first real-world demonstration of accurate trajectory tracking over tested flight regimes spanning the full envelope of a tail-sitter UAV without relying on aerodynamic identification campaigns. The source code of our framework is available at: https://github.com/SYSU-HILAB/AP-PnC.

</details>


### [91] [Reflex-Informed Neuromuscular Reinforcement Learning for Muscle-Driven Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11733)
*Jian Zhou,Xingyu Zhang,Rui Ma,Yu Cao,Shane Xie,Zhi-qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“反射信息引导的神经肌肉强化学习”（Reflex-Informed Neuromuscular RL）框架，用于肌肉驱动的人体运动生成：以固定的相位依赖反射控制器作为底层神经肌肉控制机制，强化学习策略输出四个具有生物力学意义的残差参数，动态调节髋摆动、膝支撑与踝推进相关的关键反射增益和阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉驱动运动生成虽具有物理基础，但现有方法难以同时满足生理合理性与对肌肉骨骼能力变化和外部扰动的适应性，这是该领域的核心挑战。

Method: 提出 Reflex-Informed Neuromuscular Reinforcement Learning 框架。底层使用固定的相位依赖反射控制器作为神经肌肉控制机制；上层 RL 策略根据当前状态输出四个生物力学有意义的残差参数，用于调制髋摆动、膝支撑、踝推进相关的反射增益与阈值。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在正常行走条件下生成了生理合理的运动，具有更高的运动学精度与动态一致性，并改善了双侧对称性和步到步一致性；学习到的策略在肌肉无力和外部扰动下无需重新训练仍保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 将固定反射控制与残差式强化学习相结合，可在保持生理合理性的同时提升对肌肉能力变化及外部干扰的适应性，为肌肉驱动运动生成提供了一种有效途径。

Abstract: Muscle-driven locomotion provides a physically grounded approach to generating realistic human movement. However, achieving both physiological plausibility and adaptability to changes in musculoskeletal capacity and external disturbances remains a fundamental challenge. To address this limitation, we propose a Reflex-Informed Neuromuscular Reinforcement Learning framework for muscle-driven locomotion. Within this framework, a fixed phase-dependent reflex controller serves as the underlying neuromuscular control mechanism, while the reinforcement learning policy produces four biomechanically meaningful residual parameters to modulate key reflex gains and thresholds associated with hip swing, knee support, and ankle propulsion according to the current state. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework generates physiologically plausible locomotion with improved kinematic accuracy and dynamic consistency, as well as better bilateral symmetry and stride-to-stride consistency under nominal walking conditions. The learned policy remains robust under muscle weakness and external perturbations without retraining.

</details>


### [92] [SEED-UMI: Sharing the Exoskeleton between human and robot for onE-to-one Dexterous demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11753)
*Tengbo Yu,Jiahao Wu,Daohan Li,Bingxu Chen,Hao Liu,Xiaojian Ma,Hangxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEED-UMI让人类与机器人佩戴同一套外骨骼，将重定向问题转化为成对跨具身监督，直接用外骨骼腕部相机原始图像训练策略，在五项接触密集任务上数据采集效率提升3.0倍，平均成功率达70.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手模仿学习的瓶颈在于难以采集能够忠实迁移到机器人的接触密集演示。此前的可穿戴外骨骼系统仅记录人类侧数据，并通过在自由空间中校准的开环映射进行重定向，在接触发生时性能退化。

Method: 提出SEED-UMI框架：人类与机器人佩戴相同外骨骼，关节编码器成为物理共享的测量量，安装在外骨骼上的腕部相机在人类数据采集与机器人策略执行期间观测同一外部机构。由此将重定向转化为成对的跨具身监督，策略可直接在原始的外骨骼中心腕部图像上训练，无需分割或图像修补。

Result: 在五项接触密集任务上，SEED-UMI的数据采集效率比基于外骨骼的遥操作高3.0倍，平均策略执行成功率为70.0%。

Conclusion: 通过人类与机器人共享同一外骨骼硬件与观测视角，SEED-UMI消除了传统开环重定向在接触下的退化问题，实现了高效数据采集与直接原始图像策略学习，验证了共享具身硬件在灵巧手模仿学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Imitation learning for dexterous hands is bottlenecked by the difficulty of collecting contact-rich demonstrations that transfer faithfully to the robot. Prior wearable-exoskeleton systems record only on the human side and retarget via open-loop mappings calibrated in free space, which degrade under contact. We present SEED-UMI, a framework in which both the human and the robot wear the same exoskeleton: joint encoders become a physically shared measurement, and wrist cameras mounted to the exoskeleton observe the same outer mechanism during both human data collection and robot policy rollouts. This turns retargeting into paired cross-embodiment supervision and lets policies train directly on raw exoskeleton-centric wrist images, without segmentation or inpainting. On five contact-rich tasks, SEED-UMI achieves 3.0x greater data collection efficiency than exoskeleton-based teleoperation and a 70.0% average rollout success rate.

</details>


### [93] [Visual-SLAM for the detection of hidden tomatoes in greenhouses by Hierarchical Localization and GLOMAPfor robotized harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11766)
*Fernando Cañadas-Aránega,José C. Moreno,José L. Blanco-Claraco,Francisco Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向温室农业的低成本单目视觉SLAM系统，结合GLOMAP与Hierarchical Localization，实现番茄作物的三维建图，并通过人工真值验证了重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室内部作物监测是研究重点，但传统高性能传感器（如LiDAR、双目相机）成本高昂，限制了推广应用。因此需要一种成本更低、专为农业场景（如温室番茄建图）定制的视觉建图方案。

Method: 采用单目相机构建Visual-SLAM系统；在ROS 2 Humble上开发节点，部署于机器人上采集番茄图像并离线处理；将基于Structure-From-Motion的GLOMAP建图器与Hierarchical Localization工具箱集成，生成三维模型。定位采用层次化粗到细策略：先全局检索生成位置假设，再在候选区域内融合局部特征。

Result: 系统正确识别了番茄簇，并能准确刻画被遮挡、传统视觉技术难以获取的番茄；重建的三维模型与人工真值在果实尺寸、质心位置和朝向上对比，验证了几何精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的低成本单目视觉流程可有效完成温室番茄三维建图，具备几何准确性，为后续生长模式分析与农业管理优化奠定基础。

Abstract: Advanced crop monitoring inside greenhouses is becoming one of the primary objectives of research centers. High-performance sensors, such as LiDAR or stereo cameras, have traditionally been employed for this purpose, though these often have a high cost. This work proposes a Visual-SLAM system using a monocular camera, which is significantly more cost-effective and specifically tailored for agricultural applications, such as mapping tomato crops in a greenhouse. Tests were carried out on a real tomato bunch, located in the Agroconnect experimental greenhouse. A ROS 2 Humble node was developed to run on the robot in order to capture images of these crops, which were then stored for offline processing. To generate a 3D mapped model for the crop in the greenhouse, the GLOMAP mapper, based on Structure-From-Motion, was integrated with the Hierarchical Localization toolbox. This initial mapping is a foundation for future, more advanced algorithms to analyze growth patterns, and optimize agricultural management. The system leverages a hierarchical localization paradigm based on a coarse-to-fine strategy: it first performs global retrieval to generate location hypotheses, then combines local features within the identified candidate regions. The results show a correct identification of the tomato cluster, correctly characterising the tomato that is occluded and inaccessible by classical vision technologies. The reconstructed 3D model was further validated against manual ground-truth measurements of fruit size, centroid position, and orientation, confirming the geometric accuracy of the proposed low-cost monocular pipeline.

</details>


### [94] [Rapid Learning of Dexterous In-Hand Pen Writing through Real-Time Jacobian Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11775)
*Kai Stewart,Yasunori Toshimitsu,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理机器人上"手-物体"系统实时任务雅可比估计的具身控制方法，无需解析模型、仿真训练或预采集示范，仅用笔记本CPU即可在约18秒初始化后完成灵巧的手内写字，并在三种拟人手系统上验证了亚毫米级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟人手的手内灵巧操作是机器人灵巧性尚未攻克的难题。手-物交互具有接触丰富、高度动态的特点，现有学习类方法（强化学习、模仿学习）通常依赖大量建模或数据采集：现代仿真器无法完全复现所需的接触复杂度，而面向模仿学习的灵巧示范数据采集本身仍是未解问题。因此作者希望寻找一种计算简单、数据高效的替代方案。

Method: 在物理机器人上对"手与物体的组合系统"进行实时任务雅可比估计，并据此构建具身控制器。该控制器不依赖解析的手-物运动学/接触模型，不需要仿真训练，也不需要预先采集的任务示范，仅使用笔记本电脑的CPU运行，并在初始化约18秒后开始手内写字并持续在线自适应。作者将同一估计器/控制器形式化方法应用于三种拟人机械手系统（一台实体、两台仿真），以验证其与具体本体无关的通用性。

Result: 在实体机器人上实现了握持笔的类人手内关节运动，可在空中和纸面上书写字母与图形，多次运行的平均面内精度达0.6毫米（亚毫米级）。

Conclusion: 这是首次展示拟人手仅通过纯手内运动、握持笔书写任意单笔画轨迹；它表明，实现灵巧操作不必依赖计算与数据开销巨大的强化学习和模仿学习，计算简单且数据高效的算法同样可行，为灵巧操作提供了新的替代路线。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation of a grasped object with an anthropomorphic hand is an unsolved frontier for robot dexterity. The contact-richness and highly dynamic nature of object-hand interactions tend to require extensive modeling or data-collection efforts for learning-based approaches. Modern simulators used for reinforcement learning (RL) cannot fully replicate the required contact complexity, while collecting dexterous demonstrations for imitation learning (IL) remains an open problem. In this research, we present an embodied control approach based on real-time task Jacobian estimation of the combined hand and object system on the physical robot. Using only the CPU on a laptop, the proposed controller begins in-hand pen writing after approximately 18 s of initialization and continues to adapt online, without an analytic hand--object kinematic/contact model, simulation training, or precollected task demonstrations. We demonstrate that the same estimator/controller formulation works on three anthropomorphic robotic hand systems (one physical, two simulated) to show human-like, in-hand articulation of a grasped pen by an embodiment-independent formulation. Sub-millimeter in-plane precision (mean 0.6 mm across runs) is achieved across letters and shapes written in the air and on paper on a physical robot. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an anthropomorphic hand writing arbitrary single-stroke trajectories with a grasped pen through purely in-hand motion, and it showcases an alternative to compute- and data-heavy approaches such as RL and IL for achieving dexterous manipulation through computationally simple and data-efficient algorithms.

</details>


### [95] [Learning Agent-based Model Predictive Control for Holistic Vehicle Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11871)
*Jiaming Zhong,Reza Valiollahi Mehrizi,Mohammad Pirani,Chao Yu,Alireza Kasaiezadeh,Yash Vardhan Pant,Amir Khajepour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LAMPC混合控制方案，结合基于模型的AMPC与高斯过程回归学习，预测未知贡献以提升多智能体系统整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMPC作为分布式方案能协同各智能体实现全局最优，但其最优性高度依赖预测精度，需已知所有智能体及其贡献，实际中过于理想化。

Method: 将基于模型的AMPC与基于数据的学习方法结合；以在线数据管理策略增强的高斯过程回归（GPR）为学习核心预测未知贡献；设计多步预测机制沿预测时域利用GPR学习潜力；以预测均值补全MPC系统模型；构建随机框架，基于预测方差采用软机会约束保证控制安全与可行性。

Result: 仿真与实验表明，LAMPC优于传统AMPC：在已充分学习场景中跟踪性能更高，在欠学习场景下亦能始终保证约束满足；方案实时实现高效，且灵活适配任意控制智能体拓扑。

Conclusion: LAMPC是一种实用且灵活的混合控制方案，借助学习能力弥补AMPC对全局信息依赖的局限，兼顾性能、安全性与实时性。

Abstract: Agent-based model predictive control (AMPC) has recently been proposed as a distributed scheme that collaborates with all agents to achieve optimal holistic performance. However, its optimality highly depends on the prediction accuracy that requires all agents or their contributions to be known, which is too idealistic for actual implementation. This research proposes a novel practical hybrid control scheme - learning agent-based MPC (LAMPC), combining the model-based AMPC approach and data-based learning methods to improve the holistic vehicle performance for multi-agent systems. The Gaussian process regression (GPR) enhanced by an online data management strategy serves as the learning core to predict unknown contributions. A novel multi-step prediction mechanism leverages the GPR learning potential along the horizon. The predicted mean, representing the learned unknown contributions, completes the system model in the MPC for more accurate control. Meanwhile, a stochastic framework is formulated to guarantee control safety and feasibility using soft chance constraints based on the prediction variance. Both simulations and experiments show that, with the learning capability, LAMPC outperforms the traditional AMPC. LAMPC can achieve higher tracking performance in well-learned scenarios and always guarantee constraint satisfaction even in less-learned scenarios. Moreover, the proposed hybrid control scheme is efficient for real-time implementation and is flexible to any control agent topology.

</details>


### [96] [UniMPA: A Unified Memory-Prediction-Action Model via Action-Grounded Transition Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11875)
*Wei Li,Rui Shao,Jie He,Lingsen Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UniMPA统一记忆-预测-动作模型，通过共享的"动作锚定转移接口"解决VLA模型观察-动作学习中的三类转移可实现性问题（转移歧义、预测-执行失配、经验-实现失配）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽提升了机器人操作能力，但观察到动作的学习受限于"转移可实现性鸿沟"，具体表现为三个耦合问题：(1)转移歧义，视觉相似的当前观察可能对应不同操作阶段并隐含不同后续转移；(2)预测-执行失配，视觉上合理的预测未来观察不一定对应物理可实现的转移；(3)经验-实现失配，历史上可执行的动作模式未必能在当前场景实现预期转移，需要上下文自适应。

Method: UniMPA通过共享的动作锚定转移接口统一解决上述问题：(1)引入持续-选择性未来预测（Persistent-Selective Future Prediction），以持续潜变量流跟踪任务级进展、以转移关键像素流通过记忆锚定预测选择性解析细粒度交互变化，从而消解转移歧义；(2)预测的转移查询时序视觉-动作记忆库（Visual-Action Memory Bank），检索历史上已实现的视觉-动作经验，将未来预测锚定在可执行证据上，以评估其物理可执行性；(3)动作-视觉记忆库（Action-Visual Memory Bank）从历史动作演化中检索视觉锚定的动作原型，并通过原型偏置流（Prototype-Biased Flow）将流源推向历史支持的动作流形，实现上下文感知的细化。

注：所提供摘要未包含实验设置与定量结果。

Result: 所提供摘要为方法性概述，未报告实验数据、基准测试或定量结果。

Conclusion: UniMPA以统一的记忆-预测-动作框架，通过持续-选择性未来预测、双记忆库检索与原型偏置流，在共享的动作锚定转移接口下系统性应对转移歧义、预测-执行失配与经验-实现失配三个耦合问题，为VLA机器人操作中观察到动作的转移可实现性提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have improved robotic manipulation, yet observation-to-action learning remains limited by a fundamental transition realizability gap, manifested in three tightly coupled problems: (i) Transition ambiguity. Visually similar current observations may correspond to different manipulation phases and imply different subsequent transitions. (ii) Prediction--execution mismatch. A visually plausible predicted future observation does not necessarily correspond to a physically realizable transition. (iii) Experience--realization mismatch. A historically executable action pattern may not necessarily realize the intended transition in the current scene and therefore requires context-aware adaptation. Accordingly, we propose UniMPA, a Unified Memory-Prediction-Action model that addresses these problems through a shared action-grounded transition interface. (i) UniMPA introduces Persistent-Selective Future Prediction to resolve transition ambiguity by modeling the intended future state evolution. A persistent latent stream continuously tracks task-level progress, while a transition-critical pixel stream selectively resolves fine-grained interaction changes through memory-grounded prediction. (ii) To assess the physical executability of the anticipated transition, the predicted transition queries a temporal Visual-Action Memory Bank. The bank retrieves historically realized visual-action experience, grounding future prediction in executable evidence. (iii) To adapt executable experience to the current scene, an Action-Visual Memory Bank retrieves visually grounded action prototypes from historical action evolution. Prototype-Biased Flow then shifts the flow source toward a historically supported action manifold for context-aware refinement.

</details>


### [97] [EVPeriscope: Extended Perception across Aerial and Ground Vehicles with Event-based Propeller Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11920)
*Dexter Ong,Vijay Kumar,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EVPeriscope：用地面机器人上的朝上事件相机检测四旋翼桨叶高频视觉特征，实现空地相对定位与四旋翼200 Hz闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧密协同的异构空地团队需要可靠的空中-地面相对定位，但传统帧相机与基准标记对运动模糊、光照变化敏感，且受载荷限制。

Method: 在地面机器人上安装朝上的事件相机，检测四旋翼螺旋桨的高频视觉签名，实现四旋翼的检测、定位与控制；四旋翼可在地面机器人机载传感器退化或被遮挡时充当其扩展感知系统。

Result: 在风速高达15 mph的恶劣野外条件下、白天与夜间均完成实验；在密集植被中地面机器人传感器被遮挡时仍支持定位与闭环导航；四旋翼控制系统以200 Hz完全基于机载感知与计算运行。

Conclusion: 基于事件相机的桨叶感知为空地异构团队提供了一种鲁棒的相对定位与控制方案，可在光照变化、遮挡和复杂野外环境下工作。

Abstract: Reliable relative localization between aerial and ground robots is a key requirement for tightly coordinated heterogeneous teams. This can be difficult to do using conventional frame-based cameras and fiducial markers because they are sensitive to motion blur, lighting variations, and payload constraints. This paper presents EVPeriscope, an event-based perception system that enables detection, localization and control of a quadrotor using an upward-facing event camera on a ground robot by detecting the high-frequency visual signature of its propellers. This system allows the quadrotor to function as an extended perception system for the ground robot when onboard sensors exhibit degradation or occlusion. We demonstrate the capabilities of this marsupial ground-aerial system via experiments in challenging field conditions with wind speeds of up to 15 mph, in both daylight and at night. We show that the system supports localization and closed-loop navigation through dense foliage where the ground robot's sensors are occluded. Our control system for the quadrotor operates at 200 Hz entirely with onboard sensing and computation. More details and experiment videos can be found on the project page: https://ongdexter.github.io/evperiscope.

</details>

### [98] [On the formulation and analysis of a stage-structured eco-evolutionary model with pulsed disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10581)
*Abigail D'Ovidio Long,Abigail Adjei,Swati Patel*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文建立并分析了一个n维阶段结构种群的常微分方程模型，研究周期性脉冲扰动下的生态-进化动态，证明抗性等位基因存在时种群将趋于完全抗性，并给出仅依赖完全抗性动态的持久性条件，最后以斑翅果蝇为案例进行应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受脉冲控制及其对有害生物种群演化影响的驱动，需要一个能同时刻画生态动态（生命史信息）与进化动态（等位基因频率变化）的耦合模型，以理解周期性扰动下抗性进化的机制。

Method: 构建n维阶段结构种群的常微分方程生态-进化耦合模型；结合已有生态-进化模型工作与线性系统理论进行分析；推导持久性条件；并针对斑翅果蝇建立参数化模型进行应用研究。

Result: 证明若种群中存在抗性等位基因，种群将始终趋向完全抗性；建立持久性条件，并表明这些条件仅取决于完全抗性下的系统动态；通过斑翅果蝇案例的参数化模型，揭示物种生态与进化特征同脉冲扰动所致抗性进化之间关系的复杂性。

Conclusion: 该模型将生态与进化动态耦合，提供了抗性进化的理论判据与持久性条件，并可应用于害虫治理实践，为理解脉冲扰动下的抗性演化提供了分析框架。

Abstract: Motivated by the pulsed control and resulting evolution of harmful biological populations, we develop and analyze an ordinary differential equation model of an n-dimensional stage-structured population undergoing periodic disturbances. The eco-evolutionary model couples both ecological dynamics through life cycle information, and evolutionary dynamics of allele frequency changes. Building off prior work in eco-evolutionary models and theory of linear systems, we show that if a resistant allele is present in the population, the population will always approach full resistance. Furthermore, we establish persistence conditions and show that these depend solely on system dynamics at full resistance. Finally, we demonstrate the utility of this model via an application to the Spotted-Winged Drosophila, an invasive species often treated periodically with insecticides. We use a parameterized model to further understand the complexities of relationships between ecological and evolutionary features of a species and its resistance evolution due to pulsed disturbances.

</details>


### [99] [Competition drives excessive recruitment in collective search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11715)
*Hyunjoong Kim,Gayashan Jayavilal,Joshua B Plotkin*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文构建两个群体共享单一觅食点的招募博弈模型，发现长期结果为共存或垄断，并刻画了二者边界及多重纳什均衡条件下的过度招募现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体集体搜索时会通过招募将已发现资源分享给同伴。招募提高在已知地点的觅食者数量，但单个觅食者收益随人数增加而下降，因此存在中间最优招募率；且同一地点可能被多个群体使用。作者旨在分析两个群体共享同一收益递减觅食点时的互动与长期结果。

Method: 建立两群体从单一觅食点觅食的模型，收益随总觅食者人数下降，两群体仅通过共享收益间接互动。求解长期结果（共存或垄断）及二者边界，并分析最佳反应函数、参数影响和纳什均衡。

Result: 长期结果为共存于觅食点或某一群体垄断。群体的最佳反应非单调：随对手招募率上升而提高自身招募率以维持垄断，当维持垄断不再最优时招募率发生不连续下降。群体在地点很快饱和且对手群体较小时放弃垄断。两群体规模相当时存在多个纳什均衡，任一方都可能获得较大份额。两个势均力敌的群体竞争时，双方招募率均高于使其共同收益最大化的水平。

Conclusion: 共享觅食收益下的招募竞争可导致垄断或共存，且均衡中常出现过度招募；势均力敌时个体最终收益低于同等总规模的单一未分裂群体。

Abstract: Groups that search collectively often exploit what they find by recruiting: one member directs others to a site it has found. Recruitment raises the number of members foraging at a known site, but the return per forager may fall as that number grows, so there is an intermediate optimal recruitment rate. In addition, a site may be used by more than one group. Here we analyze a model of two groups that forage from a single site whose return declines with the total number of foragers present. The two groups interact only through this shared return. The long-run outcome is either coexistence at the foraging site or monopoly by one group, and we analyze the boundary between these two outcomes. A group's best response to its rival is not monotone: it increases its own recruitment rate with the rival's recruitment rate in an attempt to preserve a monopoly, and then its recruitment rate drops discontinuously when it is no longer optimal to preserve a monopoly. We analyze how model parameters govern this shift: a group relinquishes monopoly when the site saturates at few foragers and when the rival group is small. When the two groups have comparable size there are multiple Nash equilibria, so either group may end up with the larger share. And when two equally matched groups compete, both recruit above the rate that maximizes their common return, so that each individual ends with less than it would in a single undivided group of the same total size.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [A New Sparse Algorithm for Polynomial GCD over Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10626)
*Qiao-Long Huang,Michael Monagan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 提出一种整系数多项式最大公约数(GCD)新算法，位复杂度对输入输出规模及各个变量次数界均为多项式级；核心是通过新变量替换将多变量GCD归约为单变量GCD，并已在Maple中实现，对高次数、多变量但GCD项数少的多项式高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量多项式GCD的标准做法是归约到单变量GCD，但往往需要多次单变量计算且复杂度对输入/输出规模及各自变量次数界难以保证多项式级。作者希望设计一种位复杂度严格多项式级、且在实际中高效的算法。

Method: 采用标准思路，将多变量多项式GCD归约为单变量多项式GCD；关键创新是一种新的变量替换，把多变量多项式转化为“可分”(separated)形式，即关于主变量的系数均为单项式，从而仅需一次单变量GCD。对显式位复杂度进行理论分析，并在Maple中实现该算法。

Result: 算法位复杂度在输入规模、输出规模及各变量次数界下均为多项式级；Maple实现表明，对于次数高、变量数多但GCD项数少的多项式，算法高效。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了整系数多变量多项式GCD的一个位复杂度多项式级算法，通过变量替换实现单次单变量GCD归约，兼顾理论复杂度保证与稀疏GCD情形下的实际效率。

Abstract: We describe a new greatest common divisor (GCD) algorithm for polynomials with integer coefficients. The bit complexity of the new algorithm is polynomial in the input and output sizes and the individual degree bounds.Our algorithm follows the standard approach by reducing multivariate polynomial GCD to univariate polynomial GCD.
  Our algorithm reduces a multivariate polynomial GCD to a single univariate polynomial GCD. The main idea of our algorithm is a new variable substitution which reduces a multivariate polynomial to a separated one, that is, the coefficients in a main variable are all monomials. The explicit bit complexity is analyzed and we have implemented our algorithm in Maple. It is shown that our algorithm is efficient for polynomials with high degree, large number of variables, but small number of terms in GCD.

</details>


### [101] [Sums of distinct divisors of factorials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10902)
*Scott D. Hughes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明阶乘 $n!$ 的“最小不同因子表示数” $h(n!)$ 满足 $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$，显著改进了此前 $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ 级别的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究整数表示为某整数 $N$ 的不同因子之和所需的最少因子个数 $h(N)$。此前 Tenenbaum-Yokota 引理4与 Yokota 1995 年背包笔记仅给出 $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ 量级的上界，作者希望借助更精确的阶乘因子间隙估计获得更优、且具显式常数的最佳上界。

Method: 将 Tenenbaum-Yokota 的递减贪心构造与 Berend-Harmse 更精确的阶乘因子间隙估计相结合；对小于和大于 $\sqrt{n!}$ 的部分分别计数步数，并对上段通过倒数因子（互反因子）处理，从而在因子间隙指数中保留主项系数。

Result: 证明了 $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$，得到显式常数 $2\log2$，并将上界量级从 $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ 改进到 $n/\log n$。

Conclusion: 通过结合递减贪心构造与更精细的因子间隙估计，并分段计数、利用倒数因子处理上段，可对阶乘的不同因子加法表示数给出带显式常数的主项上界。

Abstract: For practical $N$ let $h(N)$ be the least $k$ such that every integer $1\le m\le N$ is a sum of at most $k$ distinct divisors of $N$. We prove $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$. This improves the bounds of order $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ established in Tenenbaum-Yokota's Lemma 4 and Yokota's 1995 knapsack note. We combine their decreasing greedy construction with the sharper factorial divisor-gap estimate of Berend-Harmse. Counting the steps separately below and above $\sqrt{n!}$, with the upper range handled through reciprocal divisors, retains the leading coefficient in the gap exponent and yields the explicit constant $2\log2$.

</details>


### [102] [Ordinary 3-Isogeny Graphs and Improvement of Supersingularity Testing for Twisted Hessian Curves over Prime Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11037)
*Yuji Hashimoto,Koji Nuida*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 通过分析扭转Hessian曲线间3-同源性质，证明在p≡2 (mod 3)时F_p上的普通扭转Hessian曲线均位于3-火山表面，并据此改进了超奇异判定算法，同时推广了超奇异j-不变量为立方数的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓ-同源图与ℓ-火山结构是Sutherland超奇异判定算法的核心。作者希望针对扭转Hessian曲线这一特殊曲线模型，利用3-同源的特性来优化该算法，并深入理解j-不变量与火山结构之间的关系。

Method: 利用扭转Hessian曲线之间3-同源的性质进行分析，考察p≡2 (mod 3)、ℓ=3时F_p上普通扭转Hessian曲线在3-火山中的位置；并研究扭转Hessian曲线H(a,d)的j-不变量是否为F_{p^2}中的立方数与其在火山中位置的关系。

Result: 证明当p≡2 (mod 3)且ℓ=3时，F_p上每条普通扭转Hessian曲线都位于3-火山的表面；给出了针对F_p（p≡2 mod 3）上扭转Hessian曲线的改进版Sutherland超奇异判定算法；并证明对F_{p^2}上的扭转Hessian曲线H(a,d)，其j-不变量不是F_{p^2}中的立方数当且仅当H(a,d)是普通的且位于3-火山的底部（floor）。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了扭转Hessian曲线在3-火山结构中的特殊位置规律，既提升了超奇异判定算法的效率，又将"超奇异j-不变量为立方数"这一已知结论推广到更一般的情形。

Abstract: For any primes $p \neq \ell$, $\ell$-isogeny graphs of ordinary elliptic curves defined over $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ have a typical structure called $\ell$-volcanoes, and the structure is the core of Sutherland's supersingularity testing algorithm for elliptic curves. In this paper, by exploiting the properties of $3$-isogenies between twisted Hessian curves, we show that when $p \equiv 2 \pmod{3}$ and $\ell = 3$, every ordinary twisted Hessian curve defined over $\mathbb{F}_p$ lies on the surface of the $3$-volcano. As an application, we give an improved version of Sutherland's supersingularity testing algorithm specialized to twisted Hessian curves defined over $\mathbb{F}_p$ with $p \equiv 2 \pmod{3}$. We also give a generalization of the known fact that any supersingular $j$-invariant is a cube in $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$; we show that for any twisted Hessian curve $H(a,d)$ defined over $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$, its $j$-invariant is not a cube in $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ if and only if $H(a,d)$ is ordinary and lies on the floor of a $3$-volcano.

</details>


### [103] [Effective estimates for exponential sums with multiplicative coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11070)
*Nicolas Robles*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文改进了乘法函数在素点附近指数和估计的第二项误差，去掉了 Bachman 结果中的 √log R 因子，同时保留 Montgomery–Vaughan 的系数假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Montgomery 与 Vaughan 证明了乘法函数指数和的误差项为 NR^{-1/2}(log R)^{3/2}；Bachman 对 1-有界函数改进为 NR^{-1/2}√(log R log log R)。本文旨在进一步去掉 √log R 因子，并在更一般的系数假设下获得更精确的估计。

Method: 结合短区间上的 Brun–Titchmarsh 不等式与由 Carleson–Hunt 定理导出的极大 Fourier 估计；利用局部界允许任意依赖于素数的前缀，并给出记录与有理数距离的更精确估计。

Result: 在 |f(p)|≤A、∑_{n≤x}|f(n)|^2≤A^2x 的条件下，若 |α-a/q|≤q^{-2}，(a,q)=1，且 3≤R≤q≤N/R，则 ∑_{n≤N} f(n)e(nα) ≪_A N/log N + (N/√R)√(log log(3R))，隐含常数有效。该结果去掉了 Bachman 第二项中的 √log R 因子，并证明了平方根位移依赖的尖锐性。

Conclusion: 论文在 Montgomery–Vaughan 的系数假设下改进了乘法函数指数和的误差估计，去掉了 √log R 因子，并证明了平方根位移依赖的尖锐性。

Abstract: Let $f$ be multiplicative, with $|f(p)|\le A$ at primes and $\sum_{n\le x}|f(n)|^2\le A^2x$ for every $x\ge1$. If $|α-a/q|\le q^{-2}$, $(a,q)=1$, and $3\le R\le q\le N/R$, we prove \[
  \sum_{n\le N}f(n)\operatorname{e}(nα)
  \ll_A \frac{N}{\log N}
  +\frac{N}{\sqrt R}\sqrt{\log\log(3R)} \] with effective implied constants. Montgomery and Vaughan proved this with second term $NR^{-1/2}(\log R)^{3/2}$, and, for $1$-bounded functions, Bachman replaced it by $NR^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log R\log\log R}$. We remove the factor $\sqrt{\log R}$ from Bachman's second term while retaining the original coefficient hypotheses of Montgomery and Vaughan. A more precise estimate records the distance from a rational number. The proof combines the Brun-Titchmarsh inequality on short intervals with maximal Fourier estimates derived from the Carleson-Hunt theorem; the local bounds permit arbitrary prime-dependent prefixes. We also prove sharpness of the square-root displacement dependence.

</details>


### [104] [Rational Approximations for Reciprocals of Multiple Zeta Values and Trivariate Cauchy Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11072)
*Ce Xu,Jianqiang Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将经典一阶Cauchy数（Gregory系数、Nörlund数）通过多重对数函数倒数的Laurent展开推广到三变量情形，研究了高阶Gregory系数的消失性、交替性与最终正性，并给出zeta值与双重zeta值倒数的新恒等式族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Gregory系数均为非零且最终交替，作者希望借助多重对数函数倒数的Laurent展开，将Cauchy数（Gregory系数与Nörlund数）推广到三变量、高阶情形，并探讨这些新序列是否保留经典性质。

Method: 利用多重对数函数（及其任意正整数次幂"order"）倒数的Laurent展开定义三变量Cauchy数推广；对对数情形用WZ方法证明性质；对多重对数与双重对数特殊情形验证猜想。

Result: 在对数情形，用WZ方法证明对每个阶ℓ>1，某高阶Gregory系数必为零（与经典全非零形成对比）；证明高阶对数情形序列对每个固定阶最终交替，保留经典性质。一般情形下，新序列最终正的猜想获数值支持，并在多重对数与双重对数特殊情形得到证实。副产品：对每个zeta值与双重zeta值，找到无穷族恒等式，将其倒数表示为有理数与反常积分之和。

Conclusion: 通过多重对数倒数Laurent展开成功将Cauchy数推广为三变量高阶形式，揭示高阶Gregory系数存在零点及序列最终交替性，提出并部分证实最终正性猜想，同时导出zeta值倒数的新恒等式族。

Abstract: In this paper, we will study a trivariate extension of the Cauchy numbers of both the first kind (also called Gregory coefficients) and the second kind (also called Nörlund numbers) via the Laurent expansion of the reciprocal of any positive integer power (which is called the order) of multiple polylogarithms. In the case of logarithm, we will show by the WZ method that for each order $\ell>1$ some Gregory coefficient of order $\ell$ must vanish, in contrast to the fact that all classical Gregory coefficients are nonzero. We also prove in this higher order logarithm case that the sequence is eventually alternating for each fixed order, a property enjoyed by the classical Gregory coefficients. In the most general setting, we conjecture that these new sequences are all eventually positive, which is supported by strong numerical evidence. Finally, we confirm this conjecture in the special case of polylogarithms and double polylogarithms. As a by product, for each zeta value and double zeta value, we find an infinite family of identities expressing its reciprocal as a sum of a rational number and an improper integral.

</details>


### [105] [A $p$-adic monodromy theorem for curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11106)
*Hansheng Diao,Yong Suk Moon,Zijian Yao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了p进光滑射影曲线上任意de Rham p进局部系统在有限覆叠拉回后变为半稳定，即具有潜在半稳定性，并建立了圆盘与圆环上de Rham p进局部系统的p进单值化定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的p进单值化定理（Berger、André–Kedlaya–Mebkhout）刻画了p进局部系统的半稳定性，但仅限于局部（如圆盘/圆环）情形。本文旨在将其推广到相对情形，即在p进域上的光滑射影曲线这一整体几何框架下研究de Rham局部系统的潜在半稳定性。

Method: 通过建立圆盘与圆环上de Rham p进局部系统的p进单值化定理作为局部工具，再结合对光滑射影曲线上的局部系统沿有限覆叠拉回的技术，将局部结果推广到整体曲线情形。

Result: 证明了p进域上光滑射影曲线上的每个de Rham p进局部系统都是潜在半稳定的，即沿曲线的某个有限覆叠拉回后变为半稳定；同时得到圆盘和圆环上de Rham p进局部系统的p进单值化定理。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了经典p进单值化定理的相对（整体）版本，说明de Rham p进局部系统在曲线上普遍具有潜在半稳定性，并将局部圆盘/圆环上的单值化结论纳入统一框架。

Abstract: We prove that every de Rham $p$-adic local system on a smooth projective curve over a $p$-adic field is potentially semistable; that is, it becomes semistable after pulling back along a finite cover of the curve. This establishes a relative version of the classical $p$-adic monodromy theorem of Berger and André--Kedlaya--Mebkhout. Along the way, we also establish a $p$-adic monodromy theorem for de Rham $p$-adic local systems on disks and annuli.

</details>


### [106] [Moments and Non-Vanishing of Maass Form Symmetric Square L-Functions in Short Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11119)
*Olga Balkanova,Dmitry Frolenkov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了Maass形式对称平方L-函数中心值三次矩的均值Lindelöf估计的短区间长度，从H≥T^{18/19+ε}改进到H≫T^{6/7+ε}，并给出短区间内中心L-值非消失比例的无条件下界(7β-2)/8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Li先前得到Maass形式对称平方L-函数中心值三次矩在短区间(T-H,T+H)（长度H≥T^{18/19+ε}）上的均值Lindelöf估计。本文旨在改进该区间长度条件，并进一步改进短区间内中心L-值非消失比例的下界，此前即使在Dirichlet L-函数Lindelöf假设下，长度H=T^β区间内的非消失比例下界仅为(3β-1)/4。

Method: 证明的关键要素是一个新的Maass对称平方L-函数第二扭转矩的渐近公式。基于该公式，作者推导出改进的均值Lindelöf估计及非消失比例下界。

Result: 均值Lindelöf估计对H≫T^{6/7+ε}成立；并建立短区间内中心L-值非消失比例的无条件下界(7β-2)/8。

Conclusion: 本文显著放宽了三次矩均值Lindelöf估计所需的短区间长度条件，并通过新的第二扭转矩渐近公式，给出了优于以往（即便在Lindelöf假设下）的非消失比例无条件下界。

Abstract: Recently, Li obtained a mean Lindelöf estimate for the cubic moment of the central values of Maass form symmetric square $L$-functions over short intervals $(T-H, T+H)$ of length $H \ge T^{18/19+ε}$. We improve this result by showing that the estimate holds for $H \gg T^{6/7+ε}$. The key ingredient in our proof is a new asymptotic formula for the second twisted moment of Maass symmetric square $L$-functions. Based on this formula, we also improve the lower bound for the proportion of non-vanishing central $L$-values in short intervals. Previously, even under the assumption of the Lindelöf hypothesis for Dirichlet $L$-functions, the proportion of non-vanishing values in intervals of length $H = T^β$ was only known to be at least $\frac{3β-1}{4}$. We establish an unconditional lower bound of $\frac{7β-2}{8}$.

</details>


### [107] [Log-crystalline representations and $(\varphi, Γ)$-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11266)
*Abhinandan,Denis Benois*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了绝对非分歧混合特征局部域上小仿射代数的对数étale基本群的p进表示，建立了其与étale (φ, Γ_R)-模的分类对应，并定义了对数晶体表示，证明其等价于R上的Wach模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典（非对数/光滑情形）的p进表示理论，特别是晶体表示与Wach模的对应，推广到Fujiwara--Kato意义下的对数设置中，以处理带有"水平"对数结构的几何对象。

Method: 设F为绝对非分歧混合特征局部域，O_F为其整数环。对O_F上的小仿射代数R，取其一般纤维赋予"水平"对数结构后的对数étale基本群G_R，研究其p进表示，并用étale (φ, Γ_R)-模进行分类；同时定义并研究G_R的对数晶体表示。

Result: 给出了G_R的p进表示基于étale (φ, Γ_R)-模的分类；定义了对数晶体表示这一概念；证明了对数晶体表示与R上的Wach模等价。

Conclusion: 该工作将对数结构融入p进Hodge理论，把先前非对数晶体情形下的结果推广至对数情形，建立了对数晶体表示与Wach模的等价性。

Abstract: Let $F$ be an absolutely unramified mixed characteristic local field with rings of integers $O_F$. For a small affine algebra $R$ over $O_F$, we consider the logarithmic étale fundamental group $G_R$ of its generic fibre equipped with a "horizontal" log-structure, in the sense of Fujiwara--Kato. We study $p$-adic representations of $G_R$ and obtain a classification of these representations in terms of étale $(\varphi, Γ_R)$-modules. Moreover, we define and study the notion of log-crystalline representations of $G_R$, a generalisation of crystalline representations from the non-logarithmic/smooth case. Furthermore, in the logarithmic setting, we show that log-crystalline representations of $G_R$ are equivalent to Wach modules for $R$, extending our previous results from the (non-logarithmic) crystalline case.

</details>


### [108] [The irrationality measure of π is at most 7.101862832357](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11276)
*Yufei Bai*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Zeilberger–Zudilin积分中引入两个独立分子指数并取特定值，构造整数线性形式，将π的无理性测度上界降至μ(π)<7.101862832357，较原上界下降约0.01890%，并证明该参数点是辅助上界函数的严格二维局部极小点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进π的无理性测度上界是超越数论中的经典问题。Zeilberger–Zudilin积分给出已知较好的上界7.103205334137…，本文试图通过增加参数自由度来降低该上界，并分析参数点在最优化意义上的性质。

Method: 在Zeilberger–Zudilin积分中引入两个独立的分子指数（参数A₁, A₂），并特化取A₁=A₂=1857/2785；由此构造出关于1和π的整数线性形式，用于估计π的无理性测度。同时推导显式辅助上界函数，并分析该参数点在其容许算术腔室内的局部极小性质。

Result: 所得整数线性形式证明μ(π)<7.101862832357，较Zeilberger–Zudilin的上界7.103205334137…降低超过0.001342501780；未取整上界之差为0.0013425017806509…，约0.01890%。该参数点是显式辅助上界函数在其容许算术腔室内的严格二维局部极小点。

Conclusion: 通过引入双参数并选取特定值，成功改进了π的无理性测度上界。该参数点仅为该辅助函数在容许算术腔室内的局部最优，并非所有构造中的全局最优；改进幅度虽小但明确。

Abstract: We introduce two independent numerator exponents into the Zeilberger--Zudilin integral and specialize them to \[
  A_1=A_2=\frac{1857}{2785}. \] The resulting integer linear forms in $1$ and $π$ prove \[
  μ(π)<7.101862832357. \] This lowers the Zeilberger--Zudilin upper bound $7.103205334137\ldots$ by more than $0.001342501780$; the difference between the unrounded bounds is $0.0013425017806509\ldots$, approximately $0.01890\%$. The same parameter point is a strict two-dimensional local minimizer of the explicit auxiliary upper-bound function in its admissible arithmetic chamber. This is a local statement about that function, not a claim that the point is a global optimizer among all constructions.

</details>


### [109] [On the bounded-conductor finiteness conjecture in equal characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11456)
*Yufan Luo,Yiqi Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究模 $p$ 伽罗瓦表示在等特征全局函数域上的 Moon–Taguchi 有界导子有限性猜想：在存在处处非分歧特征零提升时证明有限性，并在去掉该假设时构造无穷多反例，从而完全解决二维情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Moon–Taguchi 有界导子有限性猜想考察全局域上模 $p$ 伽罗瓦表示在固定维数、导子有界条件下的同构类有限性。此前工作主要针对交叉特征（表示特征与域特征不同）情形，等特征（域特征等于 $p$）情形尚不清楚，本文旨在研究该等特征情形，并在二维情形下完整回答 Moon–Taguchi 提出的问题。

Method: 设 $K$ 为特征 $p$ 的全局函数域，考察连续、半单、处处非分歧、几何的表示 $\rho: G_K \to \mathrm{GL}_n(\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p)$。首先在额外假设"存在处处非分歧的特征零提升"下，证明这类表示只有有限多个同构类。随后通过构造无穷多个特征 $p$ 的全局函数域 $K$，对每个整数 $r \ge 4$ 给出连续、满射、绝对不可约、处处非分歧、几何的表示 $\rho_r: G_K \twoheadrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$，以说明去掉提升假设后猜想一般不成立。

Result: （1）条件性有限性定理：对任意特征 $p$ 的全局函数域 $K$ 和任意 $n \ge 1$，存在有限多个同构类的连续、半单、处处非分歧、几何表示 $\rho: G_K \to \mathrm{GL}_n(\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p)$，且这些表示均可处处非分歧地提升到特征零。（2）反例：构造出无穷多个特征 $p$ 的全局函数域 $K$，对每个 $r \ge 4$ 有满射的绝对不可约处处非分歧几何表示 $\rho_r: G_K \twoheadrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$，故猜想在无提升假设下一般不成立。（3）推论：得到处处非分歧的伽罗瓦扩张 $L/K$，在 $\mathbf{F}_p$ 上正则，且 $\mathrm{Gal}(L/K)\cong \prod_{r\ge4}\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$。

Conclusion: 结合已知的交叉特征有限性定理，本文完全解决了 Moon–Taguchi 在二维情形下提出的问题：在特征 $p$ 的全局函数域上存在此类处处非分歧扩张，而在特征不等于 $p$ 的全局函数域上此类扩张不存在。这表明等特征与交叉特征情形在二维情形下呈现本质差异。

Abstract: We investigate the equal-characteristic case of the Moon--Taguchi bounded-conductor finiteness conjecture for mod $p$ Galois representations over global function fields. We first establish a conditional finiteness theorem: for any global function field $K$ of characteristic $p$ and any integer $n \ge 1$, there are only finitely many isomorphism classes of continuous, semisimple, everywhere unramified, and geometric representations $ρ: G_K \to \mathrm{GL}_n(\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p)$ that admit an everywhere unramified characteristic-zero lift. Furthermore, we prove that the conjecture fails in general without this lifting hypothesis. Concretely, we construct infinitely many global function fields $K$ of characteristic $p$ admitting a continuous, surjective, absolutely irreducible, everywhere unramified, and geometric representation $ρ_r: G_K \twoheadrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$ for each integer $r \ge 4$. As a corollary, we construct an everywhere unramified Galois extension \(L/K\), regular over \(\mathbf F_p\), with Galois group
  \[
  \mathrm{Gal}(L/K)\cong
  \prod_{r\geq4}\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbf F_{p^r}).
  \]
  Combined with known cross-characteristic finiteness theorems, this completely resolves the question posed by Moon and Taguchi in dimension two: such extensions exist over global function fields of characteristic \(p\), whereas they cannot exist over global function fields of characteristic different from \(p\).

</details>


### [110] [Sharp unconditional moment bounds for products of L-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11619)
*Markus Valås Hagen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究临界线上不可约 GL(1) 与 GL(2) L-函数的乘积混合矩，建立了尖锐的矩界，从而在若干范围内确定了混合矩的阶；并首次无条件确定了负判别式三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩的量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负判别式三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩的阶长期未被无条件确定，混合矩是解决该问题的关键途径。

Method: 下界证明引入计算混合矩的新方法，将 Heath-Brown 的经典方法与 Harper、Heap、Radziwill、Soundararajan 的现代机器相结合。

Result: 在临界线上建立了 GL(1) 与 GL(2) L-函数乘积混合矩的尖锐矩界，并在一定范围内确定了这些混合矩的阶。

Conclusion: 首次无条件确定了负判别式三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩的量级，并给出了计算混合矩的新方法。

Abstract: We investigate the mixed moments of products of irreducible $\mathrm{GL}(1)$ and $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ $L$-functions on the critical line, and establish sharp moment bounds for these quantities. In particular this establishes the order of these mixed moments in certain ranges. This determines unconditionally, for the first time, the order of magnitude of the first moment of cubic Dedekind zeta functions with negative discriminant. The proof of the lower bound introduces a new method for computing mixed moments, that is a mix of the classical method of Heath--Brown with the modern machinery of Harper, Heap, Radziwill, and Soundararajan.

</details>


### [111] [Internal congruences modulo powers of $2$ for overpartition tuples with odd parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11806)
*Manjil P. Saikia,Prabal Talukdar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文证明了奇数元 overpartition 的 2-adic 同余与奇平方数处的精确赋值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 overpartition（超分拆）取奇部数目的同余性质，尤其是模 2 的幂次下序列在倍增下标时的行为。

Method: 用初等且对 m 一致的方法：构造一族满足三项递推的整数多项式，控制所有 U-算子恒等式，并通过整除性每步产生一个 2 的因子。

Result: 对奇数 m≥1 与 i≥3，生成函数模 2^{i+2} 同余于 2^{i+1} 乘以奇平方数的生成函数；从而得到 2 的幂次同余，且 2-adic 赋值相等当且仅当下标为奇平方数。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了统一、初等的 overpartition 同余框架，凸显三项递推多项式与整除性在 U-算子证明中的核心作用。

Abstract: Let $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(n)$ denote the number of overpartition $m$-tuples of $n$ into odd parts. We prove that for every odd $m\ge1$ and every $i\ge3$, \[\sum_{n\ge0}\Bigl(\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m\bigl(2^in\bigr)-\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m\bigl(2^{i-1}n\bigr)\Bigr)q^n \equiv 2^{\,i+1}\sum_{k\ge0}q^{(2k+1)^2} \pmod{2^{\,i+2}} .\] Thus $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^in)\equiv \overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n)\pmod{2^{i+1}}$, with equality of $2$-adic valuations exactly at the odd squares. The proof is elementary and uniform in $m$: a single family of integer polynomials, given by a three-term recurrence, governs every $U$-operator identity involved, and a divisibility statement supplies one power of $2$ per iteration.

</details>


### [112] [Generalized Frobenius Partitions Modulo Powers of $2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11813)
