*Manjil P. Saikia*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了k-着色广义Frobenius划分数生成函数的2^m模同余，并用于完全确定cφ_18(2n+1) mod 16 与 cφ_10(2n+1) mod 8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究k-着色广义Frobenius划分数cφ_k(n)的模2幂同余，推广Chan-Wang-Yang的m=2情形，并解决Das-Nath-Sarma提出的猜想。

Method: 通过建立生成函数的模2^m同余关系：对任意m≥2、k≡2 (mod 2^m)，将∑cφ_k(n)q^n表示为θ函数与(q^2;q^2)_∞/(q;q)_∞^2之积乘以∑cφ_{k/2}(n)q^{2n}模2^m；再取k=18、k=10具体计算。

Result: 对任意m≥2、k≡2 (mod 2^m)给出上述生成函数模2^m同余，m=2时回收Chan-Wang-Yang结果；k=18时完全确定cφ_18(2n+1) mod 16，证明∑cφ_18(6n+1)q^n≡4∑q^{r(3r-1)/2} (mod 16)，从而证实cφ_18(30n+19)≡cφ_18(30n+25)≡0 (mod 16)的猜想；k=10时确定cφ_10(2n+1) mod 8。

Conclusion: 该同余框架统一并推广了已有结果，给出更多模16同余及模8刻画，验证了Das-Nath-Sarma的猜想。

Abstract: Let $cφ_k(n)$ denote the number of $k$-colored generalized Frobenius partitions of $n$. We prove that, for every $m\geq2$ and every $k\equiv2\pmod{2^m}$, \[
  \sum_{n\geq0}cφ_k(n)q^n\equiv\frac{\varphi(q)\,(q^2;q^2)_\infty}{(q;q)_\infty^2}\sum_{n\geq0}cφ_{k/2}(n)q^{2n}\pmod{2^m}, \] where $\varphi(q)$ is the classical theta function. For $m=2$ this recovers a congruence of Chan, Wang, and Yang. Applied with $k=18$, we determine $cφ_{18}(2n+1)$ modulo $16$ completely. In particular, we also prove \[
  \sum_{n\geq0}cφ_{18}(6n+1)q^n
  \equiv4\sum_{r\in\mathbb{Z}}q^{r(3r-1)/2}\pmod{16}, \] which proves the congruences $cφ_{18}(30n+19)\equiv cφ_{18}(30n+25)\equiv0\pmod{16}$ recently conjectured by Das, Nath, and Sarma (2026). It also yields further congruences modulo $16$ and a simple modulo-$8$ characterization that recovers and extends a recent congruence of those authors. As a second application we set $k=10$ and determine $cφ_{10}(2n+1)$ modulo $8$.

</details>


### [113] [Bounded asymptotic bases for linear forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11848)
*Christian Táfula*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究带系数线性型的加性基问题，证明除(1,1)外所有二元系数向量均存在有限重表示基，并推广至含p进结构的多元族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典加性数论考察用集合A表示整数（如Goldbach、Waring问题），此处关注带系数b_i的线性型表示，并额外要求表示数有界（bounded number of representations）。系数含p进层级（p^d倍数）时结构特殊，需明确哪些系数向量允许这样的"有界表示基"存在。

Method: 构造性方法：对给定系数向量b，构造集合A使每个充分大整数n有界次表示为b_1x_1+...+b_hx_h（x_i∈A）。核心利用带系数线性型的加性组合与p进（同余）结构处理，特别对(b_1,b_2)≠(1,1)及b=(u_1, p^d u_2,...,p^{(h-1)d}u_h)（p∤u_1...u_h）给出A的显式构造。

Result: 对每个二元向量b≠(1,1)，存在满足条件的集合A；并证明对一般高维族b=(u_1, p^d u_2, ..., p^{(h-1)d} u_h)（p不整除各u_i）同样存在这样的A。即有限重表示基在广泛系数范围内存在。

Conclusion: 刻画了带系数线性型中"有界表示基"的存在性：二元情形仅(1,1)为例外，高维p进等比型族亦成立。该结果推广了经典加性基理论，表明系数结构（尤其p进层级）在决定有界重表示集存在性中起关键作用。

Abstract: For a vector of positive integers $\mathbf{b} = (b_1,\ldots,b_h)$ with $\gcd(b_1,\ldots,b_h) = 1$, we study sets $A \subseteq \mathbb{N}$ for which every sufficiently large integer has a bounded positive number of representations \[ n = b_1 x_1 + \cdots + b_h x_h \qquad (x_1,\ldots,x_h\in A). \] We prove that such a set exists for every binary vector $\mathbf{b} \neq (1,1)$, and for some general higher-dimensional families, including $\mathbf{b} = (u_1, p^d u_2, \ldots, p^{(h-1)d} u_h)$ where $p\nmid u_1\cdots u_h$.

</details>


### [114] [Chowla's non-vanishing conjecture over $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11855)
*Peter Koymans,Carlo Pagano,Mark Shusterman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明在函数域上，虚二次Dirichlet特征的L函数中心值几乎必然非零，比例为100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨L函数中心值非零比例问题（类似Chowla猜想/Soundararajan的100%非零结果），将经典数域结果推广到函数域F_q(T)上的虚二次Dirichlet特征。

Method: 在 q ≡ 1 mod 8 的素幂条件下，对 F_q(T) 的虚二次Dirichlet特征，运用解析数论/函数域L函数的一阶矩与二阶矩方法（如 mollification 或随机模型）确定中心值非零的密度。

Result: 证明对所有此类虚二次Dirichlet特征，100%（按适当密度意义）满足 L(1/2, χ) ≠ 0。

Conclusion: 在函数域设定下，L函数中心值非零比例达到100%，支持了广义的Chowla猜想类结论，并给出条件 q ≡ 1 mod 8 下的完整非零性。

Abstract: Let $q \equiv 1 \bmod 8$ be a prime power. We prove that $L\left(\tfrac{1}{2}, χ\right) \neq 0$ for 100% of the imaginary quadratic Dirichlet characters of $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$.

</details>


### [115] [The asymptotic in Waring's problem over function fields beyond twice the degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11888)
*Matthew Hase-Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在 \(\mathbb F_q[T]\) 上的 Waring 问题中，当 \(n>2d\)、特征大于 \((d-1)^2\) 且 \(q\) 满足显式下界时，证明了带幂次节省误差的预期渐近公式；该范围对目标多项式一致情形一般是尖锐的。核心技术是聚合小弧估计，通过奇异轨迹余维分类并用交理论控制参数空间次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究函数域 \(\mathbb F_q[T]\) 上 Waring 问题的渐近公式，目标是在较优的变量数、特征与 \(q\) 条件下得到带幂次节省误差的预期渐近，并刻画其适用范围。

Method: 主创新为聚合小弧估计：按关联奇异轨迹的余维对泛函进行分类，并利用交理论给出相应参数空间的次数上界；同时给出 \(q\) 的显式下界，当 \(n\ge(2+\varepsilon)d\) 时该下界为关于 \(d\) 的多项式，次数为 \(2+4/\varepsilon\)。

Result: 在 \(n>2d\)、特征大于 \((d-1)^2\) 且 \(q\) 足够大（显式下界）时，得到 \(\mathbb F_q[T]\) 上 Waring 问题的预期渐近并具幂次节省误差；并指出该范围对目标多项式一致情形一般是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 论文在函数域 Waring 问题中给出了接近最优条件范围的渐近结果，并通过聚合小弧与交理论方法有效控制了奇异轨迹带来的误差；同时给出了 \(n\ge(2+\varepsilon)d\) 时 \(q\) 下界的多项式依赖形式。

Abstract: We prove the expected asymptotic in Waring's problem over $\mathbb F_q[T]$, with a power-saving error, whenever $n>2d$, the characteristic is greater than $(d-1)^2$, and $q$ satisfies an explicit lower bound. This range is sharp in general for the expected asymptotic uniformly in the target polynomial.
  Our main new input is an aggregate minor arc estimate: we count functionals according to the codimension of their associated singular loci and use intersection theory to bound the degrees of the resulting parameter spaces. In particular, if $n\ge (2+\varepsilon)d$, the required lower bound on $q$ is polynomial in $d$ of degree $2+4/\varepsilon$.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [EVTradeMatch: A Mobility-Aware Multi-Objective Matching Framework for EV--EV Energy Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10551)
*Md. Mahfujur Rahman,Alistair Barros,Raja Jurdak,Darshika Koggalahewa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 EVTradeMatch，一个预测引导的多目标优化框架，用于考虑移动性的电动汽车（EV）间能量交易，通过将充电节点适宜性预测分数融入多目标混合整数线性规划，并用定制 NSGA-II 求 Pareto 近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限充电基础设施下，EV 间点对点能量交易可提升充电灵活性，但有效交易需在行程特定条件下协调供需匹配；既有方法（如接近度、拍卖式）难以兼顾覆盖、能量、移动成本与节点适宜性。

Method: 基于前期 EVNextTrade 学习排序模型，将充电节点适宜性定义为预测派生分数；将其作为引导信号与显式优化目标而非硬性选择规则。将 EV--EV 匹配建模为多目标混合整数线性规划，最大化匹配覆盖、转移能量与节点适宜性，最小化移动成本，满足时空、一对一匹配与节点容量约束；设计定制 NSGA-II 求解广域动态场景的 Pareto 高效解。

Result: 相比接近度与拍卖式 SOTA 方法，转移能量提升 74.2--82.8%，节点适宜性提升 8.3--84.4%，匹配覆盖提升 3.74--25.07 个百分点；均衡 NSGA-II 解达 53.07±0.76% 覆盖、转移 1701.94±17.03 kWh，移动成本更高作为对行程最小化方法的显式权衡；Pareto 前沿支持按运营优先级灵活选择高覆盖、高能量、低移动成本或高适宜性方案。

Conclusion: 预测引导的多目标优化能有效协调移动性感知的 EV--EV 能量交易，Pareto 前沿提供多目标权衡的灵活决策支持。

Abstract: Peer-to-peer energy trading among electric vehicles (EVs) can improve charging flexibility under limited charging infrastructure, but effective EV--EV trading requires coordinated provider--consumer matching under journey-specific conditions. This paper proposes EVTradeMatch, a prediction-guided multi-objective optimization framework for mobility-aware EV--EV energy trading. Building on the EVNextTrade study, a prior learning-to-rank model for charging-node recommendation, we define charging-node suitability as a prediction-derived score reflecting the appropriateness of assigning a provider--consumer pair to a candidate charging node based on mobility, energy, and contextual trading features. This score is used as a guidance signal and as an explicit optimization objective rather than as a hard selection rule. The EV--EV matching problem is formulated as a multi-objective mixed-integer linear program that maximizes matching coverage, transferred energy, and charging-node suitability while minimizing mobility cost under spatial, temporal, one-to-one matching, and charging-node capacity constraints. To approximate Pareto-efficient solutions in wide-area dynamic settings, we develop a tailored non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (NSGA-II). Experimental results show that EVTradeMatch improves transferred energy by 74.2--82.8% and charging-node suitability by 8.3--84.4% compared with proximity- and auction-based state-of-the-art methods, while improving matching coverage by 3.74--25.07 percentage points. Balanced NSGA-II solutions achieve 53.07$\pm$0.76% matching coverage and transfer 1701.94$\pm$17.03 kWh, with higher mobility cost as an explicit trade-off against travel-minimizing methods. Pareto-front analysis shows that the framework supports flexible selection among high-coverage, high-energy, low-mobility-cost, and high-suitability solutions according to operational priorities.

</details>


### [117] [Disturbance rejection for classes of nonlinear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10553)
*Saverio Messineo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对两类非线性系统的非自适应全局鲁棒扰动抑制问题，基于高增益范式与滑模范式设计控制架构，分别实现闭环输入-状态稳定性（C1）与全局一致有界性（C2），并使吸引子可任意缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作中经典的动态扩展方法在存在未知强迫扰动时不再可行，且需要处理零动态状态不可测、不匹配加性扰动以及输出相关非线性（甚至导数无界）等困难，故需建立非自适应、全局鲁棒的扰动抑制方案。

Method: 对C1（严格反馈形式、零动态线性且Hurwitz、状态不可测、含不匹配加性强迫扰动）采用基于高增益范式的控制架构，用非线性工具证明闭环ISS，并嵌入基于滑模范式的有界控制单元以改善扰动抑制、降低高增益开销。对C2（最小相位、不确定、相对阶大于一、含匹配加性强迫扰动、输出相关非线性可能无界且导数无界）先用开环观测器替代经典动态扩展，再将C1的结果推广至C2，构造输出反馈动态控制器。

Result: C1：闭环对强迫扰动达到输入-状态稳定性，并全局渐近收敛到可任意缩小的吸引子；额外滑模单元保持控制作用一致有界。C2：输出反馈动态控制器实现闭环全局一致有界，并渐近调节到可任意缩小的吸引子。

Conclusion: 所提出的非自适应、全局鲁棒扰动抑制框架统一处理了两类非线性系统，借助高增益与滑模的互补设计在保证稳定性/有界性的同时降低了控制代价，且开环观测器解决了未知强迫扰动下经典动态扩展不可用的问题。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of non-adaptive global robust disturbance rejection for two distinct classes of nonlinear systems. The first class, denoted by C1, consists of nonlinear systems in strict-feedback form, with linear and Hurwitz zero-dynamics (whose states are unavailable for feedback), and enhanced - within this work - by forcing, unmatched, additive disturbances. Nonlinear tools are herein employed to demonstrate that the proposed control architecture - based on the high-gain paradigm - achieves closed-loop input-to-state stability with respect to the forcing disturbances, along with global asymptotic convergence towards an attractor which can be rendered as small as desired. Then, owing to the established input-to-state stability property, a uniformly bounded control action is additionally embedded within the control architecture. The additional unit, designed following the sliding-mode paradigm, is aimed at improving the disturbance rejection task, by potentially lowering the required high-gain control expenditure. The second class of systems, denoted by C2, is constituted by minimum-phase, uncertain, nonlinear systems with relative degree greater than one, featuring possibly unbounded, with possibly unbounded derivatives, output-dependent nonlinearities, with matched additive forcing disturbances. To solve the problem of output-feedback, non-adaptive, global robust disturbance rejection for systems within C2, first, an open-loop observer is employed in lieu of a classic dynamic extension adopted in earlier works, as the latter is no longer implementable due to the presence of unknown forcing disturbances. Subsequently, the results derived for C1 are then adapted to C2, to yield an output-feedback dynamic controller providing closed-loop global uniform boundedness, along with asymptotic regulation towards an attractor which can be rendered as small as desired.

</details>


### [118] [Constructing Pareto Sets Using Noether's Second Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10555)
*Evgeny Nikulchev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于诺特定理，建立多目标变分控制问题中系统规范对称性与Pareto集结构之间的基本联系，提出可降维的"规范正则化"方法，并用算例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标变分控制问题的Pareto集通常维数高、求解困难。作者希望借助物理中的对称性与守恒律工具，揭示Pareto前沿的结构本质并降低问题求解维度。

Method: 以诺特定理（Noether's second theorem）为理论基础，分析描述被控对象的动力系统规范对称性与Pareto集结构的关系，将Pareto前沿解释为系统及质量准则在某对称群下不变性所产生的守恒律；进而提出"规范正则化"概念，在不改变Pareto集的前提下剔除不影响准则的变量。

Result: 证明了Pareto前沿可视为系统不变性与质量准则对称性对应的守恒律；规范正则化能够在不改变Pareto集的情况下降低解空间维数；在具有两个冲突准则的线性二次调节器（LQR）与标准ZDT1测试问题上给出数值算例验证。

Conclusion: 对称性与守恒律为多目标变分控制问题提供了解析构造Pareto集的新框架，规范正则化可有效降维，数值算例支持该方法的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents an analytical approach to constructing the Pareto set in multiobjective variational control problems, based on Noether's second theorem. A fundamental connection is established between the gauge symmetries of the dynamical system describing the plant and the structure of the Pareto set. It is shown that the Pareto front can be interpreted as a conservation law arising from the invariance of the system and the quality criteria with respect to a symmetry group. A gauge regularization concept is proposed, which allows eliminating variables that do not affect the criteria without changing the Pareto set, thereby reducing the dimension of the solution space. Numerical examples are provided for the linear-quadratic regulator with two conflicting criteria and for the standard ZDT1 test problem.

</details>


### [119] [PEJWAK: A Profile-Echoing Self-Anchored Aggregation Architecture for Multi-Criteria Decision Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10562)
*Seyyed Ahmad Edalatpanah*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出PEJWAK——一种"剖面回响式自锚定"聚合架构，用于多准则决策分析；准则贡献由备选方案自身剖面条件化，兼具可分解性与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在直接加权聚合（信息损失、无法反映准则间条件效应）与参数密集型非可加交互模型（参数多、难审计）之间，缺乏一种既保留准则身份、又能体现备选方案剖面条件效应且可审计的聚合机制。

Method: 将每个准则与源自同一备选方案的内生参考几何耦合，在最终聚合前完成锚定，从而保留准则身份并使其贡献受剖面条件化。给出一般化形式，分离锚函数、锚定核、保留指数、贡献系数与外层聚合规则；典型成员采用加权算术自锚、保留指数取准则重要性值、贡献系数由归一化平方根映射导出。另设计确定性MCDA审计流程。

Result: 解析结果证明：连续性、单调性、对角校准、内蕴性、全域联合连续、精确边界行为、不可分性、等权二元限制下双对称性失效，以及精确的对数保留律。审计提供完整准则级分解、精确权重稳定区间与排序跃迁、贡献规则排序相位、集合依赖性诊断及基于排列的精确排序亲和度分析。典型PEJWAK无需epsilon正则化即可处理精确零值：当其余活跃得分可维持正锚时，活跃零仅移除其自身准则贡献。

Conclusion: PEJWAK提供了介于直接加权聚合与参数密集型非可加交互模型之间的、可审计的剖面条件化聚合机制。

Abstract: PEJWAK is introduced as a profile-echoing self-anchored aggregation architecture for multi-criteria decision analysis. Each criterion is coupled geometrically with an endogenous reference derived from the same alternative before final aggregation, so criterion identity is preserved while its expressed contribution is conditioned by the alternative's profile. A general formulation separates the anchor function, anchoring kernel, retention exponents, contribution coefficients, and outer aggregation rule. The canonical member uses a weighted-arithmetic self-anchor, sets each retention exponent equal to the corresponding criterion-importance value, and derives contribution coefficients through a normalized square-root map. Analytical results establish continuity, monotonicity, diagonal calibration, internality, full-domain joint continuity, exact boundary behavior, nonseparability, failure of bisymmetry in the equal-weight binary restriction, and an exact logarithmic retention law. A deterministic MCDA audit provides complete criterion-level decomposition, exact weight-stability intervals and rank transitions, contribution-rule rank phases, set-dependence diagnostics, and exact permutation-based rank-affinity analysis. Canonical PEJWAK handles exact zeros without epsilon regularization: an active zero removes only its own criterion contribution whenever the remaining active scores sustain a positive anchor. The architecture therefore supplies an auditable profile-conditioned aggregation mechanism between direct weighted aggregation and parameter-intensive nonadditive interaction models.

</details>


### [120] [Supply Chain Analytics: A Data-Driven Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10563)
*Elioth Sanabria*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何将统计学习与鲁棒决策框架结合，以应对供应链网络中的结构不确定性与运营扰动，涵盖需求预测、库存控制、网络履约及分布鲁棒优化等主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链网络日益依赖实时数据来应对结构不确定性、市场波动和运营中断，但统计型数据驱动学习与鲁棒决策框架之间存在脱节，亟需在物流与运营管理中弥合这一差距。

Method: 以数学严谨的方式系统论述供应链分析：从经验需求预测出发，采用样本最小化、处理时变库存补货的动态规划递推、网络履约框架，以及基于传输理论的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）来规避罕见事件风险；同时引入列生成求解车辆路径问题与非齐次排队机制等规范性控制算法。

Result: 提出了将预测性统计模型与规范性控制算法相整合的理论框架，为设计具有韧性的数据驱动自动化系统提供了基础工具。

Conclusion: 该文兼具理论蓝图与算法指南的双重作用，可供在机器学习、数学优化与应用概率交叉领域开展研究的学者与从业者参考。

Abstract: Modern supply chain networks increasingly rely on real-time data to navigate structural uncertainties, market volatility, and operational disruptions. This manuscript bridges the gap between statistical data-driven learning and robust decision-making frameworks in logistics and operations management. We present a comprehensive, mathematically rigorous treatment of supply chain analytics, moving from empirical demand forecasting to optimal inventory and network control under uncertainty. Key topics explored include sample minimization, dynamic programming recursions for time-varying inventory replenishment, network fulfillment frameworks, and advanced distributionally robust optimization (DRO) via transport theory to hedge against rare events. By integrating predictive statistical models with prescriptive control algorithms, such as column generation for vehicle routing and non-homogeneous queueing regimes, this text provides the foundational tools necessary for designing resilient, data-driven automated systems. It serves as both a theoretical blueprint and an algorithmic guide for researchers and practitioners operating at the intersection of machine learning, mathematical optimization, and applied probability.

</details>


### [121] [Operator Splitting Methods with Online Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10732)
*Wanyu Zhang,Wenzhi Gao,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出自动调优算子分裂法预条件子的框架，将其解释为包络函数上的预条件梯度下降，用在线学习自适应学习预条件子，实现渐近更快收敛并加速Lasso、$\ell_1$逻辑回归和二次规划求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子分裂方法（前向-后向、Douglas-Rachford、ADMM）中的预条件子通常需人工设定，调参困难且影响收敛速度，缺乏自动、有原则的调优机制。

Method: 将分裂方法解释为作用于相应包络函数的预条件梯度下降，并利用在线学习在优化过程中自适应学习预条件子。

Result: 获得渐近更快的收敛速率，并加速求解Lasso、$\ell_1$正则化逻辑回归和二次规划等重要优化问题；大量数值实验验证了理论结果和方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为算子分裂法的预条件子自动调优提供了统一且有理论保障的原则性方法，兼具更优收敛性与实际加速效果。

Abstract: This paper develops a principled framework for automatically tuning preconditioners in operator splitting methods, including forward-backward splitting, Douglas-Rachford splitting, and the alternating direction method of multipliers. We interpret these splitting methods as preconditioned gradient descent applied to their corresponding envelope functions and use online learning to adaptively learn the preconditioner during optimization. Our framework achieves asymptotically faster convergence rates and accelerates the solution of important optimization problems, including Lasso, $\ell_1$-regularized logistic regression, and quadratic programming. Extensive numerical experiments validate our theoretical results and demonstrate the practical effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [122] [Method for Constrained Computational Guidance and Control with Application to Hypersonic Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10813)
*Emily M. Palmer,Anil V. Rao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约束计算制导与控制方法，通过在最优控制问题中引入基于光滑极大值近似的惩罚项，防止扰动系统违反约束，提升可行性，并在可重复使用运载器最大横向航程再入问题上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性地重新求解约束最优控制问题以实现制导更新时，扰动系统可能导致约束违反，从而降低最优控制问题的可行性，因此需要一种能在扰动下保持约束满足与问题可行性的方法。

Method: 方法通过周期性重解约束最优控制问题实现制导更新；为提高可行性，在最优控制问题中增加惩罚项，当扰动系统接近约束极限时增大约束与其极限之间的裕度；该惩罚项采用光滑极大值近似来实现。

Result: 在可重复使用运载器（RLV）最大横向航程再入问题上进行验证，该问题的参考解包含长时段的约束弧；结果表明该方法对于路径约束下的最优制导与控制是可行的。

Conclusion: 所提出的约束计算制导与控制方法能够有效避免扰动系统中的约束违反，在含长时段约束弧的RLV再入问题中验证可行，适用于路径约束下的最优制导与控制。

Abstract: A method for constrained computation guidance and control is described. The method employs guidance updates by periodically re-solving a constrained optimal control problem. Feasibility of the optimal control problem is improved by preventing constraint violations in the perturbed system. Constraint violation is prevented by augmenting the optimal control problem with a penalty term intended to increase the margin between a constraint and its limit when the perturbed system is within certain proximity of the constraint limit. The additional penalty term employs a smooth maximum approximation to increase the margin between the constraint and its limit. The method developed in this paper is demonstrated on the maximum cross-range reusable launch vehicle (RLV) entry problem, for which the reference solution contains a long-duration constrained arc. The results demonstrate that the method developed in this paper is viable for path-constrained optimal guidance and control.

</details>


### [123] [A Simple Complexity Lower Bound for Solving $Ax=b$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10874)
*Ohad Shamir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出了一个简短直接的证明：在无维度限制、条件数为κ的一般矩阵A上，将Ax=b近似求解到相对误差ε，最坏情况下需要Ω(κ log(1/ε))次矩阵-向量乘法，即使允许随机算法；并由此推出强凸二次函数优化的经典Ω(√κ log(1/ε))下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dereziński、Epperly和Meyer [2026]用更优雅且更通用的方法给出了该设定下的下界，受其启发，作者希望寻求一种更简短、更直接的证明。

Method: 通过直接的论证证明近似求解Ax=b到相对误差ε所需的矩阵-向量乘法下界，并利用一个简单归约将其连接到强凸二次函数优化的下界。

Result: 证明了最坏情况下需要Ω(κ log(1/ε))次矩阵-向量乘法，即使对随机算法也成立；该结果基本重现了Dereziński等人的下界。归约推出强凸二次函数优化的经典Ω(√κ log(1/ε))下界，且适用于随机算法。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个简短直接的方法重新导出已知的复杂度下界，验证了该下界的鲁棒性（对随机算法成立），并展示了与二次优化下界之间的简洁联系。

Abstract: In this note, we provide a short and direct proof that approximately solving $Ax=b$ to relative error $\varepsilon$, where $A$ has condition number $κ$ and unrestricted dimension, requires $Ω(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ matrix-vector multiplications in the worst case, even for randomized algorithms. This essentially recovers the lower bound of Dereziński, Epperly and Meyer [2026] for this setting, whose elegant and more general approach inspired us to seek a short direct proof. A straightforward reduction implies the classical $Ω(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ lower bound for optimizing strongly convex quadratic functions, applicable to randomized algorithms.

</details>


### [124] [The Polarity Process for the Maximum-Volume Inscribed Ellipsoid Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10888)
*Kaizhao Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于极性（polarity）几何迭代研究多面体最大体积内接椭球（MaxIE）问题，通过将log-volume作为势函数建立全局线性收缩收敛分析，给出有限体积比迭代界，并分析非精确极性过程及条件算术复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khachiyan和Todd在1993年提出的极性迭代方法此前仅有渐近收敛论证，缺乏体积比收敛速率。本文旨在补足这一理论缺口，给出显式的收敛速率与迭代界，并处理子问题近似求解的实际情形。

Method: 给定内点，构造平移后的极多面体，求解其最小体积覆盖椭球（MinCE），再极性化该覆盖椭球得到新的内接椭球。以极性MinCE的log-volume作为势函数，证明其凸性并给出显式梯度公式，建立沿轨迹的势能间隙全局线性收缩。进一步分析非精确极性过程，推出产生指定体积近似的充分oracle容差。结合path-following Newton法、重心坐标下降和away-step Frank-Wolfe给出条件算术估计。

Result: 证明了势函数凸性与显式梯度，建立全局线性收缩，收缩因子存在且依赖实例。得到独立的收敛证明与有限的体积比迭代界；推导出非精确MinCE求解的充分容差条件；给出基于三种MinCE算法的条件算术复杂度估计；数值实验比较不同oracle选择与两个MaxIE基线。

Conclusion: 极性迭代方法可获得独立于既有渐近论证的线性收敛保证及有限迭代界，且可推广到非精确子问题求解。性能取决于MinCE求解器与实例几何的匹配，需针对实例选择合适算法。

Abstract: We study the maximum-volume inscribed ellipsoid (MaxIE) problem for a polytope through a geometric iteration based on polarity. Given an interior point, the method forms the shifted polar polytope, computes its minimum-volume covering ellipsoid (MinCE), and then polarizes this covering ellipsoid back to obtain a new inscribed ellipsoid. This procedure was suggested by Khachiyan and Todd in 1993. Prior work studied basic polarity identities and gave an asymptotic-convergence argument for the exact process, but did not furnish a volume-ratio rate. Independently of that asymptotic argument, we develop a new analysis based on the log-volume of the polar MinCE as a potential function. We prove its convexity with an explicit gradient formula and establish global linear contraction of the potential gap along each trajectory. The contraction factor is existential and instance-dependent. This gives both a separate convergence proof and a finite volume-ratio iteration bound. We further analyze an inexact polarity process in which each MinCE subproblem is solved only approximately, and derive sufficient oracle tolerances for producing a prescribed volume approximation. Combining this outer analysis with existing algorithms for MinCE gives conditional arithmetic estimates based on the path-following Newton method, the barycentric coordinate descent, and the away-step Frank-Wolfe method. Numerical experiments compare these oracle choices with two MaxIE baselines and illustrate that performance depends on matching the MinCE solver to the instance geometry.

</details>


### [125] [Deterministic and Random Bipartite Matching on General Networks: Convex Flow Reformulation, Asymptotic Properties, and Fast Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10938)
*Yuhui Zhai,Yanfeng Ouyang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一般网络上的最小距离二分匹配问题，提出精确的边分离凸流重构及基于平滑单调重排与一阶阻力近似的快速估计方法，并刻画随机供需分布下最优匹配距离的标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般网络上的最小距离二分匹配应用广泛，但精确求解计算代价高；同时随机供需分布下最优匹配的统计性质与距离标度规律尚缺乏理论刻画与快速算法。

Method: 针对确定性问题，给出精确的边分离凸流重构（edgewise-separable convex-flow reformulation）；引入边级不平衡剖面的平滑单调重排近似以高效求解；进一步做一阶阻力近似，解析导出闭式的一步拉普拉斯估计量。针对随机问题，分析供需随机分布下期望最优匹配距离的标度，并基于极限阻力网络设计快速近似算法。

Result: 当供需分布相同，期望最优匹配距离随点数平方根标度；否则呈线性标度。前者下最优流被证明为中心对称且次高斯；后者下极限阻力网络刻画供需失衡的重分配方式，并启发基于极限阻力的快速近似算法。数值实验表明所提估计量能较好逼近精确匹配代价且显著降低计算时间，随机匹配的理论性质经大规模蒙特卡洛仿真验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般网络上的最小距离二分匹配提供了可高效求解的凸重构与闭式估计量，并揭示了随机匹配的标度规律与统计性质，兼顾理论保证与计算效率。

Abstract: Minimum-distance bipartite matching on general networks has numerous applications various fields. This paper first focuses on deterministic problems and presents an exact edgewise-separable convex-flow reformulation. By introducing a smooth monotone rearrangement approximation of the edge-wise imbalance profiles, the convex-flow reformulation's can be solved efficiently. If we further conduct a first-order resistance-based approximation of the convex program, a one-step Laplacian-based estimator can be analytically derived in closed forms. The paper also studies random problems where supply and demand points are randomly distributed. We show that the expected optimal matching distance scales with the square root of the number of points if the supply/demand point distributions are identical, or linearly otherwise. In the former case, the optimal flow is proven to be centered, symmetric, and sub-Gaussian. In the latter case, the limiting resistance network characterizes how supply-demand imbalance is redistributed and motivates a fast algorithm that approximate the optimal flow based on the limiting resistance. Numerical experiments show that the proposed estimators closely approximate the exact matching cost while substantially reducing computation time. The proven theoretical properties of the random matching solution are numerically verified by large-scale Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [126] [Conformal-DRO: Distributionally Robust Optimization with Conformalized Ambiguity Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11073)
*Luhao Zhang,Shixiang Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Conformal-DRO，利用嵌套共形区域为未来潜在分布构造模糊集，在仅有一次观测的潜在异质场景下实现有限样本、无需估计潜在分布的分布鲁棒优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动 DRO 假设条件结果分布固定，仅用模糊集刻画估计误差，无法处理"每个实例有未观测的潜在分布、却只贡献一个观测"的潜在分布异质性，此时即使已知混合分布，潜在不确定性依然存在。

Method: 以嵌套共形预测区域构造未来潜在分布的模糊集；在可交换性假设下，该集合以不低于 1-α 的概率覆盖真实潜在分布，且无需估计潜在分布及其混合机制。共形路径诱导出数据驱动的传输几何，α 决定模糊集半径；最坏情况问题化为共形壳层上的有限线性规划，并具有稀疏对抗解。

Result: 模糊集在有限样本下以至少 1-α 的概率覆盖未来潜在分布；最坏情况问题可化为有限线性规划并具稀疏对抗解；所得鲁棒值给出所选决策期望代价的有限样本证书。

Conclusion: Conformal-DRO 将共形预测与 DRO 结合，在潜在分布异质且单观测设定下提供无需估计潜在分布的有限样本鲁棒保证，并具备可计算性。

Abstract: Data-driven distributionally robust optimization (DRO) typically treats the conditional outcome law as fixed and uses ambiguity sets to capture estimation error. This paper studies latent distributional heterogeneity, where each instance has an unobserved law but contributes only one observation, so uncertainty persists even if the mixture law is known. We propose Conformal-DRO, which uses nested conformal regions to construct an ambiguity set for the future latent law. Under exchangeability, the set covers this law with probability at least $1-α$ in finite samples, without estimating underlying latent laws or their mixing mechanism. The conformal path induces a data-driven transport geometry, while $α$ determines the radius. The worst-case problem reduces to a finite linear program over conformal shells and admits sparse adversarial solutions. The resulting robust value provides a finite-sample certificate for the selected decision's expected cost.

</details>


### [127] [The best approximation tuple: an extension of the Cheney-Goldstein algorithm and results to the multiple sets case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11105)
*Yair Censor,Daniel Reem*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将Cheney-Goldstein关于两集合最佳逼近对(BAP)的算法推广到多集合及集合为有限族交的情形，提出最佳逼近元组(BAT)问题，并证明在欧氏空间中卫星集为严格凸紧集时算法收敛到唯一解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Cheney-Goldstein(1959)工作仅处理两个集合的最佳逼近对问题，且每个集合为单一集合。本文希望将其推广到多于两个集合，并能处理每个集合为有限族集合之交的情形，从而突破循环与一般泛函极小化元相关的理论障碍。

Method: 将某一集合视为"枢轴集"，其余集合视为"卫星集"，而非寻求作为目标泛函极小化元的循环；算法被推广以同时处理多集合与集合交结构。收敛分析不依赖Fejér单调性，而是利用正交投影关于非扩张性定义中等式与不等式的若干不常用性质。

Result: 证明了算法在欧氏情形下收敛到问题的唯一解，其中卫星集为严格凸且紧的集合。

Conclusion: 通过区分枢轴集与卫星集并借助正交投影的特殊性质，成功将最佳逼近对问题推广为最佳逼近元组问题并给出收敛性保证，尽管缺乏Fejér单调性使分析非标准。

Abstract: In this paper we extend the algorithm and several results published in the celebrated 1959 paper of Cheney and Goldstein about the best approximation pair (BAP) problem in two separate directions. One is the consideration of more than two sets. The other is the ability to handle each set as an intersections of a finite family of sets. We call the resulting problem the "Best Approximation Tuple (BAT) problem". The fundamental observation that leads to this generalizations is to recognize and handle one set (the "pivot set") as different from the remaining sets (the "satellite sets") instead of seeking cycles as the minimizers of a target functional. This enable us to overcome a certain theoretical obstacle related to cycles and minimizers of general functionals. We prove the convergence of the algorithm to the unique solution of the problem in the Euclidean case with strictly convex and compact satellite sets. Because of the lack of Fejér monotonicity, our convergence analysis is not standard, and is based on almost unknown properties of orthogonal projections regarding equality and inequality in the definition of nonexpansiveness.

</details>


### [128] [Cooperative Integer Programming Games: Core Stability and Optimal Coalition Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11116)
*Hyunwoo Lee,Robert Hildebrand,I. Esra Buyuktahtakin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出合作整数规划博弈（CIPGs），研究最优联盟结构（OCS）与稳定联盟结构（OSCS），给出MIP建模、稳定割与求解算法，并在合作背包博弈上验证至30个玩家的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的多个智能体常需共享预算以共同完成不可分任务，其合作收益由整数规划的联合最优值决定，而现有联盟博弈模型难以刻画这种整数决策与预算汇聚结构。因此需要刻画其联盟特征函数，并同时求解最优与稳定的联盟结构。

Method: 提出合作整数规划博弈（CIPGs）：特征函数将每个联盟映射为联合整数规划的最优值。推导稳定性不等式，使每个形成联盟相对自身位于Core中；给出聚合与 disaggregated 两种混合整数OCS公式，证明后者整数等价且LP松弛更紧；基于稳定性不等式设计提升稳定割、割平面算法中的多种分离策略、可保证稳定性的SCS可行原始启发式（构造warm start），并加入收益精化步骤计算各联盟的Shapley值与核仁。

Result: 在基准合作背包博弈上，方法可在16名玩家时证明最优性；在30名玩家时MIP gap低于1%，且仅需评估约10^9个联盟值中的766个。

Conclusion: CIPGs与所提割平面及启发式框架能高效求解最优稳定联盟结构，显著降低联盟值评估量，并在较大规模合作背包博弈中保持近优精度。

Abstract: We introduce cooperative integer programming games (CIPGs), in which agents pool budget constraints to accomplish indivisible tasks jointly and the characteristic function maps every coalition to the optimal value of a pooled integer program. Our goal is to identify an optimal coalition structure (OCS) and a stable one (OSCS). We derive a stability inequality that keeps each formed coalition in the Core with respect to itself, and present two mixed-integer OCS formulations, aggregated and disaggregated, proving that the disaggregated formulation is integer-equivalent yet yields a tighter LP relaxation. Building on the stability inequality we develop lifted stability cuts, several separation strategies inside a cutting-plane algorithm, an SCS-feasible primal heuristic that constructs warm starts with guaranteed stability, and a payoff-refinement step computing the Shapley value and the nucleolus of every formed coalition. On benchmark cooperative knapsack games, the method certifies optimality with up to 16 players and reaches MIP gaps below 1% at 30 players while evaluating 766 of the roughly $10^9$ coalition values.

</details>


### [129] [Minimal null control time of some 1D linear hyperbolic balance laws with constant coefficients and properties of related kernel equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11118)
*Long Hu,Guillaume Olive*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究一维常系数双曲守恒律系统单侧边界控制的零可控性，给出最小零控制时间并刻画其与正交性条件数量的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究仅有单一负速度或正速度的常系数双曲守恒律系统，在单侧边界控制下，其最小零控制时间由何种条件决定，并希望得到显式的判别方法。

Method: 基于对系统相关核方程（kernel equations）的细致分析，并建立一个新的适定性（well-posedness）结果，从而推导最小零控制时间与正交性条件之间的关系。

Result: 证明当系统仅有一个负或正速度时，最小零控制时间依赖于某特定序列的正交性条件，该序列由系统系数显式表达但由非线性递推关系定义；并给出两种非平凡情形下所需检验的正交性条件数量的显式上界。

Conclusion: 所得结果刻画了双曲守恒律系统单侧边界控制的最小零控制时间，并可推广至有限时间镇定（finite-time stabilization）性质。

Abstract: In this work, we study the null controllability by one-sided boundary controls of one-dimensional hyperbolic balance laws with constant coefficients. Our first result shows that, when the system has only one negative or positive speed, the minimal null control time of such systems depends on some orthogonality conditions for a particular sequence. This sequence is explicit in function of the coefficients of the system but it is defined by a nonlinear recurrence relation. Our second result then completes the previous one by giving explicit bounds on the number of orthogonality conditions that have to be checked in two nontrivial situations. The proofs rely on a careful analysis of the so-called kernel equations associated with the system, including a new well-posedness result. Our results are also valid for the finite-time stabilization property.

</details>


### [130] [A sequential smoothing majorant stochastic approximation method for nonconvex nonconcave minimax problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11171)
*Zhouxing Luo,Wei Liu,Xin Liu,Xiaojun Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出序贯平滑优majorant随机逼近（SMSA）方法求解非凸-非凹极小极大问题，借助新的majorant随机逼近将内层最大化精度界从O(β_N)提升至O(β_N^2)，并给出全局最优值与极小点集的非渐近界、Clarke稳定点的相合性及有界性证明，数值实验验证了更优的逼近保真度与更低的稳健测试损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸-非凹极小极大优化中内层最大化问题非凹，标准随机逼近的近似精度界仅为O(β_N)，逼近误差较大，难以获得可靠的稳定点与最优性保证，亟需更精确的逼近机制。

Method: 提出SMSA方法：引入新的majorant随机逼近（精度界O(β_N^2)，β_N为样本覆盖半径），结合序贯平滑与majorant构造以应对内层非凹性；在β_N↓0几乎必然条件下分析全局最优值、极小点集及Clarke稳定点的相合性，并证明序列有界。

Result: 精度界由O(β_N)提升至O(β_N^2)；建立了全局最优值与极小点集的非渐近界，证明Clarke稳定点相合、生成序列有界，且返回点为majorant随机逼近模型的近似Clarke稳定点；在合成算例与两个UCI数据集上的稳健逻辑回归实验中，逼近保真度更高、平均稳健测试损失更低。

Conclusion: SMSA通过更精确的majorant随机逼近有效缓解内层非凹带来的困难，在理论与实验上均优于标准采样逼近，为非凸-非凹极小极大优化提供了可靠的求解与稳定点保证。

Abstract: We propose a sequential smoothing majorant stochastic approximation (SMSA) method for nonconvex-nonconcave minimax optimization problems. To overcome the nonconcavity of the inner maximization problem, we introduce a new majorant stochastic approximation with an $O\left(β_N^2\right)$ accuracy bound, where $β_N$ is the sample coverage radius. The accuracy bound significantly improves upon the $O\left(β_N\right)$ approximation bound for the standard stochastic approximation. Moreover, we establish nonasymptotic bounds for both global optimal values and minimizer sets, and prove consistency for the Clarke stationary points, as $β_N \downarrow 0$ almost surely. We show that the generated sequence by the SMSA method is bounded, and that the returned point is an approximate Clarke stationary point of the majorant stochastic approximation models. Numerical experiments on a synthetic toy example and robust logistic regression on two UCI datasets demonstrate improved approximation fidelity and lower mean robust test losses relative to the standard sampled approximation.

</details>


### [131] [Single-Loop Gradient Algorithms for Pessimistic Bilevel Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11183)
*Qichao Cao,Bo Zeng,Shangzhi Zeng,Jin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文针对研究较少的悲观双层优化（PBO），提出基于平滑近似的全一阶、单循环梯度方法，并给出收敛性保证与实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乐观双层优化已有大量进展，但悲观双层优化（PBO）研究不足，尤其缺乏全一阶、单循环的梯度类方法。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 通过重表述、惩罚与正则化构造PBO的平滑近似，并在极小点与稳定性两方面建立收敛保证；基于该框架分别设计确定性PBO和随机PBO的单循环算法，仅使用一阶梯度信息，避免二阶导数和内层子问题求解。

Result: 为所提算法建立了非渐近收敛速率，提供理论保证；在合成与实际问题上进行系统实证研究，证明算法高效有效。在垃圾邮件分类和Smart Predict-then-Optimize任务上，PBO优于经典乐观双层对应方法。

Conclusion: PBO在实践建模中具有较强潜力，可提供更稳健的解，值得进一步关注与应用。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization has recently attracted growing attention, particularly in the development of efficient numerical methods. Despite substantial progress on optimistic bilevel optimization, pessimistic bilevel optimization (PBO) remains much less explored, especially the design of fully first-order, single-loop gradient-based methods. To address this gap, we propose a smooth approximation of PBO through reformulation, penalization and regularization, and establish convergence guarantees in terms of both minimizers and stationarity. Building on this framework, we then develop two single-loop algorithms for deterministic and stochastic PBOs, respectively. Both use only first-order gradient information and avoid second-order derivatives and inner-loop subproblem solves. Non-asymptotic convergence rates for the proposed algorithms are established to provide theoretical guarantees. Through a systematic empirical study of both synthetic and practical problem instances, we demonstrate that our algorithms are highly effective and efficient. In particular, our results on spam classification and Smart Predict-then-Optimize further illustrate PBO's advantages over its classical optimistic bilevel counterpart, highlighting its strong potential for practical modeling and the delivery of robust solutions.

</details>


### [132] [Optimal Control for Cancer Chemotherapy Using Hybrid Quantum Particle Swarm Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11197)
*Bereket Sitotaw Kidane,Md Samiul Haque Motayed,Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于正则化的混合量子粒子群优化（QPSO）方法，用于求解肿瘤异质性与突变背景下的癌症化疗最优控制问题，并通过哈密顿函数的一阶、二阶最优性检验验证解的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症化疗最优控制受肿瘤异质性与突变影响，治疗难度大。传统方法如庞特里亚金最大值原理（PMP）依赖协态方程的初始猜测，导致精度与收敛性受限，因此需要一种不依赖良好初值、兼顾全局搜索与最优性条件的求解方法。

Method: 采用量子粒子群优化（QPSO）进行全局探索，近似最优控制轨迹；随后进行基于正则化的精修，以保证轨迹的平滑性及与最优性条件的一致性；并利用哈密顿函数进行一阶与二阶最优性检验。

Result: 数值案例研究考察了多种药效函数，展示了周期性给药与局部给药在实现稳健肿瘤抑制中的作用，并揭示了不同药物组合对最优化疗策略的影响。

Conclusion: 基于正则化的混合QPSO方法可有效克服PMP对初值敏感的问题，为异质性肿瘤化疗最优控制提供兼顾全局寻优与最优性验证的求解框架。

Abstract: Optimal control in cancer chemotherapy is challenged by tumor heterogeneity and mutations, which complicate treatment effectiveness. Traditional methods, such as Pontryagin's maximum principle (PMP), are often hindered by their reliance on an initial guess for the costate equation, affecting accuracy and convergence. To address these limitations, this work introduces a hybrid quantum particle swarm optimization (QPSO) method based on regularization. QPSO is employed for global exploration to approximate the optimal control trajectory, followed by a regularization-based refinement to ensure smoothness and consistency with optimality conditions. The Hamiltonian function is used for first- and second-order optimality checks, verifying solution quality. Numerical case studies explore various drug effectiveness functions, demonstrating the role of periodic and localized drug delivery in achieving robust tumor suppression and providing insights into the impact of different drug combinations on optimal chemotherapy strategies.

</details>


### [133] [Optimal chemo-immunotherapy scheduling: A hybrid QPSO-SQP approach with Michaelis-Menten pharmacodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11213)
*Bereket Sitotaw Kidane,Md Samiul Haque Motayed,Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文将Michaelis-Menten饱和药效直接嵌入化疗-免疫联合治疗的控制通道，使动力学非控制仿射、Hamiltonian对控制非线性，在正暴露惩罚下导出显式三区间逐点极小化律，并用QPSO-SQP混合算法求解该非凸转写问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的联合化疗-免疫治疗最优调度常被建模为控制仿射最优控制问题，其结果一般是边界选择（bang-bang型）方案，除非出现奇异弧。这种结构虽然便于求解，却忽略了高剂量率下的饱和药效动力学，导致所导出的方案与实际给药行为不符。

Method: 将Michaelis-Menten饱和直接纳入治疗通道，使动力学非控制仿射、Hamiltonian对每个控制均非线性。在严格正暴露惩罚下，Hamiltonian具有显式的三区间逐点极小化律：每个输入取下界、上界或唯一内点极小值。在内点区间上严格Legendre条件成立，因此连续调制给药作为正则内点极值出现，而非奇异弧或平滑伪迹。由于所得转写问题非凸，采用QPSO-SQP混合流程：QPSO提供约束感知的暖启动，SQP在配点网格上保证可行性与KKT最优性；并通过共态重构验证PMP结构及预测的边界/内点区间切换。

Result: Hamiltonian的显式三区间逐点极小化律成立；内点区间满足严格Legendre条件，连续调制给药对应正则内点极值而非奇异弧或平滑伪迹；共态重构结果与PMP结构以及所预测的边界/内点区间切换一致；QPSO-SQP混合算法在该非凸转写问题上实现了可行性与KKT最优性。

Conclusion: 在治疗通道中引入Michaelis-Menten饱和可突破控制仿射框架的边界选择局限，使连续调制给药由正则内点极值自然产生；三区间极小化律与严格Legendre条件的结合为刻画边界/内点区间切换提供了理论依据；QPSO-SQP混合流程为求解相应非凸最优控制问题提供了可行且满足KKT最优性的数值途径。

Abstract: Optimal scheduling of combined chemo-immunotherapy is often formulated as a control-affine optimal control problem, which generically yields boundary-selected (bang-bang-type) protocols unless singular arcs occur. This structure, while convenient, neglects saturating pharmacodynamics at high dose rates. We incorporate Michaelis-Menten saturation directly into the therapy channels, making the dynamics non-control-affine and the Hamiltonian nonlinear in each control. With strictly positive exposure penalties, the Hamiltonian admits an explicit three-regime pointwise minimization law: each input is chosen at the lower bound, the upper bound, or as a unique interior minimizer. On interior intervals the strict Legendre condition holds, so continuously modulated dosing arises as a regular interior extremal rather than a singular-arc or smoothing artifact. The resulting transcription is nonconvex; we therefore use a hybrid Quantum Particle Swarm Optimization (QPSO)-Sequential Quadratic Programming (SQP) pipeline, where QPSO provides a constraint-aware warm start and SQP enforces feasibility and Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) optimality on a collocation grid. Costate reconstruction corroborates the Pontryagin Minimum Principle (PMP) structure and the predicted boundary/interior regime transitions.

</details>


### [134] [Sequential Extremal Principle: Refinements and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11232)
*Nguyen Duy Cuong,Alexander Kruger,Nguyen Hieu Thao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究序列极值性与平稳性性质，重点考察对应于固定平移序列的情形，给出精确的定量刻画，并建立对偶必要条件与序列扩展极值原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极值原理与平稳性条件多以精确（非序列）形式给出，但其在扰动下不一定稳定。本文旨在引入序列版本的极值性与平稳性，提供定量刻画并考察其稳定性，从而处理最小值未必取到的约束极小化问题。

Method: 以固定平移序列为切入点，对序列极值性与平稳性（含近似与非近似）给出精确的定量刻画；借助广义分离条件 $(GS)$、$(GS_\alpha)$ 以及原始-对偶互补条件 $(PD)$，推导固定与非固定平移序列下序列极值性与平稳性的对偶必要条件，并建立序列扩展极值原理的一个版本。

Result: 证明序列（及传统）极值性与近似平稳性具有某种稳定性，而非近似平稳性不具备；在固定与非固定平移序列下均建立了序列极值性与平稳性的对偶必要条件；给出了序列扩展极值原理；并针对最小值未必取到的约束极小化问题证明了对偶最优性/平稳性条件。

Conclusion: 序列框架下的极值性与近似平稳性比非近似平稳性更稳健，适用于最小值未必达到的约束优化问题；所提出的广义分离与原始-对偶条件为统一处理此类问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Sequential extremality and stationarity properties are discussed with the emphasis on those corresponding to fixed sequences of translations. Exact quantitative characterisations of the properties are provided. We show, in particular, that the sequential (as well as conventional) extremality and approximate stationarity properties possess certain stability, while the (non-approximate) stationarity does not. Dual necessary conditions for the sequential extremality and stationarity properties with fixed and non-fixed sequences of translations are established. A version of the sequential extended extremal principle is formulated. In the statements, we employ certain generalised separation conditions $(GS)$ and $(GS_α)$ as well as a complementary primal-dual condition $(PD)$. To illustrate the model, we prove dual optimality/stationarity conditions for a constrained minimisation problem in which the minimal value is not necessarily attained.

</details>


### [135] [pqSEDMD: Subspace Methods for Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11292)
*Camilo Garcia-Tenorio,Alan Vande Wouwer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文提出pqSEDMD算法，将p-q拟范数EDMD（pqEDMD）与子空间辨识方法相结合，以在过程噪声与测量噪声并存时稳健地辨识非线性系统，并以Duffing振子为基准验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态模式分解（DMD）及其非线性变体扩展DMD（EDMD）能从测量数据中提取（非）线性动力系统的时空特征，但面对真实数据时存在一个关键挑战：真实数据固有过程噪声与测量噪声两种不确定性来源，导致分解类方法辨识性能下降。

Method: 将作者此前提出的pqEDMD（在正交多项式基上进行p-q拟范数降维的EDMD改进算法）与子空间辨识方法相结合，提出p-q拟范数子空间扩展动态模式分解（pqSEDMD）。该方法利用子空间辨识可直接从输入-输出数据获得多变量线性系统精确状态空间模型的鲁棒性，在函数空间中对非线性系统进行线性逼近，融合两类方法的优势。

Result: 以Duffing振子作为基准问题，展示了pqSEDMD算法的有效性，并阐明了与算法开发相关的多个重要方面。

Conclusion: pqSEDMD通过融合pqEDMD的拟范数降维与子空间辨识的噪声鲁棒性，实现了对含噪数据下非线性系统的稳健线性函数空间近似，为处理真实数据中过程与测量噪声的辨识任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The dynamic mode decomposition (DMD), along with its variant for nonlinear systems, the extended DMD (EDMD), are powerful tools for the extraction of meaningful spatio-temporal characteristics of (non)linear dynamical systems from measurement data. Despite some efforts to handle the identification task when dealing with real-world data, the decomposition based methods face a critical challenge: real-world data has two inherent sources of uncertainty, the process and measurement noise. Subspace identification methods, are robust tools able to provide accurate state-space models for multi-variable linear systems directly from input-output data. Combining these two methods, we introduce the p-q quasi-norm Subspace Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (pqSEDMD). An algorithm that uses our previous improvements to the EDMD by the use of a p-q-quasi-norm reduction on an orthogonal polynomial basis, the pqEDMD algorithm, along with subspace identification methods. The result is a robust approximation of nonlinear systems in a linear function space, combining the strengths of the two methodologies. Throughout the paper we will use the Duffing oscillator as a benchmark problem to show the effectiveness of the algorithm and illustrate many important aspects related to the development.

</details>


### [136] [InFlow: entropic stochastic dual dynamic programming with HJB cross-certification for seasonal energy storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11339)
*Steeven B. Affognona,Babacar M. Ndiaye,Pierre Mendy,Cheikh M. F. Kebe*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出验证导向框架InFlow，融合周期性SDDP、熵鲁棒性与控制空间Bellman退火，并以独立离散化的HJB作为基准，验证水库优化调度的可靠性与鲁棒性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性水库调度需在强周期性与入库不确定性下保持可靠，现有优化方法缺乏独立的验证基准与可解释的鲁棒性刻画。

Method: 结合周期性随机对偶动态规划（SDDP）、熵鲁棒性、控制空间Bellman退火，以及独立离散化的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman（HJB）基准；将熵转移算子解释为KL惩罚的最坏情况期望，并通过Lagrange对偶与固定半径KL模糊集联系；证明一致软Bellman逼近界与Gibbs倾斜SDDP割的有效性；基准采用归一化季节性CIR入库过程并显式处理退化的干季边界。

Result: HJB蓄水梯度与HiGHS蓄水平衡对偶强一致，水价值相关性为0.995与0.996；HJB网格从61²细化到81²，年均水价值仅变0.6%、峰值变化小于0.1%；3000条样本外轨迹显示gamma=5使名义均值成本增4.6%、CVaR90降11.2%，入库减40%的干季情景下均值差异不显著而CVaR90改善11.5%；鲁棒性扫描确认风险调整值单调；两级串联水库复现网格oracle边际水价值相关性0.999并恢复上下游价值比2。

Conclusion: InFlow可作为透明的方法学基准，而非标定的实际调度模型，用于验证鲁棒水库优化方法的可靠性与风险-成本权衡。

Abstract: Seasonal reservoir operation requires optimization methods that remain reliable under strongly periodic and uncertain inflow dynamics. We present InFlow, a verification-oriented framework combining periodic stochastic dual dynamic programming (SDDP), entropic robustness, control-space Bellman annealing, and an independently discretized Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) benchmark. The entropic transition operator is interpreted as a KL-penalized worst-case expectation and linked to fixed-radius KL ambiguity sets through Lagrange duality. We establish a uniform soft-Bellman approximation bound and prove the validity of Gibbs-tilted SDDP cuts. The benchmark relies on a normalized seasonal Cox-Ingersoll-Ross inflow process whose positivity condition fails during the driest weeks, requiring explicit treatment of the degenerate boundary. Independent verification shows strong agreement between HJB storage gradients and HiGHS storage-balance duals, with water-value correlations of 0.995 and 0.996 for the standard and exact-cut-only implementations. Refining the HJB grid from 61^2 to 81^2 states changes the annual mean water value by only 0.6% and the peak value by less than 0.1%. Out-of-sample simulations based on 3,000 independent trajectories show that a robustness level of gamma=5 increases nominal mean cost by 4.6% while reducing CVaR90 by 11.2%. Under a dry-season stress scenario with inflows reduced by 40%, the mean-cost difference becomes statistically insignificant whereas CVaR90 improves by 11.5%. A robustness sweep confirms monotonicity of the risk-adjusted value. Finally, a two-reservoir cascade reproduces grid-oracle marginal water values with correlation 0.999 and recovers the predicted upstream-to-downstream value ratio of two. InFlow is intended as a transparent methodological benchmark rather than a calibrated operational reservoir model.

</details>


### [137] [Time-optimal elevator control with higher-order state constraints: analysis and computation of boundary contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11348)
*Dmitry Karamzin,Aleksandra Zhukova,Roman Chertovskih,Pedro A. Aguiar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带高阶状态约束的时间最优电梯问题，提出基于高阶约束系统Pontryagin最大值原理的构造性间接求解框架，将无穷维最优控制问题转化为有限维非线性代数方程组以高效求解，并刻画了边界接触几何及接触颤振现象，最后在四阶电梯模型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间最优电梯问题在存在高阶状态约束时的求解具有挑战性。传统方法难以直接处理无穷维最优控制问题及高阶约束带来的复杂边界行为，因此需要一种构造性的间接求解框架来高效计算候选最优轨迹并理解其结构特性。

Method: 提出基于专为高阶约束系统表述的Pontryagin最大值原理的构造性间接解框架：首先推导专门的最优性条件并分析极值轨迹结构；其次将无穷维最优控制问题转化为含切换时间、边界接触时间与乘子参数的有限维非线性代数方程组；最后刻画边界接触几何并解释接触颤振现象。

Result: 证明了正规极值不能在约束边界上停留正时间，但边界接触序列可无限累积；通过有限维非线性方程组可用标准求解器高效计算候选最优轨迹；在四阶电梯模型上进行了演示，且计算结果通过基于IPOPT的直接优化方法独立验证。

Conclusion: 该间接求解框架为高阶状态约束下的时间最优电梯问题提供了高效且可验证的求解途径，并揭示了高阶约束系统中接触颤振的本质特征。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the classical time-optimal elevator problem in the presence of higher-order state constraints. The main contribution is a constructive indirect solution framework based on a Pontryagin Maximum Principle specifically formulated for higher-order constrained systems. First, we derive specialized optimality conditions and analyze the resulting structure of extremal trajectories. Second, we show that the infinite-dimensional optimal control problem can be transformed into a finite-dimensional system of nonlinear algebraic equations involving switching times, boundary-contact times, and multiplier parameters. This provides a computationally efficient procedure for calculating candidate optimal trajectories using standard nonlinear equation solvers. Third, we characterize the geometry of boundary contacts and explain the emergence of contact-chattering phenomena in higher-order constrained systems. In particular, we show that normal extremals cannot evolve along the constraint boundary for a positive amount of time, although sequences of boundary contacts may accumulate indefinitely. The proposed approach is demonstrated on a fourth-order elevator model, and the computed solutions are independently verified using a direct IPOPT-based optimization method.

</details>


### [138] [Fully Coupled Nonlinear Forward-Backward Stochastic Difference Equations: Spectral Contraction and Infinite-Horizon Maximum Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11354)
*Rui Chen,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出显式谱收缩方法研究无限时域全耦合非线性前向-后向随机差分方程及其在随机控制中的应用。通过将估计组装为二维非负矩阵，利用谱半径给出显式充分折扣阈值，并构造等价加权乘积范数建立收缩性。进而针对带凸控制约束的无限时域控制问题推导Pontryagin型随机最大值原理、点态法锥形式与验证定理，并用递归风险调整投资组合算例说明结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限时域全耦合非线性前向-后向随机差分方程的适定性与收缩性分析通常依赖隐式条件，难以给出可直接检验的折扣阈值；同时其在带凸控制约束的随机控制问题中的应用仍需系统刻画。

Method: 构建显式谱收缩框架：将全耦合系统的估计整理为二维非负矩阵，以其谱半径作为显式充分折扣阈值；构造相应的等价加权乘积范数，证明收缩性。在此基础上，针对无限时域、凸控制约束及累积折扣运行成本的控制问题，推导Pontryagin型随机最大值原理及其等价的点态法锥形式，并给出验证定理。

Result: 得到由二维非负矩阵谱半径刻画的显式充分折扣阈值与等价加权乘积范数下的收缩性；建立Pontryagin型随机最大值原理、点态法锥形式及验证定理；给出递归风险调整投资组合算例，并导出该算例的投影型充分最优性条件。

Conclusion: 显式谱收缩方法为无限时域全耦合非线性前向-后向随机差分方程提供了可检验的折扣阈值与收缩性判据，并有效支撑带凸约束随机控制的Pontryagin型最优性刻画；数值算例展示了理论结果的可用性。

Abstract: This paper develops an explicit spectral-contraction approach to studying fully coupled nonlinear forward--backward stochastic difference equations on infinite horizon and their applications to stochastic control. The estimates for this fully coupled system are assembled into an explicit two-dimensional nonnegative matrix. Its spectral radius yields an explicit sufficient discount threshold, and a corresponding equivalent weighted product norm is constructed to establish the contraction property. Moreover, for an infinite-horizon control problem with convex control constraints and an accumulated discounted running cost, we derive a Pontryagin-type stochastic maximum principle, its equivalent pointwise normal-cone formulation, and a verification theorem. Finally, a recursive risk-adjusted portfolio example is given to demonstrate the applications of our theoretical results, and a projection-type sufficient optimality condition for this example is derived.

</details>


### [139] [Control Strategies for Multi-Species Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11422)
*Dante Kalise,Lucas M. Moschen,Grigorios A. Pavliotis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将单密度的Wasserstein Hessian与Riccati方程谱方法推广到多物种McKean-Vlasov耦合方程乘积Wasserstein空间，给出单一物种驱动下局部指数镇定的充要条件及最小控制输入数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合多物种McKean-Vlasov方程的稳态镇定问题，此前谱方法仅适用于单一密度情形，需推广至物种间耦合的乘积Wasserstein空间，并厘清仅驱动一个物种时的可控性极限。

Method: 基于Wasserstein Hessian与Riccati方程的谱方法，在Wasserstein空间乘积上作推广；针对仅一个物种受控的情形，推导以指定速率实现局部指数镇定的充要条件与最小控制输入数；对双物种情形，将条件化为未驱动物种线性化动力学特征空间上交叉作用核Fourier系数的秩条件。

Result: 得到单一物种驱动下局部指数镇定的充要条件及所需最小控制输入数；双物种时该条件等价于交叉作用核Fourier系数在未驱动物种特征空间上的秩条件；并以两社群含噪Kuramoto模型与细胞分选模型验证。

Conclusion: 谱方法可有效推广至多物种耦合情形，交叉作用核的Fourier秩条件刻画了单物种驱动的可控性边界，为多物种平均场系统的反馈镇定提供了可计算判据。

Abstract: We stabilize stationary states of coupled multi-species McKean--Vlasov equations by feedback control, extending a spectral approach based on the Wasserstein Hessian and a Riccati equation from a single density to a product of Wasserstein spaces. We give necessary and sufficient conditions for local exponential stabilization at a prescribed rate when only one species is actuated, together with the minimum number of control inputs required. For two species, the condition reduces to a rank condition on the Fourier coefficients of the cross-interaction kernel over the eigenspaces of the unactuated species' linearized dynamics. A two-community noisy Kuramoto model and a cell-sorting model illustrate the results.

</details>


### [140] [Asynchronous Replanning in Two Population Linear Quadratic Mean Field Games: Information Requirements and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11424)
*Yuxin Jin,Wang Yao,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究两个种群线性二次平均场博弈中的异步重规划问题，刻画重规划所需信息、可恢复性、局部算法行为及有限种群的误差界，并分析交替响应的稳定性与Zeno积累下的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个种群可能从不同信念出发并采用不同计划，各自只能观测自身聚合轨迹与公开的修正记录，而续期最优响应又依赖对手当前计划，因此需要厘清异步重规划究竟需要哪些信息、这些信息何时可恢复，以及局部算法与有限种群实现的行为。

Method: 将重规划所需信息识别为初始观测区间末端的聚合状态与对手当前有效续期计划这一"状态-计划对"；对线性观测用核包含刻画可恢复性，并用有界分解量化观测误差敏感性；利用公开事件记录与共同最优响应映射递归确定后续对手计划，构造局部算法；对有限种群导出采样误差的事件级线性递推与有限前缀误差界；分离相互续期响应的唯一可解性与交替响应稳定性，并在末期Zeno积累处结合谱稳定性与移动边界估计。

Result: 所需状态-计划对可能在全隐信念不可恢复时仍可恢复；局部算法在每个有限机会前缀上复现理想基准，且由于最优响应持续性，实施的修正是交替进行的；得到有限种群的有限前缀误差界，并在正决策裕度下证明自主死区规则的记录匹配；在末期Zeno积累处，谱稳定性与移动边界估计共同保证续期计划收敛到从实际极限状态重启的均衡。

Conclusion: 异步重规划所需信息弱于完整隐信念，状态-计划对的可恢复性与灵敏度可被精确刻画；局部算法在有限前缀上与理想基准一致且修正自然交替；有限种群误差可控并满足记录匹配；唯一可解性与稳定性可分离，Zeno极限下续期计划收敛至重启均衡。

Abstract: We study asynchronous replanning in a two population linear quadratic mean field game in which the populations may begin from different beliefs and hence use different plans. Each population observes its own aggregate trajectory and a public record of implemented revisions, while its continuation best response depends on the opponent's current plan. We identify the information required for replanning as the aggregate state at the end of the initial observation interval together with the opponent's active continuation plan. For linear observations, recoverability of this state-plan pair is characterized by a kernel inclusion, and a bounded factorization quantifies sensitivity to observation error. In particular, the required pair may be recoverable even when the full hidden belief is not. Once initialized, the public event record and the common best-response map recursively determine subsequent opponent plans, and the resulting local algorithm reproduces an ideal benchmark on every finite opportunity prefix; implemented revisions alternate as a consequence of best-response persistence. For finite populations, we derive an eventwise linear recursion for sampling errors, obtain finite prefix error bounds, and prove record matching for an autonomous deadband rule under a positive decision margin. Finally, we separate unique solvability of mutual continuation responses from stability of alternating responses, and show that at a pre-terminal Zeno accumulation, spectral stability together with a moving-boundary estimate yields convergence of the continuation plans to the equilibrium restarted from the actual limiting state.

</details>


### [141] [Efficient Algorithms for Energy-Aware Single-Machine Scheduling with Battery Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11455)
*Luca Forte,Stijn De Vuyst,Pieter Leyman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究单机环境下，结合分时电价与电池储能系统（BESS）的节能调度问题，以最小化能源成本，并提出两种数学启发式算法协调生产调度与储能充放电决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造业面临降低运营成本与碳排放的双重压力，将生产活动与电价对齐成为关键。BESS可进一步降低购电成本，但其引入使作业调度与储能管理之间产生复杂耦合，显著增加问题求解难度，因此需要高效算法应对。

Method: 在固定时间范围内研究单机调度问题，电价采用分时电价（TOU）机制；考虑可从电网充电、并在高价时段放电的BESS。将生产调度与BESS充放电使用决策进行联合优化，提出并分析两种数学启发式（matheuristic）算法变体以协调两类决策。

Result: 作者提出了两种数学启发式算法变体用于协调生产调度与BESS使用决策；摘要未给出具体数值实验结果或性能对比数据。

Conclusion: 该研究聚焦于分时电价与BESS协同下的单机能耗成本最小化问题，通过数学启发式方法处理调度与储能的复杂耦合，为解决此类节能调度问题提供了算法框架与思路。

Abstract: Energy-aware scheduling has become a central challenge in modern manufacturing environments. As industries increasingly aim to reduce operational costs and carbon emissions, aligning production activities with electricity tariffs is increasingly important. The integration of battery energy storage system (BESS) further enhances the potential to reduce energy purchase costs; however, incorporating such systems introduces complex interdependencies between job scheduling and BESS management decisions, significantly complicating the problem structure.\\In this work, we study the problem of minimizing the energy cost of executing a set of jobs on a single machine within a fixed time horizon, where electricity prices follow a Time-of-Use (TOU) tariff. In addition, we consider the presence of a BESS that can be charged from the grid and discharged during high-price periods to reduce overall energy costs. To efficiently address this problem, we propose and analyze two matheuristic algorithm variants designed to effectively coordinate production scheduling and BESS usage decisions.

</details>


### [142] [Global Linear Convergence of the Proximal Bundle Method under Unknown Piecewise Smoothness and Quadratic Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11480)
*Zhenwei Lin,Zhe Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究近端束方法(PBM)保留更多切割平面为何在实践中表现更优，针对具有二次增长且分段光滑结构未知的凸目标，给出理论解释：保留足够多的切割平面可使PBM利用目标的分段光滑结构，表现得如同优化光滑函数，从而实现线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端束方法在实践中保留更多切割平面时表现更好，但这一现象缺乏理论解释，本文旨在填补该理论空白。

Method: 针对具有二次增长和未知分段光滑结构的凸目标函数，分析PBM在保留足够多切割平面情形下的行为，揭示其可有效利用目标的分段光滑结构。

Result: 保留足够多的切割平面时，PBM能够利用目标的分段光滑结构，表现得如同在优化光滑函数，并据此对观察到的线性收敛现象给出理论解释。

Conclusion: 保留足够多切割平面对PBM至关重要，它使方法能够利用分段光滑结构并获得线性收敛，从理论上解释了实践中保留更多切割平面带来的性能提升。

Abstract: We study why the proximal bundle method (PBM) can perform better in practice when it retains more cutting planes. We consider convex objectives with quadratic growth and an unknown piecewise-smooth structure. Our key observation is that retaining sufficiently many cutting planes allows PBM to exploit the objective's piecewise-smooth structure and behave as if it were optimizing a smooth function. We provide a theoretical explanation for the observed linear convergence of PBM on piecewise-smooth objectives when it retains sufficiently many cutting planes.

</details>


### [143] [Non-monotone direct-search methods for deterministic and stochastic derivative-free optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11567)
*Anjie Ding,Trang H. Tran,Luis Nunes Vicente*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为max-$M$非单调直接搜索法在确定性及随机无导数优化中建立了完整复杂度理论，三类情形的迭代复杂度均为$\mathcal{O}(\epsilon^{-2})$，与单调直接搜索法持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无导数优化中，标准直接搜索法要求试探点按与步长平方成比例的量下降目标函数，这在复杂地形上可能使算法陷入次优解邻域。非单调的max-$M$条件允许目标函数暂时上升，有助于穿越狭窄弯曲山谷，但因缺乏单调下降，其理论分析困难得多，亟需建立相应的复杂度理论。

Method: 针对max-$M$非单调直接搜索（将试探函数值与最近$M$个不同迭代点中最大的目标值比较）展开理论分析。确定性目标下，分别针对基于正生成集的完全poll建立最坏情形迭代界，以及针对概率下降poll建立期望迭代界；随机情形下使用独立函数估计，在随机误差尾界假设下分析随机变体。分析工具为一类新的价值函数（merit function），按有序的步长平方倍数修正存储的目标值，并结合更新报酬（renewal-reward）停时论证处理概率方法。

Result: 三类结果——确定性完全poll的最坏情形界、概率下降poll的期望界、随机估计下的期望迭代复杂度——均达到标准的$\mathcal{O}(\epsilon^{-2})$复杂度，与单调直接搜索方法的迭代复杂度相匹配。

Conclusion: max-$M$非单调直接搜索在保持穿越复杂地形优势的同时，其复杂度不劣于单调方法；所提出的价值函数与停时分析框架为缺乏单调性保证的直接搜索提供了有效的理论工具。

Abstract: In derivative-free optimization (DFO), one minimizes functions for which the gradient is unavailable or expensive to compute. In many applications, objective function values and gradients are noisy due to simulations or system randomness. A class of standard direct-search methods for DFO accept a trial point when it decreases the objective function by an amount proportional to the squared stepsize. However, when applied to complex landscapes, such a requirement may trap the algorithm in a neighborhood of sub-optimal solutions. We study a non-monotone direct-search alternative where the trial function value is compared with the largest objective function obtained through the $M$ most recent distinct iterates. This max-$M$ non-monotone condition permits temporary increases in the objective function and can help navigate narrow curved valleys; however, its theoretical analysis is significantly more challenging due to the lack of monotonic decrease. In this paper, we develop a comprehensive complexity theory for the max-$M$ non-monotone direct-search in both deterministic and stochastic DFO problems. For deterministic objectives, we establish a worst-case iteration bound for a complete poll based on a positive spanning set and an expected iteration bound for a probabilistic-descent poll. We then analyze a stochastic variant using independent function estimates and show the expected iteration complexity under tail-bound assumptions of the stochastic errors. All three results have the standard complexity of $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-2})$, which matches the iteration complexity of monotone direct-search methods. Our theory is enabled by a new family of merit functions that correct the stored objective values by ordered multiples of the squared stepsize, together with a renewal-reward stopping-time argument for the probabilistic methods.

</details>


### [144] [Values of Absorbing Recursive Games Express All Real Algebraic Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11583)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了Oliu-Barton与Vigeral的猜想：任意Q上次数为m≥1的实代数数α，都是某个有理m×m吸收博弈的未折扣值；由此可得有理m×m吸收博弈的未折扣值集合恰为次数不超过m的实代数数集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多两人零和随机博弈类具有orderfield性质：若所有收益与转移概率属于R的某子域，则未折扣值也属于该子域。吸收博弈不具备该性质。Oliu-Barton与Vigeral猜测了该性质失效的精确范围，本文旨在证明这一猜测。

Method: 通过构造性证明，在吸收博弈的一个特殊子类中实现任意给定的实代数数α：所构造的博弈是严格吸收且递归的，即每个行动对都以正概率吸收，且所有非吸收阶段收益为零；当α>0时还可取为正递归形式，具有正吸收收益。

Result: 对任意Q上次数m≥1的实代数数α，存在有理m×m吸收博弈以其为未折扣值，且该博弈属于严格吸收、递归（α>0时为正递归）的特殊子类。作为推论，有理m×m吸收博弈的未折扣值集合恰好等于次数至多为m的实代数数集合。

Conclusion: 证实了Oliu-Barton与Vigeral关于吸收博弈中orderfield性质失效范围的猜想，并给出了未折扣值集合与实代数数次数之间的精确对应关系。

Abstract: Many classes of two-player zero-sum stochastic games have the orderfield property: if all payoffs and transition probabilities lie in a subfield of $\mathbb{R}$, so does the undiscounted value. Absorbing games fail this property, and Oliu-Barton and Vigeral [\emph{Absorbing games with irrational values}, Oper.\ Res.\ Lett.\ 51 (2023) 555--559] conjectured the precise extent of the failure: every real algebraic number $α$ of degree $m\geq 1$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ is the undiscounted value of a rational $m\times m$ absorbing game. We prove this conjecture, and in fact within a special subclass of absorbing games: for every such $α$, the game realizing it is \emph{strictly absorbing} and \emph{recursive}, i.e., every action pair is absorbing with positive probability and all non-absorbing stage payoffs are zero; when $α>0$ it can moreover be taken \emph{positive recursive}, with positive absorbing payoffs. As a corollary, the set of undiscounted values of rational $m\times m$ absorbing games is exactly the set of real algebraic numbers of degree at most $m$

</details>


### [145] [Verified Linear Programming through Tolerance-Aware Precision Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11721)
*Ernesto Casablanca,Martin Sidaway,Paolo Zuliani,Sadegh Soudjani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究单纯形算法的数值稳定性，给出精度提升浮点实现与精确有理实现产生相同枢轴决策和最终基的条件，并提出容差感知的精度提升单纯形算法及delta-完备终止准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性规划在优化、形式验证、SMT求解等领域应用广泛。精确性要求下，浮点运算的数值误差会破坏可靠性，而精确有理运算又带来显著计算开销，因此需要兼顾精度与效率的方案。

Method: 分析单纯形算法的数值稳定性，建立精度提升浮点实现与精确有理实现在枢轴决策及最终基上一致的充分条件；基于该分析设计容差感知、精度提升的单纯形算法，并提供形式化正确性保证；引入delta-完备终止准则，当最优目标值的认证上下界之差不超过用户给定阈值delta时终止。

Result: 表明正确性依赖于算术精度与数值容差的谨慎处理；所提算法在保持形式化正确性保证的同时，可重建并精确认证最终结果；delta-完备终止准则提供受用户控制且可认证的最优性间隙。

Conclusion: 该工作为线性规划在精确性保证与计算效率之间提供了理论依据与算法框架，实现可认证的数值稳定求解。

Abstract: Linear programming plays a fundamental role in computer science, with applications in optimization, formal verification, SMT solving, and numerous other domains. When exactness guarantees are required, numerical inaccuracies arising from floating-point arithmetic can compromise soundness, whereas exact rational arithmetic often incurs significant computational overhead. In this paper, we investigate the numerical stability of the simplex algorithm and establish conditions under which a precision-boosting floating-point implementation provably produces the same pivot decisions and final basis as an exact rational implementation, from which the final result is reconstructed and certified exactly. Our analysis shows that correctness depends on both arithmetic precision and a careful handling of numerical tolerances. Based on these results, we develop a tolerance-aware, precision-boosting simplex algorithm with formal correctness guarantees. Finally, we introduce a delta-complete termination criterion that allows the algorithm to terminate once certified upper and lower bounds on the optimal objective differ by at most a user-specified threshold delta, providing a certified, user-controlled optimality gap.

</details>


### [146] [Sparsity Regularized and Robust Mean Variance Portfolio Selection Under Ellipsoidal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11749)
*Deniz Akkaya,Emre Can Yayla,Buse Şen,Mustafa Ç. Pınar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带ℓ0惩罚的稳健稀疏均值-方差投资组合选择问题，刻画局部/全局最优解结构，设计分支定界算法与新型剪枝规则，并在真实市场数据上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均值-方差投资组合中存在稀疏性需求（控制交易成本与资产数量），同时均值收益向量存在不确定性。将ℓ0惩罚与椭球不确定集结合，可同时实现稀疏化与稳健化，但由此产生的优化问题非凸、非连续，求解困难，亟需针对其结构的高效算法。

Method: 在均值-方差框架中引入ℓ0惩罚以促进资产配置稀疏性，并用椭球不确定集刻画均值收益向量的不确定性，构建稳健稀疏优化模型。刻画局部最优解与全局最优解的结构性质，并将其应用于风险最小化与收益最大化两种表述。基于结构洞察设计分支定界算法，提出一种新的剪枝规则，可在单步内排除指数级数量的候选投资组合。

Result: 在真实市场数据上进行大量计算实验，并与混合整数二阶锥规划（MISOCP）求解器对比，结果表明所提方法有效且具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过刻画稳健稀疏投资组合问题的解结构并利用定制分支定界与高效剪枝，可显著提升求解效率；实验验证了该方法相对通用MISOCP求解器的优势。

Abstract: We investigate mean-variance portfolio selection with an $\ell_0$-penalty to promote sparsity in asset allocations. Uncertainty in the mean return vector is incorporated through an ellipsoidal uncertainty set, yielding a robust sparse optimization framework. We characterize the structure of both local and global minimizers and exploit these properties in the risk minimization and return maximization formulations. Building on this structural insight, we develop a branch-and-bound algorithm tailored to the resulting robust sparse portfolio problems, together with a new pruning rule that can discard exponentially many candidate portfolios in a single step. Extensive computational experiments on real market data, together with comparisons against a mixed-integer second-order cone programming solver, demonstrate the effectiveness and competitiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [147] [Stochastic Gradient Methods with Online Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11751)
*Wanyu Zhang,Wenzhi Gao,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出随机在线缩放梯度方法（SOSGM），将近期自适应预条件框架推广至随机优化，在大批量或方差缩减下建立收敛保证，并可与对角/低秩预条件器及重球动量兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的自适应预条件框架主要针对确定性优化，其在随机优化场景下的适用性与收敛性尚不明确，本文旨在将该框架推广到随机优化以获得实用且高效的自适应一阶方法。

Method: 提出SOSGM，作为arXiv:2505.23081与arXiv:2509.11007中自适应预条件框架的随机推广；在标准假设下，采用大批量（large batchsize）或方差缩减技术推导收敛保证；方法兼容常见对角和/或低秩预条件器以及重球动量，同时保持与Adam相当的内存与计算开销。

Result: 在标准假设下获得了SOSGM的收敛保证；大量数值实验显示其经验性能强劲；使用对角预条件器时，SOSGM及其变体在一系列统计学习任务上显著优于现有自适应一阶方法。

Conclusion: SOSGM为随机优化提供了通用、高效的自适应预条件方法，在理论上具备收敛保证，在实践中表现优于主流自适应一阶方法，并保持与Adam相当的成本。

Abstract: This paper introduces Stochastic Online Scaled Gradient Methods (SOSGM), a generalization of the recently developed adaptive preconditioning framework in arXiv:2505.23081 and arXiv:2509.11007 to stochastic optimization. Under standard assumptions, we establish convergence guarantees for SOSGM using large batchsize or variance reduction. SOSGM is compatible with popular diagonal and/or low-rank preconditioners as well as heavy-ball momentum, while maintaining memory and computation cost comparable to Adam. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the strong empirical performance of SOSGM. Using a diagonal preconditioner, SOSGM and its variants substantially outperform existing adaptive first-order methods across a range of statistical learning tasks.

</details>


### [148] [Constant Steps Are s-Composable: An Exact Interpolation Certificate for Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11784)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了Grimmer、Shu和Wang提出的关于平衡常数步长schedule在任意horizon下s-composable的猜想，对任意n≥1给出显式的、无维度的正线性组合证书，从而验证了尖锐的混合终端Lyapunov不等式及相应的目标间隙与梯度范数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grimmer、Shu和Wang提出：当r为方程r^n(1+n(1+r))=1在(0,1)上的唯一解、归一化步长h̄=1+r时，平衡常数schedule是否在每个horizon都s-composable？n=1,2已知成立，但一般情形n≥3尚未解决。本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 通过构造光滑凸插值不等式的显式、无维度非负线性组合来证明该猜想。证书由支撑在连续索引区间上的矩阵组装而成：其非对角元素自动为正，二次部分为对角矩阵，其余乘子归结为两族标量。作者推导出这两族的闭式表达式，并利用严格凹性与初等有理不等式证明其正性，同时显式处理所有例外horizon与边界索引。

Result: 证明了对每个n该猜想均成立。由此，对每个L-光滑凸函数，常数schedule满足原论文所猜想的尖锐混合终端Lyapunov不等式，以及相应的同时目标间隙与梯度范数界。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Grimmer-Shu-Wang猜想的全部情形（包括n≥3的开放情形），给出了显式且无维度的证明证书，并统一了所有例外horizon与边界索引的处理。

Abstract: Grimmer, Shu, and Wang asked whether a balanced constant schedule is $s$-composable at every horizon. More precisely, for an integer $n\geq 1$, let $\bar{h}=1+r$, where $r\in(0,1)$ is the unique solution of $$r^n\bigl(1+n(1+r)\bigr)=1,$$ and run gradient descent for $n$ steps with normalized stepsize $\bar{h}$. The cases $n=1,2$ were known, while the general case $n\geq3$ was left open. We prove the conjecture for every $n$. Our proof gives an explicit, dimension-free nonnegative linear combination of the smooth convex interpolation inequalities. The certificate is assembled from matrices supported on contiguous index intervals. Its off-diagonal entries are automatically positive, its quadratic part is diagonal, and its remaining multipliers reduce to two scalar families. We derive closed forms for those families and prove positivity using strict concavity and elementary rational inequalities. Consequently, for every $L$-smooth convex function, the constant schedule satisfies the sharp mixed terminal Lyapunov inequality conjectured in the original paper, together with the associated simultaneous objective-gap and gradient-norm bounds. All exceptional horizons and boundary indices are treated explicitly.

</details>


### [149] [Optimal Recursive Composition and Dyadic Phase Laws for Gradient Descent with Predetermined Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11788)
*Yu Liu,Kang Chen,Rujun Jiang,Tianyu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文刻画了通过递归组合构造的最优步长调度的收敛性，证明其收敛率中存在非常数的对数周期调制，并给出指数 $p=\log_2(1+\sqrt2)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预定步长调度（尤其是精心设计的长步）已被证明能加速光滑凸函数上的梯度下降，而一类重要调度通过递归组合构造。然而，这类优化递归调度的收敛行为尚未被精确刻画，特别是其是否具有非平凡的周期调制结构以及最优分裂方式是否存在，需要深入分析。

Method: 针对对称递归框架（primitive 和 OBS-S 构造），证明在每个时间尺度上平衡分裂均为最优，从而解决 Zhang 和 Jiang 的猜想；对于非对称框架（OBS-F 构造），分析其最优分裂虽不一定平衡，但仍保持相同的 Silver 指数。

Result: 对于对称递归框架，证明对任意 $N \geq 1$，优化调度满足 $f(x_{N-1})-f^\ast \le \frac{1}{2N^p \Phi(\log_2 N)-1} \frac{L}{2}\|x_0-x^\ast\|^2$，其中 $p=\log_2(1+\sqrt2)$，$\Phi$ 为正的、Lipschitz 的、非常数的 1-周期函数；对于非对称 OBS-F 构造，证明相同 Silver 指数渐近保持，但伴随不同的对数周期调制。

Conclusion: 优化递归步长调度的收敛率呈现非平凡的对数周期调制，平衡分裂在对称框架下逐尺度最优；该结果刻画了递归组合型加速梯度下降的精确收敛行为。

Abstract: Predetermined stepsize schedules featuring carefully chosen long steps have recently been shown to accelerate gradient descent (GD) on smooth convex functions. A prominent class of such schedules is built through recursive composition. In this paper, we characterize the convergence of these optimized recursive schedules, revealing a non-constant log-periodic modulation across prescribed horizons. Specifically, for symmetric recursive frameworks (primitive and OBS-S constructions), we prove that for every $N \geq 1$, the corresponding optimized schedules satisfy $f(x_{N-1})-f^\ast \le \frac{1}{2N^p Φ(\log_2N)-1} \frac{L}{2}\|x_0-x^\ast\|^2$, $ p=\log_2(1+\sqrt2)$, where $Φ$ is a positive, Lipschitz, nonconstant $1$-periodic function. We derive this by proving that balanced splitting is optimal at every horizon for these constructions, resolving a conjecture of Zhang and Jiang. Furthermore, for the asymmetric framework (the OBS-F construction), we show that although optimal splits are not necessarily balanced, the same Silver exponent asymptotically persists alongside a distinct log-periodic modulation.

</details>


### [150] [Constrained Deep Inventory Management Using Forward-Backward SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11817)
*Keunwoo Lim,Michael Bloem*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将带路径约束（总运营限额）的多产品补货问题建模为安全随机最优控制问题，用FBSDE表示HJB方程并学习值函数及其梯度，在每个决策时刻求解由控制障碍函数导出的二次规划，扩展了高相对阶随机构造以处理四类混合相对阶的耦合聚合约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常以期望形式施加运营限额约束，而实际补货问题中的限额是沿样本路径逐时强加的（pathwise）。同时，多产品、在途与在手库存、仓库容量和排放额度共同构成安全集合，需在损失销售（lost-sales）情形下处理混合相对阶的聚合约束，这带来了新的建模与控制难题。

Method: 将多产品损失销售补货问题表述为安全随机最优控制问题，其中在手库存、在途库存、仓库容量与排放额度定义安全集。用前向-后向随机微分方程（FBSDE）系统表示HJB方程，通过学习值函数及其梯度求解。每个决策时刻求解由控制障碍函数（CBF）导出的二次规划。由于采购订单仅进入在途管道，在途约束相对阶为一，而在手约束相对阶为二。作者将(Clark, 2021)的单障碍高相对阶随机构造扩展到四类混合相对阶的耦合聚合约束，并通过设计确立其初始条件要求。

Result: 提出了能处理四类混合相对阶耦合聚合约束的扩展高相对阶随机构造，并给出满足初始条件要求的构造方式；结合FBSDE学习与CBF二次规划，实现了逐路径强加约束下的多产品补货策略求解。

Conclusion: 在路径约束替代期望约束的设定下，将多产品补货建模为安全随机最优控制并借助FBSDE-CBF方法求解是可行的；所提出的混合相对阶扩展与初始条件设计为处理多约束耦合的安全控制提供了有效框架。

Abstract: We study multi-product replenishment subject to aggregate operating limits imposed pathwise in place of expectation. On-hand and in-transit inventory, warehouse capacity and an emission allowance define a safe set, and the resulting multi-product lost-sales problem is posed as a safe stochastic optimal control problem. The Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman (HJB) equation is represented as a system of forward--backward stochastic differential equations (FBSDEs) and solved by learning the value function and its gradient, with a quadratic program derived by control barrier function (CBF) is solved at each decision epoch. Because purchase orders enter the in-transit pipeline only, the in-transit constraints have relative degree one while the on-hand constraints have relative degree two. We extend the stochastic high-relative-degree construction of (Clark, 2021) from a single barrier to four coupled aggregate constraints of mixed relative degree, and we establish its initial-condition requirement by design.

</details>


### [151] [Conformal Margins for Electrical Transmission Capacity Under Fixed Balancing Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11823)
*Sushanth Balaraman,Shriram Srinivasan,Kaarthik Sundar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出数据驱动的输电网络稳态发电调度微调方法：用分裂共形预测构造线路裕度与AGC备用校准，嵌入凸安全再调度问题，实现有限样本的线路安全与非饱和AGC概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需求与可再生能源出力不确定、且AGC策略固定的条件下，缓解电网阻塞；现有调度未能兼顾不确定性导致的残余潮流与AGC调节裕度，需在尽量小改动计划调度的前提下提供概率安全保证。

Method: 1) 证明在非饱和AGC固定策略下，AGC后残余注入分布与计划发电调度无关；据此将线路负载分解为调度相关基线与不确定性引起的残余分量。2) 利用历史预测误差，采用分裂共形预测构造线路裕度，提供无分布假设、有限样本的逐线路或联合覆盖保证。3) 校准AGC前聚合失配，确定所规定AGC响应所需的发电裕度（headroom）。4) 将校准量纳入凸安全再调度（secure re-dispatch）问题，在保留足够输电容量与AGC裕度的同时最小化对名义调度的调整。

Result: 所得修正调度对逐线路安全与AGC非饱和运行提供有限样本概率保证。在RTS-GMLC系统算例中，样本外线路越限显著减少，且运行成本仅小幅上升。

Conclusion: AGC后残余注入分布的调度不变性是关键结构性结论，使调度相关基线与不确定性残余分量可解耦；结合共形预测的裕度校准可转化为凸再调度问题，实现有概率保证的低成本阻塞管理。

Abstract: This article presents a data-driven approach to minimally adjust forecast-based steady-state generation dispatch in electrical transmission networks. The goal is to manage congestion caused by uncertainty in demand and renewable generation under a fixed automatic generation control (AGC) policy operating without saturation. Under this policy, we first show that the post-AGC residual injection distribution is invariant to the scheduled generation dispatch. This result separates line loading into a dispatch-dependent baseline and an uncertainty-induced residual component. Using historical forecast errors, we then apply split conformal prediction to construct transmission-line margins with distribution-free, finite-sample linewise or joint coverage guarantees. We also calibrate the aggregate pre-AGC mismatch to determine the generation headroom required for the prescribed AGC response. We incorporate these calibrated quantities into a convex secure re-dispatch problem that minimally adjusts the nominal schedule while reserving sufficient transmission capacity and AGC headroom. The resulting corrected dispatch provides finite-sample probabilistic guarantees on per-line security and unsaturated AGC operation. Case studies on the RTS-GMLC system demonstrate substantial reductions in out-of-sample line-limit violations with modest operating-cost increases.

</details>


### [152] [WFDroneBench: A Benchmark for Sensor Placement and Drone Routing for Wildfire Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11829)
*Romain Puech,Danique de Moor,Ana Trisovic,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出开源Python基准库WFDroneBench，将机器学习风险地图与基于优化的传感器、充电站和无人机部署策略结合，用于早期野火检测的标准化评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火日益频繁且严重，威胁生态系统、公共健康和基础设施；早期检测至关重要，但现有监测系统能力有限。无人机可提供移动实时覆盖，然而动态火区中的传感器布设与无人机路径优化仍具挑战。

Method: WFDroneBench整合机器学习风险地图与基于优化的部署策略，针对传感器、充电站和无人机进行配置；采用标准化指标和真实野火仿真，对风险图、优化策略与监测设备进行评测，并支持预测与决策两类组件的基准测试。数据集包含49个地点的7746个场景，源自历史起火点、真实风险地图与模拟火势蔓延，并集成2种地面探测器与3种无人机路径策略。

Result: 当风险图足够准确时，风险感知策略Max-Coverage显著优于其他基线，在最困难的火情上实现最快检测；风险感知的静态基础设施即使在不完美风险图下也有帮助；无人机检测优于地面传感器。同时揭示两个开放挑战：(i) 快速可靠检测小火情；(ii) 改进风险图预测，真实起火模式与现有风险图之间的差距为机器学习创新提供了重要机会。

Conclusion: WFDroneBench为早期野火检测提供统一开源基准，验证了风险感知策略与无人机检测的优势，并指出小火情检测与风险图预测改进是未来关键方向；代码、数据和文档已公开。

Abstract: Increasingly frequent and severe wildfires threaten ecosystems, public health, and infrastructure. Early detection is vital but limited by existing monitoring systems. Drones offer mobile, real-time coverage, but optimizing sensor placement and drone routing in dynamic fire zones remains challenging. To address this, we introduce WFDroneBench, an open-source Python benchmarking library for early wildfire detection that integrates machine-learned risk maps with optimization-based deployment strategies for sensors, charging stations, and drones. It evaluates risk maps, optimization strategies, and monitoring equipment using standardized metrics and realistic wildfire simulations. The framework supports benchmarking across predictive and decision-making components: machine learning researchers can assess risk models and compare routing strategies. WFDroneBench includes 7746 scenarios across 49 locations, built from historical ignitions, real-world wildfire risk maps, and simulated fire spread, along with two ground detector and three drone routing strategies. Our experiments show that the risk-aware strategy Max-Coverage significantly outperforms other baselines when risk maps are sufficiently accurate, achieving the fastest detection on the most difficult fires. We further find that risk-aware static infrastructure helps even under an imperfect risk map and drone-based detection outperforms ground sensors.
