Finally, our results reveal two key open challenges: (i) detecting small fires rapidly and reliably, and (ii) improving risk-map prediction, where the gap between ground-truth ignition patterns and available risk maps highlights a significant opportunity for ML innovation. We openly release all code, data, and documentation.

</details>


### [153] [Strong Consistency and Optimal Tracking of the Åström-Wittenmark Self-Tuning Regulator with Unknown Input Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11831)
*Zhaobo Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了未知输入增益下Åström-Wittenmark自校正调节器的强一致性与最优跟踪问题，在无参考激励、无增益调整、无外加探测信号的条件下，对最小相位线性系统与鞅差噪声，证明了普通最小二乘结合确定性等价控制的联合保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果要么需要在参考信息增长条件下才能建立稳定性、最优跟踪与参数一致性，要么需通过调整反馈估计或添加衰减探测信号才能获得性能保证。本文旨在摆脱这些额外条件或修改，直接对未修改的递推给出联合保证。

Method: 针对一类带鞅差噪声的最小相位线性系统，采用普通最小二乘（OLS）与确定性等价控制进行未修改递推。一致性证明的关键是建立一个关于辅助最小二乘噪声预测与参考之间累积平方差的对数下界；若增益误差持续存在，该噪声信息与实际最小二乘递推将对同一加权平方和给出不相容的下界和上界。

Result: 对每个有界参考：平均输入与输出能量几乎必然有界；平均平方跟踪误差几乎必然收敛到噪声方差；只要估计参数不少于两个，所有参数估计均强一致。

Conclusion: 通过矛盾论证先确立增益收敛，进而得到稳定性与全参数一致性。结果表明，无需参考激励、增益调整或外加探测信号，即可实现强一致性与最优跟踪。

Abstract: We study strong consistency and optimal tracking of the Åström-Wittenmark self-tuning regulator with unknown input gain. Existing results establish stability, optimal tracking and parameter consistency for the unmodified recursion under growth conditions on reference information. Other approaches obtain performance guarantees by adjusting the estimates used in feedback or adding decaying probing signals. For a class of minimum-phase linear systems with martingale difference noise, we establish joint guarantees for ordinary least squares with certainty-equivalent control without reference excitation, gain adjustment or added probing. For each bounded reference, average input and output energy are almost surely bounded, average squared tracking error converges almost surely to the noise variance, and all parameter estimates are strongly consistent whenever at least two parameters are estimated. For the consistency result, the key is a logarithmic lower bound on the cumulative squared difference between an auxiliary least-squares prediction of the noise and the reference. If the gain error persisted, this noise information and the actual least-squares recursion would give incompatible lower and upper bounds on the same weighted squared sum. This contradiction establishes gain convergence before stability and full parameter consistency.

</details>

### [154] [Geometric Ginzburg-Landau theory of charge ordering and commensurability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10678)
*Aneesh Agarwal,Rutvij Gholap,Mohammad Saeed Bahramy,Robert-Jan Slager*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文建立了包含量子几何贡献的有效Ginzburg-Landau理论，提出了电荷密度波（CDW）形成与公度性转变的普适判据，并在过渡金属硫族化合物中解决了纯动能判据失效的长期难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何概念近期在物理响应、超导和光学跃迁等领域带来新见解，尤其在近平坦色散体系中效应最显著。然而其在电荷密度波形成这一重要物理现象中的作用尚不明确，且现有纯动能CDW判据在描述正确有序波矢时存在长期未解的矛盾。

Method: 推导含未探索贡献的有效Ginzburg-Landau理论，将电子-声子相互作用表现为纯粹的电子量子几何增强/抑制效应，从而建立CDW形成与公度性转变的普适判据；并以一类过渡金属硫族化合物为基准进行验证。

Result: 发现CDW形成与公度性转变的普适判据；在过渡金属硫族化合物中成功解决了既定纯动能CDW判据无法描述正确有序波矢的长期难题；理论可推广至超导等其他声子介导相。

Conclusion: 量子几何在CDW形成中起核心作用；该框架提供了稳健判据与基本见解，可直接应用于其他声子介导相及其相互作用的探究，并为研究量子几何在相变中的作用提供了通用方法。

Abstract: The concept of quantum geometry has recently led to reinvigorated insights in a wide range of fields including physical responses, superconductivity, and optical transitions, with effects most pronounced in systems with nearly flat dispersion. Here, we show that it plays an essential role in charge density wave formation (CDW) -- an important physical phenomenon that is responsible for driving various sharp changes in material transport properties including metal-insulator transitions. We derive an effective Ginzburg-Landau theory including uncharted contributions and, as a highlight, discover a general criterion for both CDW formation and commensurability transitions where underlying electron-phonon interactions manifest purely as electronic quantum geometric enhancements/suppressions. We benchmark our framework in a class of transition-metal dichalcogenides and resolve a longstanding puzzle where well-established purely kinetic CDW criteria fail in describing the correct ordering wavevector. Besides rendering robust criteria and fundamental insights that are immediately relevant to several experimental charge ordering systems, our theory can also be applied directly to other phonon-mediated phases such as superconductivity, and can be used as an important tool to explore the interplay between various such states. More generally, our framework provides a recipe for investigating the role of quantum geometry in phase transitions.

</details>


### [155] [Lattice 2-group symmetries: operators, defects, and gauging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10682)
*Lucas Z. Brito,Salvatore D. Pace*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在2+1维量子格点系统中构造并研究了有限2-群对称性的格点实现，涵盖分裂2-群与中心2-群两类，并探讨其算符、缺陷、对偶融合2-范畴及可解哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限2-群对称性在量子场论中已被广泛研究，但其在具有有限维张量积希尔伯特空间的量子格点系统中的具体实现尚不充分，需要建立格点层面的构造并与场论预期对照。

Method: 针对0-型对称群G与1-型对称群A构成的两类2-群：Postnikov类[β]∈H³(G,A)平凡的分裂2-群，以及作用ρ:G→Aut(A)平凡的中心2-群，在全张量积希尔伯特空间上构造对称算符，使其限制在格点A 1-型对称的拓扑子空间上时成为2-群对称算符；并据此提出局域对称哈密顿量族及可解极限。

Result: 格点分裂2-群对称算符是on-site的；格点中心2-群对称算符非on-site，需引入辅助比特（ancillae）才能变为on-site。ρ与[β]的各种表现——包括带对称荷算符的变换、格点2-群对称缺陷的结构、以及规范化后得到的对偶融合2-范畴对称性——均与量子场论预期一致。所提出的哈密顿量族具有自发2-群对称破缺相与对称富化拓扑序相的可解极限，其中规范化哈密顿量可作为对应2-群规范理论的可解格点实现，并计算出其基态简并度。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了2+1维量子格点系统中有限2-群对称性的系统格点实现，验证了格点构造与量子场论预期的一致性，并为自发2-群对称破缺及对称富化拓扑序提供了可解哈密顿量模型，其中规范化极限实现了2-群规范理论。

Abstract: We construct and study lattice realizations of finite 2-group symmetries in ${2+1}$d quantum lattice systems with finite-dimensional tensor-product Hilbert spaces. We focus on two broad classes of 2-groups with 0-form symmetry group $G$ and 1-form symmetry group $A$: split 2-groups with trivial Postnikov class ${[β]\in\mathcal{H}^3(G,A)}$, and central 2-groups with trivial action ${ρ\colon G\to\text{Aut}(A)}$. In both cases, we construct symmetry operators on the full tensor-product Hilbert space that become 2-group symmetry operators when restricted to the topological subspace of the lattice $A$ 1-form symmetry. While the lattice split 2-group symmetry operators are onsite, the lattice central 2-group symmetry operators are not, and can only be made onsite after introducing ancillae. We extensively explore various manifestations of $ρ$ and $[β]$ for these lattice 2-group symmetry operators and demonstrate their agreement with expectations from quantum field theory. These manifestations arise in the transformation of operators carrying symmetry charge, the structure of lattice 2-group symmetry defects, and the dual fusion 2-category symmetries obtained by gauging the lattice 2-group symmetries. We further propose families of local symmetric Hamiltonians for both classes of lattice 2-group symmetries and identify exactly solvable limits lying in phases with spontaneous 2-group symmetry breaking and nontrivial symmetry-enriched topological order. In one such limit, the gauged Hamiltonians are exactly solvable lattice realizations of the corresponding 2-group gauge theories, whose ground-state degeneracies we calculate.

</details>


### [156] [A Unified Theory of Collective Magnon and Orbiton Excitations in Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10708)
*Bishal Das,Chanchal K. Barman,Aftab Alam*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在装饰正方格子上构建扩展的Kugel'-Khomskiĭ自旋-轨道模型，统一研究两类交替磁性的集体激发，揭示手性劈裂的微观起源，并探讨有限温度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁性是一种净磁化为零但具有动量依赖自旋劈裂的共线磁相，源于非磁环境不等价。近期提出强电子关联可通过轨道有序在等价非磁环境下产生自发交替磁性。然而以往研究多关注电子结构，对这两类机制所对应的集体激发缺乏统一理解。

Method: 在装饰正方格子上发展扩展的Kugel'-Khomskiĭ自旋-轨道模型，在同一理论框架内同时纳入非磁环境不等价与关联驱动的轨道有序。采用自洽平均场自旋波-轨道波形式化方法进行求解，并结合经典蒙特卡洛模拟研究有限温度演化。

Result: 发现互不杂化但相互依赖的磁振子与轨道振子激发，呈现特征性手性劈裂。该劈裂源于两个微观贡献：来自非磁环境不等价的晶格依赖项，以及在等价非磁环境下依然存在的轨道序诱导交换各向异性项。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了两类交替磁性的集体激发。有限温度分析表明自旋波与轨道波近似因虚假的一阶相变而失效，而经典蒙特卡洛模拟恢复预期的连续二阶行为。工作为理解交替磁体中的磁振子与轨道振子集体激发建立了统一微观框架。

Abstract: Altermagnetism has recently emerged as a distinct collinear magnetic phase exhibiting momentum-dependent spin-splitting despite vanishing net magnetization, as a consequence of inequivalent non-magnetic environments. Lately, it has been proposed that strong electronic correlations may yield spontaneous altermagnetism due to orbital ordering even for equivalent non-magnetic environments. While previous studies have largely focused on the electronic structure, a unified understanding of the collective excitations associated with these two different microscopic mechanisms stabilizing altermagnetism remains absent. Here, we develop an extended Kugel'-Khomskiĭ spin-orbital model on a decorated square lattice that simultaneously incorporates inequivalent non-magnetic environments and correlation-driven orbital ordering within a common theoretical framework. Employing a self-consistent mean-field spin-wave orbital-wave formalism, we demonstrate the emergence of mutually unhybridized but interdependent magnon and orbiton excitations exhibiting characteristic chiral-splitting. We show that the splitting originates from two distinct microscopic contributions: a lattice-dependent term arising from inequivalent non-magnetic environments and an orbital-order-induced exchange-anisotropy term that survives even for equivalent non-magnetic environments. The proposed framework therefore unifies the collective excitations associated with both types of altermagnetism. We further investigate the finite-temperature evolution of the coupled spin-orbital system, revealing the breakdown of spin-wave and orbital-wave approximations through spurious 1st-order transitions, while complementary classical Monte Carlo simulations recover the expected continuous 2nd-order behaviour. Our work establishes a unified microscopic framework for understanding collective magnon and orbiton excitations in altermagnets.

</details>


### [157] [Competing Interlayer Loop Currents and Superconductivity in the Bilayer $t$-$J_\perp$-$V$ Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10926)
*Luciano Zinni,Fabricio Gómez,Jun Zhan,Matías Bejas,Xianxin Wu,Andreas P. Schnyder,Andrés Greco*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用受控大N展开研究镍酸盐双层t-J⊥-V模型，发现层间交换作用J⊥可同时驱动面外s波超导与面外键序相(z-BOP)失稳，二者竞争形成穹顶状超导区，最佳超导出现在z-BOP量子临界点附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加压及薄膜双层镍酸盐中发现高Tc超导且具强层间交换耦合，与铜氧化物超导存在潜在相似性，使其成为凝聚态物理热点；需从强关联模型角度理解其超导与竞争序机制。

Method: 采用受控大N展开（平均场及超越平均场层次），研究强关联单轨道d_{x^2-y^2}双层t-J⊥-V模型，重点分析面外自旋交换作用J⊥与库仑相互作用V的影响。

Result: J⊥同时触发出面外s波超导与面外键序相(z-BOP)失稳；z-BOP产生以虚部为主的复z轴跃迁，驱动面外电流并诱发面内电流，在竖直格子上自发形成破坏时间反演的环路电流态。环路电流相与超导竞争形成穹顶状超导区，最佳超导位于z-BOP量子临界点附近。相图包含纯环路电流区、低掺杂共存相、较高掺杂的纯超导态以及关联金属态。

Conclusion: 层间交换J⊥是驱动面外超导与环路电流序的关键，二者竞争决定相图结构，最佳超导靠近z-BOP量子临界点，为理解镍酸盐超导机理及层间耦合作用提供理论框架。

Abstract: The recent discovery of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in pressurized and thin-film bilayer nickelates, featuring a strong interlayer exchange coupling, and their potential similarities with cuprate superconductors, has made this a very active topic in condensed matter physics. In the present paper we study the strongly correlated one-orbital ($d_{x^2-y^2}$) bilayer $t$-$J_\perp$-$V$ model for nickelates, where $V$ denotes the Coulomb interactions, using a controlled large-$N$ expansion at and beyond the mean-field level. Focusing on the out-of-plane spin exchange interaction ($J_\perp$), we find that it triggers both out-of-plane $s$-wave superconductivity and an out-of-plane bond-order phase ($z$-BOP) instability. The $z$-BOP gives rise to a complex $z$-axis hopping dominated by its imaginary component, which drives out-of-plane currents and induces in-plane ones, spontaneously forming on the vertical plaquettes a loop-current state that breaks time-reversal symmetry. Competition between this loop-current phase and superconductivity yields a dome-shaped superconducting region, with optimal superconductivity occurring near the $z$-BOP quantum critical point. The resulting phase diagram features a pure loop-current region, a low-doping coexistence phase, a pure superconducting state at higher doping, and a correlated metallic state.

</details>


### [158] [Optically induced metallic state with persistent monoclinic symmetry in NdNiO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11238)
*Christian Tzschaschel,Felix Utsch,Qi Song,Spencer Doyle,Grace A. Pan,Charles M. Brooks,Julia A. Mundy,Clemens von Korff Schmising,Stefan Eisebitt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用时间分辨光学反射率和对称敏感的二次谐波产生，同步追踪NdNiO₃光致绝缘体-金属转变中的金属性和对称性，发现二者在非平衡条件下解耦，并在低激发通量下稳定一种兼具金属反射率与单斜对称性的"光学诱导单斜金属"亚稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解电子序与结构序在非平衡条件下是否仍保持耦合，是关联材料研究的核心挑战。

Method: 采用时间分辨光学反射率与对称敏感的二次谐波产生两种探测手段，同步测量NdNiO₃光致绝缘体-金属转变过程中的金属性与结构对称性，并比较不同激发通量下的行为。

Result: 金属性反射率出现所需的激发通量明显低于正交高温对称性恢复所需的通量；低通量下形成亚稳态，兼具金属相的反射率与绝缘相的单斜对称性，即光学诱导单斜金属；仅在更高通量下对称性才完全恢复到高温相。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了金属性与结构对称性在非平衡条件下的解耦，并证明同步多探针光谱是识别关联材料中涌现相的有力手段。

Abstract: Understanding whether electronic and structural order remain coupled under nonequilibrium conditions is a central challenge in correlated materials. Here, we simultaneously track metallicity and symmetry across the photoinduced insulator-to-metal transition in NdNiO$_3$ using time-resolved optical reflectivity and symmetry-sensitive second-harmonic generation. We find that metallic reflectivity emerges at significantly lower excitation fluence than restoration of the orthorhombic high-temperature symmetry. As a result, optical excitation stabilizes a metastable state that combines the reflectivity of the metallic phase with the monoclinic symmetry of the insulating phase, revealing an optically induced monoclinic metal. Only at substantially higher fluences does the symmetry fully recover to that of the high-temperature phase. These results demonstrate a nonequilibrium decoupling of metallicity and structural symmetry and establish simultaneous multiprobe spectroscopy as a powerful approach for identifying emergent phases in correlated materials.

</details>


### [159] [Instabilities in self-consistent diagrammatic approaches and how to cure them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11405)
*Marcel Gievers,Herbert Eßl,Stefan Rohshap,Anna Kauch,Alessandro Toschi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 自洽图解方法中的"误导性收敛"源于迭代格式的稳定性条件，而非图解描述本身失效；通过反转雅可比矩阵不稳定特征方向，可在全参数范围内稳定物理不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自洽图解方法广泛用于计算关联量子材料性质，但在中间至强电子相互作用区（即最富物理意义的区域）适用性严重受限。核心问题是"误导性收敛"：迭代格式倾向于收敛到非物理不动点，这与数值精度无关，因此需要厘清其根源并寻找通用解决方案。

Method: 在玻色子交换形式论的通用图解框架下，考察若干已建立的自洽多电子方法，明确验证"误导性收敛"的根源。采用近期提出的补救方案：重定义迭代过程，即反转与自洽算法不动点相关联的雅可比矩阵的不稳定特征方向。并在可精确求解的测试模型上进行系统计算验证。

Result: 验证了误导性收敛的根源仅在于各迭代格式的稳定性条件，而非其自洽图解描述的内在崩溃；所提出的迭代重定义方案在可精确求解的测试模型上取得系统性成功。

Conclusion: 所考虑图解方案的物理不动点实际上可在整个参数范围内（包括最具挑战性的非微扰/强耦合区）实现稳定化，表明该简单通用的补救措施有效，扩展了自洽图解方法在强关联区域的适用性。

Abstract: While self-consistent diagrammatic approaches are widely used to compute the physical properties of correlated quantum materials, their applicability may get severely hindered precisely in the parameter regions, where the most exciting physics is observed. One of the major issues, referred to as "misleading convergence", is the tendency of iterative schemes to converge to unphysical fixed points for intermediate-to-strong electronic interactions, regardless of numerical accuracy of the computation. Here, we explicitly verify that the origin of this problem in several established self-consistent many-electron approaches, defined in the general diagrammatic framework of the boson-exchange formalism, resides exclusively in the stability condition of the respective iteration schemes, and not in an intrinsic breakdown of their self-consistent diagrammatic description. This insight enables a simple and general remedy, as recently proposed in Phys. Rev. Lett. 137, 016502 (2026): The redefinition of the iterative procedure, by inverting the unstable eigendirections of the Jacobian associated to the fixed point of the self-consistent algorithm. We illustrate the successful outcome of this procedure by means of systematic calculations performed on testbed, exactly solvable, models. Our results demonstrate that the physical fixed point of the diagrammatic schemes we considered can be stabilized, de facto, across the entire parameter range, including the most challenging nonperturbative/strong-coupling regimes.

</details>


### [160] [Topological band structure of excitons in octochlore KTb$_{3}$F$_{10}$: an analog of the Luttinger Semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11466)
*Olivier Demortier,Françoise Damay,Paul McClarty,Stéphane Raymond,Jacques Ollivier,Sylvain Petit*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用高分辨中子谱研究KTb₃F₁₀中色散晶体场激发的玻色型激子，揭示其散射强度中的pinch point特征，并指出主导的长程磁偶极相互作用在立方对称保护下形成三带二次接触（与Luttinger半金属相关），提供了pinch point散射强度涌现的通用长波模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁有序系统中存在磁长程有序与时间反演对称破缺，其能带结构可能蕴含不同于电子/光子/声子体系的新奇拓扑现象；作者希望拓展对磁系统可能能带结构的理解，探索单态直积基态之上色散晶体场激发中磁相互作用与能带拓扑的相互作用。

Method: 以KTb₃F₁₀（octochlore晶格的实验实现）为对象，采用高分辨率中子谱测量其玻色型激子的色散关系与散射强度；并结合长程磁偶极相互作用模型以及将偶极作用截断到有限壳层数的三带二次接触模型进行分析。

Result: 在中子散射强度中观察到pinch point特征；主导耦合为长程静磁偶极相互作用，其在区中心的奇异性传递给散射强度；当偶极作用截断到有限壳层数时，pinch point仍存在，表现为立方对称保护的三带二次接触。

Conclusion: 该三带二次接触模型与Luttinger半金属相关，为pinch point散射强度的涌现提供了通用长波模型，并同时涵盖真正的长程偶极模型，从而将磁激发能带拓扑拓展到与电子体系类似的分析框架。

Abstract: Understanding the symmetry and topology of band structures in crystalline solids has been among the defining themes of a generation of condensed matter physicists, with implications across many different platforms including electronic bands, photonic crystals, phonons and magnetic excitations. In the latter systems, there is usually magnetic long-range order and time reversal symmetry breaking. Here we further enrich the possible band structures available to magnetic systems by examining dispersive crystal field excitations above a ground state that is a simple product of singlets. In these systems, there is an interplay of magnetic interactions and band topology with both crystal and time reversal symmetries. Using high-resolution neutron spectroscopy we reveal the dispersion relations of bosonic excitons in KTb$_{3}$F$_{10}$, an experimental realization of the octochlore lattice. We show that there are pinch point features in the scattering intensity. The dominant coupling in this system is the long-range magnetostatic dipolar interaction, whose singularity at the zone center is passed onto the scattering intensity. However, the pinch points survive when the range of the dipoles is truncated to finite shell number in the form of a three-band quadratic band touching protected by cubic symmetry. This model, which is related to the Luttinger semi-metal, provides a general long wavelength model for the emergence of pinch point scattering intensity encompassing also the true long-range dipolar model.

</details>


### [161] [Robust Spin-1 Haldane Topology under Itinerant Doping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11591)
*Satoshi Nishimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用DMRG研究了铁磁Hund交换耦合的两个t-J链中自旋-1 Haldane SPT相的巡游空穴掺杂，发现存在一个具有C1S0低能描述的金属性掺杂Haldane SPT相，并给出其相图与掺杂终止机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究迁移载流子如何重组Haldane自旋结构：即空穴掺杂能否在保持Haldane SPT特征(弦序、有限自旋能隙、Haldane纠缠结构)的同时形成金属态，以及该相如何随掺杂与Hund耦合演化并终止。

Method: 对由铁磁Hund交换耦合的两条t-J链模型进行密度矩阵重整化群(DMRG)计算；测量弦序、自旋能隙、纠缠结构、纠缠熵标度、电荷关联与自旋-1/2缺陷梯格位置；并做稀薄极限分析以追踪铁磁相边界。

Result: 在较宽掺杂范围内弦序、有限自旋能隙与Haldane纠缠结构共存；纠缠熵标度与单一无能隙电荷模式一致，支持C1S0金属性掺杂Haldane SPT。低掺杂时短程电荷关联降低自旋-1/2缺陷梯格密度，高掺杂时其位置近乎无关联而自旋被集体纳入关联自旋部分。该相终止于完全自旋极化的铁磁相，并在高掺杂处形成回归口袋。稀薄极限分析将两条铁磁边界分别归因于弱Hund耦合下的自旋-电荷因子化与强Hund耦合下梯格三重态结合导致的配对迁移率降低。

Conclusion: 迁移载流子可在重组Haldane自旋结构的同时保留其标志性特征，从而形成金属性掺杂Haldane SPT相；其终止与掺杂驱动的铁磁相(含高掺杂回归口袋)密切相关，机制随Hund耦合强度不同而转变。

Abstract: We study itinerant hole doping of the spin-$1$ Haldane symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phase in two $t$--$J$ chains coupled by ferromagnetic (FM) Hund exchange. Density-matrix renormalization group calculations show that string order, a finite spin gap, and the Haldane entanglement structure coexist over a broad doping range. Combined with entanglement-entropy scaling consistent with a single gapless charge mode, these results support a metallic doped Haldane SPT with a $C1S0$ low-energy description. At low doping, short-range charge correlations reduce the density of spin-$1/2$ defect rungs, whereas at larger doping their positions become nearly uncorrelated while their spins are collectively incorporated into the correlated spin sector. The doped Haldane SPT terminates in a fully spin-polarized FM phase, which forms a reentrant pocket at high doping in the global phase diagram. Dilute-limit analyses trace the two FM boundaries to spin--charge factorization at weak Hund coupling and to rung-triplet binding with reduced pair mobility at strong Hund coupling. Our results show how mobile carriers reorganize the Haldane spin structure while preserving its characteristic signatures.

</details>


### [162] [Certified reduced-basis emulation of conformal field theories on the fuzzy sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11675)
*Virgil V. Baran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合总角动量守恒的精确对角化与可认证的降基（reduced-basis）仿真器，将模糊球（fuzzy-sphere）正则化下三维CFT的临界点搜索成本降低约两个数量级，并复现了Ising模型与O(2) Wilson–Fisher CFT的既有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊球正则化为三维共形场论（CFT）提供数值途径，但需要在微观耦合参数空间中逐一检测临界性，搜索代价高昂。

Method: 将基于具有确定总角动量态的精确对角化，与一个可认证的降基仿真器相结合；后者利用每个系统尺寸下少量精确解，连续重构能量、可观测量及其对耦合的导数。

Result: 复现了模糊球Ising模型已发表的基态能量分析，以及三味模糊球O(2) Wilson–Fisher CFT的共形数据（含参数导数定义的量）。在无CFT先验输入时，基态能量判据定位的临界耦合与尺寸大一倍以上的DMRG值相差在半百分点以内，并绘制出耦合平面上的临界线。

Conclusion: 基态判据能稳健地定位临界线，但其沿临界线选取的具体点约束较弱；在此情形下计算得到的标度维度在最接近bootstrap结果的点并非该选定点。

Abstract: Fuzzy-sphere regularization gives numerical access to three-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs), but locating one requires testing for criticality across the parameter space of microscopic couplings. We reduce the search cost by about two orders of magnitude, combining an exact diagonalization based on states having definite total angular momentum with a certified reduced-basis emulator that reconstructs energies, observables and their derivatives continuously across the couplings from a handful of exact solutions per system size. The combination reproduces the published ground-state-energy analysis of the fuzzy-sphere Ising model and the published conformal data of a three-flavor fuzzy-sphere realization of the O(2) Wilson--Fisher CFT, including quantities defined by parameter derivatives. Applied to the O(2) model with no CFT input, the ground-state-energy criterion locates the critical coupling to within half a percent of a density matrix renormalization group value obtained on systems more than twice the size, and maps the critical line of the model across the coupling plane. Ground-state criteria locate the critical line robustly, but the point they select along it is weakly determined, and the computed dimensions come closest to the bootstrap elsewhere along the line.

</details>


### [163] [Acoustic Wave-Function Imaging of Sublattice Physics in a Kagome Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11678)
*L. Müller,N. Endres,B. Geldiyev,S. Widmann,K. Burkard,M. Ünzelmann,F. Reinert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在厘米尺度构建声学kagome晶格，测量完整激发谱并成像各格点波函数的振幅与相位，首次直接实验观测到kagome晶格中子晶格分辨的能带结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome能带结构包含狄拉克锥、鞍点范霍夫奇点（vHS）和平带，这些特征在kagome晶格三格点基元中表现出独特的子晶格局域性；但实验验证这种子晶格特征极具挑战，需要同时获取实空间波函数及其动量空间织构。

Method: 在厘米尺度实现声学kagome晶格，测量完整激发谱，逐格点成像波函数的振幅与相位，从而研究整个能带结构上动量依赖的子晶格织构，特别是平带以及混合型和纯子晶格范霍夫奇点处的情形。

Result: 实验结果与最小紧束缚模型计算高度吻合，首次直接实验观测到kagome晶格中子晶格分辨的能带结构。

Conclusion: 利用声学kagome平台实现波函数振幅与相位的全格点成像，成功直接验证了kagome能带结构的子晶格分辨特征，为研究子晶格相关物理提供了实验途径。

Abstract: The canonical kagome band structure hosts a Dirac cone, saddle-point van Hove singularities (vHS), and a flat band. Characteristically, these features exhibit distinct sublattice localization within the three-site basis of the kagome lattice. However, experimental proof of this sublattice character is challenging and requires direct access to both the real-space wave function and its associated momentum-space texture. Here, we implement an acoustic kagome lattice on the cm scale and measure the full excitation spectrum, thereby imaging the wave function at each lattice site, including both amplitude and phase. This enables investigation of the momentum-dependent sublattice texture across the complete band structure, particularly at the flat band and at the mixed- and pure-sublattice van Hove singularities. Our results are in excellent agreement with minimal tight-binding model calculations and constitutes the first direct experimental observation of the sublattice-resolved band structure in a kagome lattice.

</details>


### [164] [Unraveling the Kagome Antiferromagnetic $3J$ Model and Its Materials: An Integrated Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11745)
*Xin Lu,Andreas Raikos,Menghan Song,Zezong Li,Lankun Han,Shiliang Li,Sylvain Capponi,Zi Yang Meng,Chengkang Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究kagome反铁磁Heisenberg模型（3J模型）的基态与有限温度性质，通过DMRG结合NQS及热张量网络方法，识别出介于两个磁有序相之间的中间量子自旋液体（QSL）相，并计算比热以与实验对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对候选Dirac量子自旋液体材料YCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Br$_2$[Br$_{1-x}$(OH)$_x$]的3J模型，作者希望将微观模型与实验可测量信号联系起来，理解阻挫量子磁体中的QSL现象，并为其提供统一的研究框架。

Method: 采用大规模密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）并辅以神经量子态（NQS）模拟确定基态相图；使用先进的指数（XTRG）和切空间（tanTRG）热张量网络方法计算比热。

Result: 在两个磁有序相之间识别出一个中间QSL相，该QSL与各向同性极限下的kagome自旋液体基态相分离。在磁有序相中，比热随温度在约为耦合强度$J_{hex}$一部分的温度处出现肩部，而该肩部在QSL相中消失。这些普适行为与3J材料在有序和QSL候选样品中实验观测到的比热一致。

Conclusion: 该工作将微观模型与实验可测量特征相连接，例证了理解阻挫量子磁体中QSL现象的综合方法，3J材料作为代表性案例，为未来研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state and finite-temperature properties of the kagome antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model with three inequivalent couplings, dubbed the 3J model, which is designed for the candidate Dirac quantum spin liquid (QSL) material YCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Br$_2$[Br$_{1-x}$(OH)$_x$] (see, e.g., Zeng et al., 2024). Employing large-scale density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) supplemented by neural quantum states (NQS) simulations, we identify an intermediate QSL phase between two magnetically ordered phases. We also find that this QSL is separated from the kagome spin liquid ground state at the isotropic limit. To establish a direct comparison with experiments, we compute the specific heat of the model by means of advanced exponential (XTRG) and tangent-space (tanTRG) thermal tensor-network methods. In the magnetically ordered phase, the specific heat over temperature exhibits a shoulder at a temperature that is a fraction of the coupling strength $J_{hex}$, which disappears in the QSL phase. These universal behaviors are consistent with the experimentally observed specific heat in 3J materials for both ordered and QSL candidate samples. Our work thus connects microscopic models with experimentally measurable signatures, exemplifying an integrated approach (see Meng et al., 2026) to understanding QSL phenomena in frustrated quantum magnets, with 3J materials serving as a representative case and providing a foundation for future studies.

</details>


### [165] [Learning Quantum Matter through Attention in Complex Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11792)
*Mingrui Jing,Erdong Huang,Jizhe Lai,Enji Xiong,Jin-Guo Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出复值 Psiformer 神经波函数，用于磁场下相互作用电子的变分优化。在与实值 Psiformer 相同优化步数下取得更低能量，平均复值优势约 1.458 meV/电子，并支持对磁通下密度关联和电流结构的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性多电子波函数需要振幅与相位协同优化，复值内部表示能否改善神经波函数的变分搜索是一个具有实际意义的设计问题。作者希望在磁莫尔连续统中构建可同时处理复相位、磁场边界条件与费米子反对称性的神经变分拟设。

Method: 提出 Complex Psiformer：结合复值隐藏特征与 Hermitian-magnitude attention，并施加磁边界条件和费米子反对称性。与 Real Psiformer 在相同优化步数下比较，并对比各自的神经 Hartree-Fock 参考；在 25 胞体系中使用五个训练种子评估平均表现；另训练二电子复值态与有限组态相互作用参考比较；通过磁通扫描分析密度关联和连通涨落，并用规范不变电流图比较局部环流。

Result: 在两个有限超胞中，相同优化步数后 Complex Psiformer 达到比 Real Psiformer 更低的能量，且两者均优于各自神经 Hartree-Fock 参考。在 25 胞体系中，五个训练种子下复值平均优势为 1.458 meV/电子，且观测离散度更小。单独训练的二电子复值态相对有限组态相互作用参考的能隙小于实值对应态。复值态的磁通扫描显示密度关联非单调变化，高磁通下蜂窝平均密度调制减弱，而连通涨落持续存在；规范不变电流图给出优化态局部环流的定性比较。

Conclusion: 这些基准结果支持将复值 Psiformer 组合架构作为有限磁系统中研究能量和电荷排布的变分拟设。

Abstract: Magnetic many-electron wavefunctions require amplitude and phase to be optimized together. Whether a complex internal representation improves this variational search is a practical question for neural wavefunction design. We introduce Complex Psiformer for interacting electrons in a magnetic moiré continuum, combining complex hidden features and Hermitian-magnitude attention with magnetic boundary conditions and fermionic antisymmetry. After the same number of optimization steps, Complex Psiformer reaches lower energies than Real Psiformer in two finite supercells. Both Psiformers also improve on their respective neural Hartree-Fock references. Across five training seeds in the 25-cell system, the mean Complex advantage is 1.458 meV per electron, with a smaller observed spread. A separately trained two-electron Complex state has a smaller energy gap to a finite configuration interaction reference than its Real counterpart. In the Complex states, flux scans show nonmonotonic density correlations and weaker honeycomb mean-density modulation at higher flux, while connected fluctuations persist. Gauge invariant current maps provide a qualitative comparison of local circulation in the optimized states. These benchmarks support the combined architecture as a variational ansatz for studying energies and charge arrangements in finite magnetic systems.

</details>


### [166] [Block entropy area based non-local fermionic mode optimization with gradient disentanglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11811)
*Miklós Antal Werner,Gero Friesecke,Andor Menczer,Kornél Kapás,Örs Legeza*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分块熵面积的模式优化算法，用于矩阵乘积态表示下的相互作用费米子多体量子态，通过梯度构造非相互作用有效解纠缠哈密顿量，结合TDVP与DMRG实现高效低秩基态算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵乘积态（MPS）表示相互作用费米子多体量子态时，其纠缠结构强依赖于单粒子基的选择，现有的基于最近邻模式旋转与重排的优化方案能力有限，需要更高效、更系统的模式优化方法来降低纠缠、提升低秩表示效率。

Method: 以分块熵面积为全局代价函数，从其梯度构造长程、非相互作用的有效解纠缠哈密顿量；利用基于投影分裂的时间依赖变分原理（TDVP）模拟该哈密顿量驱动的含时薛定谔方程以最小化代价函数；再将密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）与基于梯度的纠缠最小化相结合，形成低秩迭代基态算法，同时给出优化的单粒子基。

Result: 在相互作用费米子的二维格点模型和Fe4S4团簇上进行了验证，结果表明该方法具有鲁棒性，并优于此前使用最近邻模式旋转与重排的方案。

Conclusion: 基于分块熵面积梯度的解纠缠哈密顿量可有效优化MPS的单粒子基，结合DMRG形成高效低秩基态算法，在二维费米子格点与Fe4S4团簇上验证了其鲁棒性与优越性。

Abstract: We introduce a systematic block entropy area based mode optimization algorithm for many-body quantum states of interacting fermions represented by matrix product states. From the gradient of a global cost function, the block entropy area, a long-ranged, non-interacting effective disentangler Hamiltonian is formed. We then simulate the time-dependent Schrödinger equation driven by the disentangler Hamiltonian by employing the time-dependent variational principle based on projector splitting, and minimize the cost function. The combination of the density matrix renormalization group with this gradient-based entanglement minimization forms an efficient low-rank iterative ground-state algorithm that also provides an optimized single-particle basis for matrix product state representation. We demonstrate the method on two-dimensional lattice models of interacting fermions and the Fe${_4}$S${_4}$ cluster, and show its robustness and superiority over earlier protocols using nearest-neighbor mode rotations and reorderings.

</details>


### [167] [Sub-cycle doublon-holon dynamics in one-dimensional Mott insulators revealed by two-color high-harmonic spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11858)
*Lance Hatch,Aditya Verma,Eric Schultz,Hanjun Yang,Priscila Rosa,Genda Gu,Igor Zaliznyak,Giulio Vampa,Laimei Nie,Hanzhe Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文测量了一维Mott绝缘体中的高次谐波发射啁啾，揭示其由强驱动的doublon-holon复合主导，并观察到与谐波阶数相关的退相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态高次谐波光谱是探测非平衡多体动力学的新兴工具，但对关联材料中强驱动、亚光周期动力学的直接测量仍鲜有探索。

Method: 在一维Mott绝缘体原型材料中测量高次谐波发射的啁啾，以编码亚光周期尺度上的doublon-holon动力学。

Result: 在带隙以上谐波中观察到正啁啾，表明高次谐波由doublon-holon复合主导；同时发现与谐波阶数相关的退相位，可用不同谐波对应的doublon-holon偏移距离来解释。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了Mott绝缘体中相干的doublon-holon动力学及其超快退相位，对其他非平衡光致现象（如Floquet工程）具有参考意义。

Abstract: Solid-state high-harmonic spectroscopy is becoming an emerging tool for probing nonequilibrium many-body dynamics. Yet, direct measurements of strongly driven, sub-optical-cycle dynamics in correlated materials during high-harmonic emission remain largely unexplored. Here, we measure high-harmonic emission chirp in a prototypical one-dimensional Mott insulator, which encodes strongly driven doublon-holon dynamics at sub-optical-cycle timescales. We observe a positive chirp for above band gap harmonics, indicating that high harmonics are dominated by doublon-holon recombinations. We further show a harmonic order-dependent dephasing, which can be understood through different doublon-holon excursion distances associated with each harmonic. These results reveal coherent doublon-holon dynamics and their ultrafast dephasing in Mott insulators, which is relevant to other nonequilibrium light-induced phenomena, such as Floquet engineering.

</details>


### [168] [Low temperature thermodynamics of $S_{\mathrm{eff}}=1/2$ triangular lattice quantum spin liquid candidate TlYbS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11881)
*Bishnu P. Belbase,Arjun Unnikrishnan,Piyush Chhallare,Mohan B. Neupane,Eun Sang Choi,Sebastian Erdmann,Muhammad U. Akbar,Mamoun Hemmida,H. -A. Krug von Nidda,Philipp Gegenwart,Arnab Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过磁化率、ESR和比热测量研究了三角晶格反铁磁体TlYbS₂单晶的基态性质，发现零场下530 mK附近存在弱热力学反常，低温比热呈C_m∝T^1.8，并观测到强易面各向异性及随磁场演化的有序相、1/3磁化平台等丰富相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何阻挫三角晶格反铁磁体是研究磁有序与量子自旋液体（QSL）竞争的重要平台，Yb基delafossite家族AYbX₂提供了结构洁净的体系。为厘清该家族中基态性质随非磁性配体替换的演化规律，作者选取TlYbS₂进行系统研究，以与已报道的TlYbSe₂和KYbSe₂进行对比。

Method: 采用单晶X射线衍射确认结构与Yb³⁺三角层及无位点无序；结合直流磁化、交流磁化率、电子自旋共振（ESR）和比热测量，温度范围从室温延伸至毫开尔文量级，并在不同磁场取向（H⊥c与H∥c）下考察磁响应。

Result: 结构为三角R-3m，Yb³⁺层间分离良好且无位点无序。零场下约530 mK出现弱热力学反常，可能对应类KYbSe₂的弱有序态；300 mK以下零场磁比热C_m∝T^1.8，接近二次温度依赖，不同于TlYbSe₂的线性依赖。磁化和ESR显示强易面各向异性，g_⊥/g_∥≈6.9。530 mK反常对磁场响应高度各向异性：H⊥c时反常可见至约2 T，并在约2.5 T以上连续演化为场诱导有序相，随后出现包括约5–8 T的1/3磁化平台和约17 T完全极化的系列场诱导相；H∥c时反常减弱并在约3 T附近被抑制，与报道的从有序态到场诱导QSL的禁闭—退禁闭转变一致。

Conclusion: TlYbS₂表现出弱有序倾向与近二次比热行为，与TlYbSe₂的线性比热及自旋子—规范涨落图像形成对比，表明非磁性配体替换可显著调控基态。其强各向异性磁相图包含1/3平台及完全极化，并在H∥c下呈现可能的场诱导QSL行为，为研究阻挫三角晶格中磁有序与QSL竞争提供了新平台。

Abstract: Geometrically frustrated triangular-lattice antiferromagnets exhibit a delicate competition between magnetic order and quantum spin liquid (QSL) behavior, with the Yb-based delafossite family $A$Yb$X_2$ providing a structurally clean platform for exploring this physics. Here, we report a comprehensive study of single-crystal TlYbS$_2$ using DC magnetization, AC susceptibility, electron spin resonance (ESR), and specific heat measurements extending from room temperature to the millikelvin regime. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction confirms a trigonal $R\bar{3}m$ structure comprising well-separated triangular layers of Yb$^{3+}$ ions with no detectable site disorder. At zero field, a weak thermodynamic anomaly is observed near $530$ mK, which may indicate the onset of a weakly ordered state similar to that reported in KYbSe$_2$. Below $300$ mK, the zero-field magnetic specific heat follows $C_{\rm m}\propto T^{1.8}$, close to a quadratic temperature dependence, in contrast to the linear temperature dependence reported for the sister compound TlYbSe$_2$, which has been described in terms of the interplay between spinons and thermally excited gauge-flux excitations. Magnetization and ESR measurements establish pronounced easy-plane magnetic anisotropy, with $g_\perp/g_\parallel \approx 6.9$. The anomaly near $530$ mK exhibits a strongly anisotropic response to magnetic field. For $H\perp c$, it remains visible up to approximately $2$ T and continuously evolves toward a field-induced ordered phase above approximately $2.5$ T, followed by a sequence of field-induced phases that includes a $1/3$ magnetization plateau between approximately $5$ and $8$ T and full polarization near $17$ T. In contrast, for $H\parallel c$, the anomaly weakens and is suppressed near $3$ T, consistent with the recently reported confinement--deconfinement transition from an ordered state to a field-induced QSL.

</details>


### [169] [Effective Ionic Valence and Local Magnetic Moment in Kagome Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11927)
*Ruoshi Jiang,Zi-Jian Lang,Yuzki Oey,Andrea Capa Salinas,Stephen D. Wilson,Yongwei Li,Ilya Shipulin,Yiwen Zhang,Deng Hu,Zhiwei Wang,Hans-Henning Klauss,Zurab Guguchia,Vadim Grinenko,Wei Ku*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过Hartree量级的局域电子结构分析，指出AV₃Sb₅与ATi₃Bi₅中V、Ti均为+2价、巡游载流子密度相近，差异体现在量子涨落的离子磁矩上；并通过引入非磁性Sn杂质解除几何阻挫，用磁化率与μSR实验证实了局域磁矩的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解kagome超导体家族AV₃Sb₅（A = K, Rb, Cs）与ATi₃Bi₅（A = Rb, Cs）之间意外的相似性及关联行为，需澄清其局域电子结构与载流子来源。

Method: 计算体系的Hartree尺度局域电子结构；理论判断V、Ti的价态与巡游载流子密度；系统引入非磁性Sn杂质以局部解除kagome晶格的几何阻挫；采用磁化率测量与μ子自旋旋转/弛豫（μSR）进行实验验证。

Result: V与Ti离子均为+2价，巡游载流子密度相近，电子数差异反映在量子涨落的离子磁矩上；阻挫晶格使局域磁矩难以被常规探针观测；引入非磁性Sn杂质后，磁化率随杂质浓度系统性升高，μSR证实存在明确的局域磁矩。

Conclusion: 所发现的离子磁矩支持从纯巡游载流子图像向包含局域离子自旋强关联的范式转变；原子间与局域-巡游关联为该超导材料家族中丰富的关联行为提供了物理基础。

Abstract: In order to understand the unexpected similarity and the correlated behavior in kagome superconductor families AV$_3$Sb$_5$ (A = K, Rb, Cs) and ATi$_3$Bi$_5$ (A = Rb, Cs), we investigate the Hartree-scale local electronic structure of these systems. Our result indicates that V and Ti ions are both of 2+ valence such that the corresponding itinerant carrier densities are similar, and the difference in electron count is instead reflected in their quantum fluctuating ionic magnetic moments. However, due to the frustrated lattice geometry of these materials, such local moments are difficult to experimentally observe via standard probes. For verification, we systematically introduce nonmagnetic Sn impurities to locally relieve the geometric frustration and experimentally demonstrate the existence of well-defined local magnetic moments via magnetic susceptibility and muon spin rotation or relaxation ($μ$SR) measurements. All experiments discover a systematic increase of magnetic susceptibility upon increasing nonmagnetic impurity level. Our discovered ionic moments suggest a paradigm shift from the existing itinerant carrier-only picture to one incorporating strong correlation from local ionic spins. The associated interatomic and local-itinerant correlations offer a solid ground for the emergence of the observed rich correlated behavior in this new family of superconducting materials.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Henstock--Kurzweil Gauge Integral in the Non--Gaussian Regime: A Machine--Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10793)
*Yuri N. Berdinsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 Lean 4 与 Mathlib 中机器验证地构造了非高斯泛函积分：以 Henstock–Kurzweil 规范积分与 Chernoff 乘积逼近为核心，证明单模积分对耦合常数 λ 有限、严格正、单调且无穷可微，其导数由收敛积分而非发散微扰级数给出；M 模影响泛函可因子化并被其高斯值控制。方法不依赖 Wick 旋转、Wiener 测度或 zeta 正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯泛函积分通常依赖 Wick 旋转、Wiener 测度或解析延拓等工具，且微扰级数往往发散。作者希望给出一种完全直接、可机器检验的构造，避免这些间接手段，并在形式化证明中保证严格性。

Method: 采用 Henstock–Kurzweil 规范积分与 Chernoff 乘积逼近；研究对象为有限个玻色模，作用量 S(φ)=(1/2)φ^T A φ + λ Σ_i φ_i^4，A 正定。使用 Chernoff / Lie–Trotter 分裂处理自由生成元与非高斯生成元的非交换性；所有陈述在 Lean 4 与 Mathlib 中形式化，文件 HkNonGaussian.lean 无 sorry，仅使用 propext、Classical.choice、Quot.sound 三条标准公理。

Result: 证明单模积分 I(ω, j, λ) 在 [0, ∞) 上有限、严格正、单调且无穷可微，其导数由 φ^{4k} 的收敛积分给出而非发散微扰级数；M 模影响泛函可因子化为单模积分之积并受其高斯值约束。给出四个显式公式层面的应用：Duffing 振子、金融中的局部波动率 (CEV)、Wilson–Cowan 神经场以及非高斯量子库。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种直接且形式化验证的非高斯泛函积分构造，绕开了 Wick 旋转、Wiener 测度、zeta 正则化与从虚时间解析延拓等传统工具，并为多个物理与金融模型给出了可验证的显式结果。

Abstract: We develop a machine-checked construction of non-Gaussian functional integrals using the Henstock--Kurzweil gauge integral and Chernoff product approximations. The central object is a finite family of bosonic modes with action S(phi) = (1/2) phi^T A phi + lambda * sum_i phi_i^4, where A is positive definite. We prove that the one-mode integral I(omega, j, lambda) is finite, strictly positive, monotone and infinitely differentiable in the coupling lambda on [0, infinity). Its derivatives are given by convergent integrals of phi^{4k} with the same weight, not by the divergent perturbative series. The M-mode influence functional factorises into one-mode integrals and is bounded by its Gaussian value. A Chernoff / Lie--Trotter splitting handles the non-commutativity of the free and non-Gaussian generators. All statements are formalised in Lean 4 with Mathlib; the accompanying file HkNonGaussian.lean is free of sorry and uses only the standard axioms propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Four illustrative applications are worked out at the level of explicit formulas: the Duffing oscillator, local volatility (CEV) in finance, Wilson--Cowan neural fields, and non-Gaussian quantum reservoirs. The construction is completely direct and does not use Wick rotation, Wiener measure, zeta-regularisation or analytic continuation back from imaginary time.

</details>


### [171] [A Time-Frequency Framework for GKP Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10802)
*Franz Luef,Eduard Ortega*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为格点GKP码建立时频框架，利用向量值Zak变换与多窗口Gabor分析将逻辑信息映射为伴随格系数块，证明其归一化块映射为等距、给出恢复投影与稳定重构，并构造可归一化的GKP逼近算子，建立弱-*收敛与渐近等距编码，最后通过相位关系恢复位移并分析其扰动稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP码的逻辑信息通常承载于连续变量的理想码字，缺乏严格的时频分析刻画，且理想码字不可归一化、实际实现只能逼近。作者希望在格点GKP码的调制空间中建立统一的时频框架，给出有限维逻辑纤维的精确表示，并解决逻辑重构、可归一化逼近与位移综合征读取及其稳定性等核心问题。

Method: 将理想码字实现于调制空间M^∞中，通过向量值Zak变换将其与连续综合征环面上的有限逻辑纤维等同；用多窗口Gabor分析将逻辑向量表示为有限块伴随格系数，构造归一化块映射并证明其为等距，进而给出显式恢复投影与稳定逻辑重构；以格包络Gabor乘子构造可归一化的GKP逼近算子，证明弱-*收敛与渐近等距编码；利用平移后系数块之间的相位关系恢复位移综合征。

Result: 证明了归一化块映射是等距，并得到显式恢复投影与稳定的逻辑重构；构造出可归一化的GKP逼近算子，建立了弱-*收敛和渐近等距编码性；成功从平移系数块的相位关系中恢复位移综合征，并量化了其在加性扰动下的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作为格点GKP码提供了严格的时频分析框架，将逻辑编码、逼近方案与位移综合征读取统一在Gabor分析与Zak变换语言下，兼具等距性、稳定重构与扰动稳定性保证，为连续变量量子纠错的容错实现提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a time--frequency framework for lattice GKP codes in which ideal codewords are realized in the modulation space $M^\infty$ and identified, through a vector-valued Zak transform, with a finite logical fibre over the continuous syndrome torus. Multi-window Gabor analysis then represents the logical vector by a finite block of adjoint-lattice coefficients. We prove that the normalized block map is an isometry, obtain an explicit recovering projection, and derive stable logical reconstruction. We further construct normalizable GKP approximants as lattice-envelope Gabor multipliers and establish weak-$*$ convergence and asymptotically isometric encoding. Finally, we recover displacement syndromes from phase relations between translated coefficient blocks and quantify their stability under additive perturbations.

</details>


### [172] [Probabilistic representation and limit theorems for particle numbers of quasi-free states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10840)
*Fanch Coudreuse,Simone Rademacher,Oliver Tse*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究局部相互作用玻色准自由态的粒子数分布，证明其可分解为独立几何分布随机变量之和，并由此导出尾界、小数定律、大偏差原理与中心极限定理，应用于玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体量子耗尽的统计描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部相互作用玻色准自由态的粒子数分布出现在数学物理多个领域，物理学文献中已有相关预测，但缺乏严格的数学刻画及其统计极限行为。

Method: 将粒子数表示为无穷多个独立几何分布随机变量之和，并在此基础上分析其概率分布结构与不同晶格间距尺度下的极限定理。

Result: 得到粒子数的指数尾界；在晶格间距发散时获得小数定律及对数速度的大偏差原理；在晶格间距趋于零时获得中心极限定理；并将结果应用于玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体量子耗尽统计，常数可由相互作用势的散射长度显式表示。

Conclusion: 该几何分解为玻色准自由态粒子数提供了统一的概率刻画，验证了物理学预测，并给出量子耗尽统计的精确描述。

Abstract: We study the particle number distribution of locally interacting bosonic quasi- free states, which arise in various areas of mathematical physics. We show that the particle number decomposes into an infinite sum of independent geometrically distributed random variables, confirming predictions from the physics literature. This representation yields exponential tail bounds for the particle number, a law of small numbers together with a large deviation principle at logarithmic speed when the lattice spacing diverges, and a central limit theorem when it vanishes. As an application, we obtain a detailed description of the statistics of the quantum depletion in Bose--Einstein condensates, with the constants explicit in terms of the scattering length of the interaction potential.

</details>


### [173] [Why we should condition denoising diffusion generative models on windows of past observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10924)
*Matthias Morzfeld,Daniel Hodyss*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 将扩散模型条件化于短时窗历史观测，可在无需重训练的情况下达到循环DA/DOP系统的最小后验误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化（DA）是依赖时间相关先验的循环过程，能把过去观测的信息传递到未来循环。而基于去噪扩散生成模型的DA通常使用固定训练集，导致静态先验、忽略过去观测，从而后验误差大于循环DA；若纳入时间相关先验，又需频繁且昂贵的重训练。

Method: 受线性系统理论（卡尔曼滤波预测对过去观测的依赖呈指数衰减）启发，将扩散模型条件化于短时窗历史观测。提出两种框架的训练流程：一是给定过去观测预测当前状态的扩散DA系统；二是给定过去观测预测未来观测的扩散“直接观测预测”（DOP）系统。并在线性典型系统上进行分析验证。

Result: 在线性典型系统上，只要时间窗足够长，两系统均能在不重训练的情况下达到完全循环DA/DOP系统所特有的最小后验误差。

Conclusion: 线性设置保证了解析可处理性，避免了神经网络训练误差的干扰，并确认：条件化于历史观测时窗是实现高效、准确扩散DA或DOP的必要条件。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) is, traditionally, a cycling process that relies on time-dependent priors to propagate information from past observations to future cycles. Using denoising diffusion generative modeling for DA is challenging because standard approaches use a fixed training data set, which in turn leads to a static prior that ignores information from past observations. Because past observations are ignored, DA systems with static priors lead to larger posterior errors than cycling DA systems. Incorporating time-dependent priors into generative models, however, requires expensive and frequent retraining. Motivated by linear systems theory - where the dependence of a prediction of a Kalman filter on past observations decays exponentially - we condition diffusion models on short windows of past observations. Specifically, we describe training procedures for two frameworks: a diffusion DA system predicting the current state given a set of past observations, and a diffusion ``direct observation prediction'' (DOP) system, predicting future observations given a set of past observations. Using a canonical linear system, we show that both systems can achieve the minimal posterior error characteristic of a fully-cycled DA/DOP system, without re-training, provided the time windows are long enough. The linear setup ensures analytical tractability, avoids confounding neural network training errors, and confirms that conditioning on windows of past observations is required for efficient and accurate diffusion-based DA or DOP.

</details>


### [174] [The kinematic structures and the inertial geometry of a moving charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11165)
*César S. López-Monsalvo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨带电粒子的运动在多大程度上决定了其运动的几何结构，以及粒子必须自带何种结构。作者证明：满足"每个能量下速度与动量的Hamilton关系"要求的辛结构只能是底空间上的典范结构及其被闭2-形式扭曲后的结构；场提供2-形式，粒子提供其前的乘子（即电荷）；进一步表明动量映射的度决定了场进入牛顿第二定律的阶数，反对称双线性形式不产生洛伦兹力而Randers度量产生之，并得出每个荷质比对应各自几何、没有一个是带电粒子的时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者追问几何有多少由粒子运动固定、多少须由粒子本身携带，试图以"功能性判据"厘清带电粒子时空的归属问题：若不同荷质比得到不同几何，则没有任何单一几何可被称为带电粒子的时空。

Method: 仅要求辛结构像Hamilton方程那样联系速度与动量，且同时对所有能量成立；在此约束下对可能的辛结构进行分类，并用场提供的闭2-形式、粒子提供的乘子（电荷）构造扭曲结构；随后以动量映射的度分析场进入牛顿第二定律的阶数，比较反对称双线性形式与Randers度量的情形，在不定号情形下考察极值与输运，并借助球面单极通量与prequantisation给出荷量子化。

Result: 满足要求的辛结构恰为典范结构及其经底空间闭2-形式的扭曲；场给2-形式、粒子给乘子（电荷），单一能量支配一族结构而粒子取电荷所定者；动量映射的度决定场进入牛顿第二定律的阶：反对称双线性形式不产生洛伦兹力，Randers度量产生之（其与黎曼度量之差在速度上线性）；该度量已在扭曲结构之内，是自由能某层上的原函数，其输运律非线性且无已知仿射联络可胜任；不定号下长度与动力学分离、极值由最短转为最长；球面上单极通量不留下此类度量但输运仍在，prequantisation在给定作用量子时把电荷限制到格点上。

Conclusion: 每个荷质比各自获得一种几何，因此按功能性判据，其中没有任何一个是带电粒子的时空。

Abstract: We ask how much of the geometry a charged particle moves in is fixed by its motion, and how much a particle must bring. We ask of a symplectic structure only that it relate velocity to momentum as Hamilton's equations do, and we ask it of every energy at once. In particular, we show that the structures meeting that demand are the canonical one and its twists by a closed two-form of the base. A field provides the two-form, a particle the multiplier before it, which we identify constitutively with its charge. Thus, a single energy governs a family of structures, and each particle takes the one its charge fixes. We then ask what a particle must bring to be given a momentum, and we show that the degree of that map settles the degree at which a field enters Newton's Second Law. An antisymmetric bilinear form returns no Lorentz force, whilst a Randers metric returns one --- a length whose difference from a Riemannian one is linear in the velocity. Moreover, we find that metric already within the twisted structure, as its primitive over a level of the free energy, and its law of transport to be nonlinear, no affine connection being known to serve. Under an indefinite signature the length parts from the dynamics, and the extremals turn from shortest to longest. On the round sphere a monopole flux leaves no such metric, whilst the transport remains and prequantisation, given a unit of action, restricts the charge to a lattice. In this manner, we conclude that each charge-to-mass ratio receives a geometry of its own, so that by a functionalist criterion none of them is the spacetime of a charged particle.

</details>


### [175] [Recent Advances on a Model for the Electromagnetic Dirac Field at Thermal Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11283)
*Umberto Morellini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 综述正温度下相对论电子-正电子场与经典电磁场相互作用模型的研究进展，目标是理解热背景如何影响该量子系统的极化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进对热背景如何影响该量子系统极化机制的理解。

Method: 采用正温度下的量子场论模型，将相对论电子-正电子场与经典电磁场耦合，对近期相关研究进行综述。

Result: 综述了该模型在正温度条件下的近期研究进展。

Conclusion: 正温度下的该模型有助于深化对热背景影响量子系统极化机制的认识。

Abstract: We review recent progress on a model for the relativistic electron-positron field interacting with a classical electromagnetic field at positive temperature. The ultimate goal is to advance our comprehension of how thermal backgrounds affect polarisation mechanisms of this quantum system.

</details>


### [176] [A proof of the Hodge universality conjecture at nonzero dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11469)
*Francisco Hernández Iglesias*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 证明标量 tau-对称 Hamilton 形变由首个 Hamilton 密度的特定系数唯一决定，结合 Buryak–Rossi 的 Hodge 类双分歧层级构造，在非零色散下建立了 Hodge 普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Riemann 层级的标量 tau-对称 Hamilton 形变是否被少量系数唯一确定，并将其与 Hodge 类双分歧层级的构造联系起来，以在非零色散情形下确立 Hodge 普适性。

Method: 在广义标准形式下考察 Riemann 层级的标量 tau-对称 Hamilton 形变，分析其首个 Hamilton 密度中 u_x^2 的非零系数以及 u_{xx}^g (g≥2) 的系数对形变的决定作用；结合 Buryak–Rossi 关于 Hodge 类双分歧层级的构造进行论证。

Result: 证明该形变由首个 Hamilton 密度中 u_x^2 的非零系数与 u_{xx}^g (g≥2) 的系数唯一确定，从而原始层级在正规 Miura 变换意义下被确定；结合 Buryak–Rossi 的构造，得到非零色散下的 Hodge 普适性。

Conclusion: 标量 tau-对称 Hamilton 形变的刚性（由少量系数唯一确定）与 Hodge 类双分歧层级的构造相结合，确立了非零色散下的 Hodge 普适性，并刻画了原层级至正规 Miura 变换的唯一性。

Abstract: We prove that a scalar tau-symmetric Hamiltonian deformation of the Riemann hierarchy in generalized standard form is uniquely determined by the nonzero coefficient of $u_x^2$ and the coefficients of $u_{xx}^g$, $g\ge2$, in its first Hamiltonian density. This determines the original hierarchy up to normal Miura transformations. Combined with the construction of Hodge-class double ramification hierarchies by Buryak--Rossi, this establishes Hodge universality at nonzero dispersion.

</details>


### [177] [On asymptotic expansions of the density of states for Poisson distributed random Schrödinger operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11603)
*Jan Fischer,David Hasler,Jannis Koberstein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究Poisson过程势的随机Schrödinger算子，在小无序极限下推导预解式迹的渐近展开，给出展开系数的显式估计，证明其无穷体积极限在谱参数趋近自由Laplacian谱时有限，并应用得到积分态密度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机Schrödinger算子的谱性质与无序强度密切相关，小无序极限下的渐近行为是理解无序效应与自由Laplacian谱边缘关系的关键，但预解式迹的展开系数及其无穷体积极限的有限性此前缺乏显式刻画。

Method: 考虑势由Poisson过程分布的随机Schrödinger算子，在小无序极限下对预解式迹进行渐近展开；给出展开系数的显式估计，并分析其无穷体积极限在谱参数趋近自由Laplacian谱时的行为。

Result: 推导出预解式迹在小无序极限下的渐近展开，获得展开系数的显式估计，并证明这些系数的无穷体积极限在谱参数趋近自由Laplacian谱时保持有限。

Conclusion: 作为应用，由上述展开与系数估计导出积分态密度的界，为小无序随机Schrödinger算子的谱分析提供了定量工具。

Abstract: We study a random Schrödinger operator with a potential distributed according to a Poisson process. Asymptotic expansions for traces of resolvents in the limit of small disorder are derived. Explicit estimates for the expansion coefficients are given and we show that their infinite volume limits are finite as the spectral parameter approaches the spectrum of the free Laplacian. As an application we derive bounds on the integrated density of states.

</details>


### [178] [A closed signature formula for the Katz-Long-Moody Hermitian form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11793)
*Haru Negami*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了Katz-Long-Moody构造所诱导不变Hermitian形式的符号差闭式公式，解决了确定性问题，并给出若干应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Katz-Long-Moody构造对自由群与辫群半直积的表示进行卷积，得到新表示；在纯辫群上对应KZ型方程的Haraoka乘性中卷积。此前companion paper提出了该构造所带不变Hermitian形式的确定性问题，需要精确判定何时该形式正定/定型，并理解共振点处符号差的变化。

Method: 对酉输入表示及单位圆上非1的卷积参数，研究商表示上典范非退化不变Hermitian形式。证明使用初等线性代数工具：行列式恒等式、显式块主元公式，以及酉矩阵Cayley变换之和的惯性公式。公式以输入局部单值群的特征角、其有序积的特征角及卷积参数表示符号差。

Result: 得到符号差的闭式公式，涵盖取商前形式的核以及共振参数处符号差的变化；精确判定诱导形式何时定型，从而回答companion paper的确定性问题。定型蕴含输出表示可酉化；当该表示不可约时逆命题成立。应用方面：显式比较秩一构造与Haraoka对Pochhammer系统的不变形式；恢复Beukers-Heckman交错判据的Gauss情形；确定Hecke与Temperley-Lieb特化的定型参数区间。

Conclusion: Katz-Long-Moody构造诱导的不变Hermitian形式的符号差可完全由输入与卷积参数计算，进而给出定型判据与可酉化关系，并统一解释若干经典结果。

Abstract: The Katz-Long-Moody construction associates to a representation of the semidirect product of a free group and a braid group, defined by the Artin action, and a nonzero parameter a new representation of the same group. On the pure braid group it corresponds to Haraoka's multiplicative middle convolution for KZ-type equations. For unitary input and a convolution parameter on the unit circle other than one, the construction equips the quotient representation with a canonical non-degenerate invariant Hermitian form. We give a closed formula for its signature in terms of the eigenangles of the input local monodromies, the eigenangles of their ordered product, and the convolution parameter. The formula accounts for the kernel of the form before passage to the quotient and for signature changes at resonant parameters. It determines precisely when the induced form is definite, answering the definiteness problem posed in the companion paper. Definiteness implies unitarizability of the output representation; the converse holds when that representation is irreducible.
  The proof uses elementary linear algebra: a determinant identity, explicit block-pivot formulas, and an inertia formula for sums of Cayley transforms of unitary matrices. As applications, we compare the rank-one construction explicitly with Haraoka's invariant form for Pochhammer systems, recover the Gauss case of the Beukers-Heckman interlacing criterion, and determine the definite parameter intervals for the Hecke and Temperley-Lieb specializations.

</details>

Zhao,R. Zwaska*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: NOvA测试束实验利用与近/远探测器功能相同的30吨分段液闪探测器，在2019年4月至2022年7月采集p-Cu碰撞产生的标记粒子数据，以约束影响振荡分析的主要系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NOvA长基线中微子振荡实验需要精确测量混合参数、质量顺序与轻子CP破坏，而探测器响应与标定相关的系统误差是影响实验灵敏度的关键因素，因此需要专门的测试束实验来加以约束。

Method: 在费米实验室测试束设施部署NOvA测试束实验，使用30吨分段液闪探测器（与NOvA近、远探测器功能一致），分析p-Cu碰撞产生的标记粒子，并具备在0.4–1.5 GeV/c动量范围内选择与鉴别电子、μ子、π介子、K介子和质子的能力。

Result: 数据采集时间为2019年4月至2022年7月，涵盖0.4–1.5 GeV/c动量范围的多种标记粒子样本，为探测器响应、能量标定以及强子和电磁能量分辨率等系统误差研究提供了实验依据。

Conclusion: 对测试束数据的分析加深了对NOvA最大系统误差来源的理解，有助于改进探测器响应建模、能量标定及强子/电磁能量分辨率，从而提升NOvA振荡分析的精度。

Abstract: NOvA is a long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiment designed to study the neutrino mixing parameters, mass ordering, and CP violation in the lepton sector. A key component of the success of the experiment is a robust understanding of the systematic uncertainties associated with detector response and calibration. To address this, NOvA deployed a Test Beam experiment at the Fermilab Test Beam Facility, which collected data from April 2019 through July 2022. The NOvA Test Beam experiment used a 30-ton segmented liquid scintillator detector functionally identical to the NOvA Near and Far Detectors to analyze tagged particles produced from p-Cu collisions, with instrumentation capable of selecting and identifying electrons, muons, pions, kaons, and protons with momentum ranging from 0.4-1.5GeV/c. Analysis of the collected data provides a better understanding of the largest systematic uncertainties impacting NOvA's analyses, which include the detector response, energy calibration, and hadronic and electromagnetic energy resolutions.

</details>


### [180] [Production of Light Nuclei and Hypernuclei in Heavy-Ion Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10963)
*Guannan Xie,Yue-Hang Leung*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 综述SQM 2026上STAR与ALICE关于轻核/超核产额、 femtoscopy关联与集体流的测量，指出统计强子化模型可作基线但无法同时描述所有轻核产额比，而A<4的聚并模型基本自洽；共振态⁴Li相对于紧致⁴He的压低、p+p高多重数中π–d femtoscopy均揭示形成时序与核结构留下可观测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻核与超核的产生机制涉及强子化、核聚并与末态动力学，是理解QCD相变后强子阶段演化的重要探针。当前不同理论基线（统计强子化、聚并模型）对跨碰撞能量与系统尺寸数据的描述能力尚不明确，且核结构与形成时序对可观测量（产额、femtoscopy关联、集体流）的影响缺乏系统评估，因此需要综述最新实验测量并检验各模型的适用边界。

Method: 综述性分析：汇总STAR与ALICE在SQM 2026报告的轻核与超核产额、femtoscopic关联及集体流测量；将这些数据与统计强子化计算、聚并（coalescence）计算进行对比，并考察少体波函数、不稳定态feed-down等模型输入假设对结论的影响；同时结合高多重数p+p中π–d femtoscopy推断（反）氘核的形成路径。

Result: 统计强子化可描述积分产额基线，但不能同时拟合所有跨能量、跨系统尺寸的轻核产额比；对A<4束缚态，现有聚并计算能大致一致地描述产额、femtoscopy关联与集体流，但超氚（hypertriton）的定量比较依赖于所假设的少体波函数；共振态⁴Li相对紧致⁴He的产额压低表明核结构与末态动力学效应，但定量模型比较还取决于不稳定态feed-down的处理；高多重数p+p中π–d femtoscopy显示多数（反）氘核经短寿命共振态强衰变后的核子融合形成。

Conclusion: 轻核与超核的产生时序和内部核结构会在产额、femtoscopy关联与集体流等物理可观测量上留下可测量印记；聚并模型在A<4范围内表现较好，但其定量结论敏感于少体波函数与feed-down处理，统计强子化则仅能充当产额基线，未来需进一步约束这些模型输入。

Abstract: We review recent STAR and ALICE measurements of light-nucleus and hypernucleus yields, femtoscopic correlations, and collective flow presented at SQM 2026. Statistical-hadronization calculations provide a useful baseline for integrated yields but do not simultaneously describe all measured light-nucleus ratios across collision energies and system sizes. For bound states with mass number $A<4$, current coalescence calculations provide a broadly consistent description of yields, femtoscopic correlations, and collective flow, although the quantitative hypertriton comparison depends on the assumed few-body wave function. The suppressed production of resonant $^{4}$Li relative to compact $^{4}$He indicates an effect of nuclear structure and late-stage dynamics. However, the quantitative model comparison also depends on the treatment of feed-down from unstable states. In high-multiplicity $p$+$p$ collisions, pion-deuteron femtoscopy further indicates that most observed (anti)deuterons are formed through nucleon fusion after strong decays of short-lived resonances. Taken together, these measurements show that production chronology and internal nuclear structure leave measurable imprints on the physics observables.

</details>

*SND Collaboration,M. N. Achasov,A. E. Alizzi,A. Yu. Barnyakov,E. V. Bedarev,K. I. Beloborodov,A. V. Berdyugin,D. E. Berkaev,A. G. Bogdanchikov,A. A. Botov,D. E. Chistyakov,T. V. Dimova,V. P. Silagadze,K. D. Sungurov,I. K. Surin,Yu. V. Usov,V. N. Zhabin,V. V. Zhulanov*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 在VEPP-2000对撞机SND探测器上寻找e+e-直接产生C偶f1(1285)共振态，测得截面(31±13±2) pb、分支比(3.5±1.4±0.3)×10⁻⁹，显著性2.5σ，并给出90%置信度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验C偶共振态f1(1285)能否通过e+e-碰撞直接产生，探测其电子宽度相关参数（产生截面与e+e-分支比），为理解该共振态的电磁耦合性质提供实验依据。

Method: 利用VEPP-2000 e+e-对撞机上的SND探测器，在质心能量1.14–1.46 GeV范围内采集约200 pb⁻¹积分亮度数据（其中约72 pb⁻¹位于f1(1285)共振峰附近），分析e+e-→f1(1285)直接产生过程并拟合提取截面与分支比。

Result: 测得共振峰处产生截面σ(e+e-→f1)=(31±13±2) pb，分支比B(f1(1285)→e+e-)=(3.5±1.4±0.3)×10⁻⁹；观测显著性为2.5σ。鉴于显著性较低，同时给出90%置信度上限：σ(e+e-→f1)<48 pb及B(f1(1285)→e+e-)<5.4×10⁻⁹。

Conclusion: 该过程观测显著性仅为2.5σ，尚不足以确认为明确信号，因此结果以截面与分支比测量值及相应90%置信度上限的形式给出，需更高统计量数据进一步验证。

Abstract: In the experiment with the SND detector at the VEPP-2000 $e^+e^-$ collider, a search is performed for the direct production of the $C$-even $f_1(1285)$ resonance in $e^+e^-$ collisions. The analysis is based on data with an integrated luminosity of about 200 pb$^{-1}$, accumulated in the center-of-mass energy range of 1.14--1.46 GeV, of which about 72 pb$^{-1}$ were recorded near the maximum of the $f_1(1285)$ resonance. The $f_1(1285)$ production cross section at the resonance maximum $σ(e^+e^-\to f_1)=(31\pm 13\pm 2)$ pb and the branching fraction $B(f_1(1285)\to e^+e^-)=(3.5\pm 1.4\pm 0.3)\times 10^{-9}$ have been measured. The significance of the observation of the $e^+e^-\to f_1(1285)$ process is $2.5σ$. Since the significance is low, we also present the upper limits at the 90% confidence level: $σ(e^+e^-\to f_1)<48\mbox{ pb}$ and $B(f_1(1285)\to e^+e^-)<5.4\times 10^{-9}$.

</details>


### [182] [Projected Sensitivity to Slow Muonphilic Dark Matter with Accelerator Muon Beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11617)
*Rongfeng Zhang,Cheng-en Liu,Ruihu Zhu,Yu Xu,Zijian Wang,Leyun Gao,Xueheng Zhang,Qite Li,Liangwen Chen,Chen Zhou,Qiang Li,Zhiyu Sun*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于Geant4模拟，研究利用加速器μ子束探测慢速μ子亲和暗物质的灵敏度，结果显示可实现比现有宇宙线限制高近两个数量级的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质本质是现代物理未解难题之一，μ子亲和暗物质是有前景的候选场景。本文旨在探索利用加速器μ子束探测慢速μ子亲和暗物质的灵敏度，以补充传统暗物质探测手段。

Method: 开发基于Geant4的模拟框架，纳入PKMu μ子层析成像系统的探测器几何结构以及专门的弹性μ-DM散射过程；采用纯μ子束并实现基于HIAF模拟的真实μ子束相空间分布。

Result: 当束流完全处于探测器接收范围内时，投影灵敏度对束流能量和横向束斑尺寸基本不敏感。基准束流强度10^5/s下，模拟纯μ子束约11秒即可超越现有宇宙线限制1.61×10^-17 cm^2（m_DM=1 GeV）；基于HIAF的真实μ子束相空间分布，一天曝光可将投影限制较宇宙线结果提升近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 束流-μ子散射实验为显著提升慢速μ子亲和暗物质探测灵敏度提供了一条稳健且有前景的途径。

Abstract: The nature of dark matter (DM) remains one of the most enduring open questions in modern physics, and muonphilic DM has emerged as a promising scenario that complements traditional DM candidates. Following the recently established cosmic-ray muon scattering approach, we investigate the sensitivity for probing slow muonphilic DM with accelerator muon beams. A Geant4-based simulation framework is developed, incorporating the detector geometry from the PKMu muon tomography system and a dedicated elastic $μ$-DM scattering process. The projected sensitivity is found to be largely insensitive to both the beam energy and the transverse beam size when the beam is fully contained within the detector acceptance. For a benchmark beam intensity of $10^5/\rm{s}$, the simulated pure-muon beam surpasses the existing cosmic-ray limit of $1.61\times10^{-17}$ cm$^2$ at $m_{\rm DM}=1$ GeV within approximately 11 seconds. A realistic muon beam phase-space distribution based on simulations for the High Intensity heavy-ion Accelerator Facility (HIAF) is also implemented, yielding projected limits that improve upon the cosmic-ray results by nearly two orders of magnitude in a one-day exposure. These results demonstrate that a beam-muon scattering experiment offers a robust and promising route toward significantly improved sensitivity to slow muonphilic DM.

</details>
